  content: |
      # Blueprint Full-Stack: Aplikasi Manajemen Proyek Gantt Chart
      
      *Dokumen khusus untuk dibaca AI Agent · Versi 1.0 · Bahasa antarmuka aplikasi: Indonesia*
      
      ## 0. Cara membaca dokumen ini
      
      Dokumen ini adalah satu-satunya sumber kebenaran. Kamu (AI Agent) membangun aplikasi lengkap dari nol di folder kerja saat ini. Anggap Node.js, npm, dan Git sudah terpasang, dan repository GitHub kosong sudah disiapkan manusia.
      
      Aturan kerja:
      
      1. Kerjakan Fase 0 sampai Fase 6 (Bagian 10) berurutan. Setiap fase punya gerbang (gate). Jangan lanjut sebelum gate lolos.
      2. Buat dan rawat `PROGRESS.md` berisi checklist fase dan acceptance (Bagian 12). Centang setiap selesai satu langkah. Jika sesi terputus dan manusia berkata 'Lanjutkan pekerjaan sebelumnya', baca `PROGRESS.md` lalu lanjutkan dari item pertama yang belum dicentang.
      3. Jangan menambah fitur di luar dokumen ini. Jika ada hal ambigu, pilih opsi paling sederhana dan catat di README bagian 'Asumsi'.
      4. Jangan menyatakan sesuatu selesai sebelum perintah verifikasinya benar-benar dijalankan dan hasilnya lolos.
      5. Jangan menaruh rahasia, token, file `.env`, atau file database di repository.
      6. Jalankan perintah secara wajar di dalam folder proyek. Jangan melakukan perintah destruktif di luar folder proyek.
      7. Kirim prompt atau pekerjaan secara bertahap per fase, bukan semuanya sekaligus.
      
      ## 1. Tujuan dan ruang lingkup
      
      Aplikasi web manajemen proyek berbasis Gantt Chart, multi-pengguna, dengan data tersimpan di database melalui REST API (bukan localStorage).
      
      Lima kemampuan wajib:
      
      1. **Task Management**: tambah, ubah, hapus tugas; durasi hari kerja; tanggal mulai; prioritas Low/Medium/High; warna indikator; sub-tugas (parent-child).
      2. **Predecessor dan Timeline**: hubungan Finish-to-Start (FS) dan Start-to-Start (SS) dengan jeda hari (lag); grafik Gantt (bar, panah dependensi, drag dan resize).
      3. **Resource Management**: anggota tim (nama, email, peran, gaji) dan alokasi anggota ke tugas.
      4. **Kalender Proyek**: pengaturan akhir pekan dan hari libur khusus; durasi hanya menghitung hari kerja.
      5. **Analisis Proyek**: Critical Path dan diagram PERT.
      
      Di luar cakupan: kolaborasi real-time, ekspor PDF, notifikasi email, multi-bahasa, deployment cloud.
      
      ## 2. Tech stack (tetap, jangan diganti)
      
      | Lapisan | Teknologi |
      | --- | --- |
      | Frontend | React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, React Router, TanStack Query, react-hook-form, zod |
      | Backend | Node.js, Express, TypeScript (tsx untuk dev), zod, jsonwebtoken, bcryptjs, helmet, cors, express-rate-limit |
      | Database | SQLite dengan Prisma ORM (file `server/prisma/dev.db`, masuk `.gitignore`) |
      | Logika inti | Paket `shared` (TypeScript murni, dipakai server dan client) |
      | Gantt dan PERT | Digambar sendiri dengan SVG, tanpa library Gantt eksternal |
      | Test | Vitest, Supertest, Testing Library |
      | Tooling | npm workspaces, concurrently |
      
      Alasan paket `shared`: mesin penjadwalan harus identik di server (sumber kebenaran, disimpan ke DB) dan di client (pratinjau saat drag).
      
      ## 3. Struktur repository
      
      ```
      gantt-project-app/
      ├─ package.json          (npm workspaces: shared, server, client)
      ├─ tsconfig.base.json
      ├─ .gitignore  .env.example  README.md  PROGRESS.md
      ├─ shared/src/
      │   dates.ts calendar.ts scheduler.ts cpm.ts pert.ts cost.ts
      │   schemas.ts types.ts index.ts  + *.test.ts
      ├─ server/
      │   ├─ prisma/  schema.prisma  seed.ts  migrations/
      │   └─ src/  index.ts app.ts config.ts db.ts
      │       middleware/  auth.ts error.ts validate.ts
      │       routes/      auth projects tasks dependencies resources
      │                    assignments calendar analysis
      │       services/    recompute.ts snapshot.ts
      │       tests/
      └─ client/
          ├─ index.html  vite.config.ts  (proxy /api ke server)
          └─ src/  main.tsx App.tsx
              api/  hooks/  lib/  styles/
              pages/       Login Register Projects Gantt Team Calendar Analysis
              components/  gantt/ pert/ tasks/ team/ calendar/ ui/
      ```
      
      ## 4. Konfigurasi dan skrip
      
      - Port: server `3001`, client `5173`. Vite mem-proxy `/api` ke `http://localhost:3001`.
      - `.env.example` (di-commit): `DATABASE_URL=file:./dev.db`, `JWT_SECRET=ganti-dengan-string-acak`, `PORT=3001`.
      - Buat `.env` lokal dengan JWT\_SECRET acak untuk menjalankan aplikasi. File `.env` tidak boleh di-commit. Server wajib gagal start dengan pesan jelas jika `JWT_SECRET` kosong.
      - Skrip di root `package.json`:
        - `dev`: jalankan server dan client bersamaan
        - `build`: build shared, server, client
        - `typecheck`: `tsc --noEmit` di semua workspace
        - `test`: Vitest di semua workspace
        - `db:migrate`, `db:seed`, `db:reset`
      - Perintah `npm install` di root harus cukup untuk memasang semua dependensi.
      
      ## 5. Model data
      
      Semua `id` berupa string cuid. Tanggal disimpan sebagai string ISO `YYYY-MM-DD`. Kolom enum memakai String dan divalidasi lewat zod.
      
      - **User**: id, name, email (unik), passwordHash, createdAt
      - **Project**: id, ownerId, name, description?, startDate, weekendDays (String, daftar angka dipisah koma, 0=Minggu sampai 6=Sabtu, default `0,6`), createdAt
      - **Holiday**: id, projectId, date, name. Unik pada (projectId, date).
      - **Task**: id, projectId, parentId?, position (Int, urutan antar saudara), name, description?, durationDays (Int ≥ 1), startDate (tanggal mulai paling awal), scheduledStart, scheduledEnd (hasil hitung mesin), priority (LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH), color (hex), progress (0 sampai 100), optimisticDays?, pessimisticDays?
      - **Dependency**: id, predecessorId, successorId, type (FS atau SS), lagDays (Int, boleh negatif, rentang -365 sampai 365). Unik pada (predecessorId, successorId).
      - **Resource**: id, projectId, name, email, role, salary (Int, rupiah per bulan)
      - **Assignment**: id, taskId, resourceId, allocationPercent (Int 1 sampai 100, default 100). Unik pada (taskId, resourceId).
      
      Relasi: hapus Project menghapus semua turunannya (cascade). Hapus Task menghapus anak, dependensi, dan assignment terkait. Hapus Resource menghapus assignment-nya.
      
      ## 6. Aturan bisnis dan algoritma (paket `shared`)
      
      Semua fungsi di bagian ini adalah fungsi murni dan wajib punya unit test.
      
      ### 6.1 Tanggal
      
      Tanggal diproses sebagai tanggal UTC murni, tanpa zona waktu lokal, agar tidak ada pergeseran satu hari.
      
      ### 6.2 Kalender
      
      - `weekendDays: number[]` (default `[0,6]`) dan `holidays: Set<string>`.
      - `isWorkingDay(d)`, `nextWorkingDay(d)` (d sendiri jika hari kerja, jika tidak maju ke hari kerja berikutnya), `shiftWorkingDays(d, n)` (n boleh negatif; d harus hari kerja), `workingDaysBetween(a, b)` (inklusif).
      - Validasi: weekendDays tidak boleh berisi ketujuh hari.
      
      ### 6.3 Durasi
      
      `durationDays` adalah bilangan bulat hari kerja (≥ 1). `scheduledEnd = shiftWorkingDays(scheduledStart, durationDays - 1)`. Bar tetap digambar menurut tanggal kalender, sehingga bar melintasi akhir pekan dan hari libur.
      
      ### 6.4 Dependensi
      
      Lag dihitung dalam hari kerja. Batas mulai tugas penerus (successor):
      
      - **FS**: `shiftWorkingDays(pred.scheduledEnd, 1 + lag)`
      - **SS**: `shiftWorkingDays(pred.scheduledStart, lag)`
      
      `scheduledStart = nextWorkingDay(max(task.startDate, semua batas dari predecessor))`. Jadi `startDate` berfungsi sebagai batas 'mulai paling awal'.
      
      Larangan (balas 422): dependensi ke diri sendiri, pasangan duplikat, siklus (kode `CYCLE_DETECTED`), dan dependensi pada tugas yang punya anak. Hanya tugas daun (leaf) boleh punya dependensi dan assignment.
      
      ### 6.5 Tugas induk (summary)
      
      - `scheduledStart` = minimum anak, `scheduledEnd` = maksimum anak, `durationDays` = `workingDaysBetween` keduanya, `progress` = rata-rata anak berbobot durasi.
      - Tugas induk hanya boleh diedit nama, deskripsi, warna, dan prioritasnya.
      - Kedalaman maksimal 3 level.
      - Tugas yang sudah punya dependensi atau assignment tidak boleh diberi anak (409 dengan pesan jelas).
      
      ### 6.6 Penjadwalan ulang (recompute)
      
      Urutan: urutkan topologis tugas daun (algoritma Kahn), hitung `scheduledStart` dan `scheduledEnd`, lalu roll-up tugas induk dari bawah ke atas. Server menjalankan ini setiap ada perubahan tugas, dependensi, durasi, atau kalender, menyimpan hasilnya, dan mengembalikan seluruh daftar tugas proyek.
      
      ### 6.7 Drag di Gantt
      
      - Geser bar tugas daun: ubah `startDate` ke tanggal baru (snap ke hari kerja). Jika hasilnya lebih awal dari batas predecessor, bar kembali ke batas tersebut dan tampil toast info.
      - Tarik tepi kanan bar: ubah `durationDays` (minimum 1) dengan `workingDaysBetween`.
      - Bar tugas induk tidak bisa di-drag.
      - Selama drag, client memakai mesin `shared` untuk pratinjau. Saat dilepas, kirim PATCH ke server dan pakai respons server sebagai hasil akhir.
      
      ### 6.8 Critical Path (CPM), hanya tugas daun
      
      Ordinal hari kerja: `ord(d)` = jumlah hari kerja dari awal proyek (ordinal 0) sampai d.
      
      - Forward pass: `ES = ord(scheduledStart)`, `EF = ord(scheduledEnd)`. Akhir proyek `PE = max(EF)`.
      - Backward pass: tugas tanpa penerus punya `LF = PE`. Untuk tugas dengan penerus, `LF` = minimum dari:
        - FS: `LS_succ - 1 - lag`
        - SS: `LS_succ - lag + dur_pred - 1`
      - `LS = LF - dur + 1`, `totalFloat = LS - ES` (minimal 0).
      - Kritis jika `totalFloat = 0`. Jalur kritis adalah rangkaian tugas kritis yang terhubung, diurutkan menurut ES.
      
      ### 6.9 Diagram PERT
      
      - Jaringan activity-on-node dari tugas daun. Setiap node menampilkan nama, durasi, ES, EF, LS, LF, dan slack.
      - Tata letak: kolom = kedalaman jalur terpanjang dari node awal. Panah mengikuti dependensi. Node dan panah kritis berwarna merah.
      - Jika `optimisticDays` dan `pessimisticDays` terisi, tampilkan estimasi `te = (o + 4m + p) / 6` dan variansi `((p - o) / 6)^2` (m = `durationDays`). Ini informasi tambahan dan tidak mengubah jadwal. Tampilkan juga total variansi dan simpangan baku di sepanjang jalur kritis.
      
      ### 6.10 Biaya dan beban kerja
      
      - Tarif harian = `salary / 22`.
      - Biaya tugas = jumlah dari (tarif harian × durasi × allocationPercent / 100) untuk tiap assignment. Total proyek = jumlah semua tugas daun. Format rupiah (IDR).
      - Peringatan over-alokasi: jika total allocationPercent seorang resource pada tugas daun yang saling tumpang tindih melebihi 100 pada suatu hari kerja, tampilkan peringatan di halaman Tim.
      
      ### 6.11 Vektor uji wajib
      
      Kalender akhir pekan Sabtu dan Minggu, tanpa libur kecuali dinyatakan lain:
      
      - Mulai Jumat 2026-10-23, durasi 3 → berakhir Selasa 2026-10-27.
      - FS lag 0 dari tugas di atas → penerus mulai Rabu 2026-10-28.
      - SS lag 2 dari tugas di atas → penerus mulai Selasa 2026-10-27.
      - Dengan libur Senin 2026-10-26: mulai Jumat 2026-10-23, durasi 3 → berakhir Rabu 2026-10-28.
      - CPM: A(3 hari) → B(2 hari), A → C(5 hari), B dan C → D(1 hari), semua FS, lag 0, proyek mulai Senin 2026-10-19. Hasil: A, C, D kritis; B punya float 3; PE = 8.
      
      ## 7. REST API
      
      Prefiks `/api`. Format JSON. Semua rute selain `/auth/register`, `/auth/login`, dan `/health` memakai header `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Setiap data dicek kepemilikannya (hanya pemilik proyek yang boleh mengakses; selain itu 404).
      
      Format error: `{ "error": { "code": "STRING", "message": "pesan bahasa Indonesia", "details": {} } }`. Status: 400/422 validasi, 401 belum login, 404 tidak ditemukan, 409 konflik.
      
      | Method dan path | Fungsi |
      | --- | --- |
      | GET `/health` | Cek server hidup |
      | POST `/auth/register` | Daftar (name, email, password min 8 karakter), kembalikan token dan user |
      | POST `/auth/login` | Masuk, kembalikan token dan user |
      | GET `/auth/me` | Data user saat ini |
      | GET, POST `/projects` | Daftar dan buat proyek |
      | GET, PATCH, DELETE `/projects/:id` | Detail (snapshot lengkap: proyek, tugas, dependensi, resource, assignment, holiday), ubah, hapus |
      | POST `/projects/:id/tasks` | Buat tugas (bisa dengan parentId) |
      | PATCH `/tasks/:id` | Ubah tugas, jadwal dihitung ulang |
      | DELETE `/tasks/:id` | Hapus tugas beserta anaknya |
      | POST `/tasks/:id/move` | Ubah parent dan posisi |
      | POST `/tasks/:id/dependencies` | Tambah predecessor (predecessorId, type, lagDays) |
      | PATCH, DELETE `/dependencies/:id` | Ubah atau hapus dependensi |
      | GET, POST `/projects/:id/resources` | Daftar dan tambah anggota tim |
      | PATCH, DELETE `/resources/:id` | Ubah atau hapus anggota |
      | POST `/tasks/:id/assignments` | Alokasikan resource (resourceId, allocationPercent) |
      | PATCH, DELETE `/assignments/:id` | Ubah atau hapus alokasi |
      | PUT `/projects/:id/calendar` | Atur weekendDays, jadwal dihitung ulang |
      | POST `/projects/:id/holidays` | Tambah hari libur (date, name) |
      | DELETE `/holidays/:id` | Hapus hari libur |
      | GET `/projects/:id/analysis` | Hasil CPM, jalur kritis, data PERT, biaya, beban resource |
      
      Aturan respons: setiap mutasi yang mengubah jadwal (tugas, dependensi, durasi, kalender) mengembalikan `{ tasks: [...] }` berisi seluruh tugas terbaru, supaya client cukup memperbarui cache.
      
      ## 8. Frontend
      
      Rute: `/login`, `/register`, `/projects`, `/projects/:id/gantt`, `/projects/:id/team`, `/projects/:id/calendar`, `/projects/:id/analysis`. Rute selain login dan register dilindungi. Token JWT disimpan di localStorage; selain token dan preferensi tampilan (misalnya zoom), semua data berasal dari API.
      
      **Umum**: tampilan rapi dan responsif (layar ponsel sampai desktop), label bahasa Indonesia, state loading, kosong, dan error di setiap halaman, toast untuk hasil aksi, konfirmasi sebelum menghapus, validasi form dengan skema zod dari `shared`.
      
      ### 8.1 Halaman Gantt
      
      - Dua panel: tabel tugas di kiri (WBS, nama, durasi, mulai, selesai, prioritas, penanggung jawab, progres; induk bisa dilipat) dan timeline SVG di kanan. Scroll vertikal keduanya disinkronkan.
      - Timeline: zoom Hari/Minggu/Bulan, tombol 'Hari ini', garis hari ini, arsiran akhir pekan dan hari libur, bar berwarna sesuai `color` dengan lapisan progres, bar induk bergaya bracket.
      - Panah dependensi: FS dari ujung kanan bar predecessor ke ujung kiri bar penerus, SS dari ujung kiri ke ujung kiri. Jalur siku-siku, posisi x berasal dari `scheduledStart` dan `scheduledEnd` (bukan hitungan hari kalender).
      - Drag dan resize sesuai 6.7. Toggle 'Sorot jalur kritis'.
      - Dialog tugas: nama, deskripsi, parent, durasi, mulai paling awal, prioritas, warna, progres, estimasi optimistis dan pesimistis (opsional), editor predecessor (baris: tugas, tipe FS/SS, lag), penanggung jawab (resource dan persen). Untuk tugas induk, kolom yang dihitung otomatis dibuat read-only.
      
      ### 8.2 Halaman Tim
      
      Tabel anggota (nama, email, peran, gaji), tambah, ubah, hapus, daftar tugas yang dialokasikan, total biaya per anggota, dan peringatan over-alokasi.
      
      ### 8.3 Halaman Kalender
      
      Checkbox tujuh hari untuk akhir pekan, daftar hari libur (tanggal dan nama) dengan tambah dan hapus, serta kalender bulanan mini yang menandai hari non-kerja. Setelah perubahan, jadwal bergeser otomatis dan toast 'Jadwal diperbarui' muncul.
      
      ### 8.4 Halaman Analisis
      
      Ringkasan (tanggal mulai, tanggal selesai, total hari kerja, total biaya, jumlah tugas kritis), tabel CPM (ES, EF, LS, LF, float, kritis), daftar jalur kritis, dan diagram PERT sesuai 6.9 yang bisa di-scroll dan di-zoom.
      
      ## 9. Seed data
      
      `npm run db:seed` bersifat idempoten (aman dijalankan ulang). Isi:
      
      - User demo: `demo@example.com` dengan password `Demo12345` (cantumkan di README sebagai akun contoh).
      - Proyek 'Pengembangan Aplikasi Mobile Toko Online', mulai 2026-10-19, akhir pekan Sabtu dan Minggu. Hari libur: 2026-11-11 'Libur internal (contoh)' dan 2026-12-25 'Hari Raya Natal'.
      - Resource: Andi Pratama (Project Manager, 15000000), Budi Santoso (Backend Developer, 12000000), Citra Lestari (UI/UX Designer, 10000000), Dewi Anggraini (QA Engineer, 9000000).
      - Tugas (induk, lalu anak, dengan dependensi):
        - **1 Perencanaan**: 1.1 Kickoff dan kebutuhan (3 hari, High, Andi, progres 100); 1.2 Penyusunan spesifikasi (4 hari, High, Andi, FS dari 1.1, progres 60)
        - **2 Desain**: 2.1 Riset dan wireframe (5 hari, Medium, Citra, SS dari 1.2 lag 2); 2.2 Desain UI high-fidelity (6 hari, Medium, Citra, FS dari 2.1)
        - **3 Pengembangan**: 3.1 Rancang database dan API (5 hari, High, Budi, FS dari 1.2); 3.2 Implementasi backend (10 hari, High, Budi, FS dari 3.1, optimistis 8, pesimistis 15); 3.3 Implementasi frontend mobile (12 hari, High, Budi 50% dan Citra 50%, FS dari 2.2 dan SS dari 3.2 lag 3)
        - **4 Pengujian**: 4.1 Skenario uji (3 hari, Medium, Dewi, SS dari 3.2 lag 4); 4.2 Integrasi dan uji sistem (6 hari, High, Dewi, FS dari 3.2 dan FS dari 3.3); 4.3 Perbaikan bug (4 hari, Medium, Budi, FS dari 4.2)
        - **5 Peluncuran**: 5.1 Persiapan rilis (2 hari, Medium, Andi, FS dari 4.3 lag 1); 5.2 Rilis dan serah terima (1 hari, High, Andi, FS dari 5.1)
      - Beri warna berbeda per kelompok. Pastikan seed menghasilkan setidaknya satu tugas non-kritis (misalnya 4.1) dan satu hari libur yang jatuh di dalam rentang proyek sehingga efek kalender terlihat.
      
      ## 10. Fase pengerjaan
      
      ### Fase 0: Fondasi
      
      Inisialisasi monorepo npm workspaces (`shared`, `server`, `client`), tsconfig, Vite + React + Tailwind, Express kosong dengan `/api/health`, skrip root, `.gitignore`, `.env.example`, `PROGRESS.md`.
      
      **Gate**: `npm install` dan `npm run typecheck` sukses pada kerangka.
      
      ### Fase 1: Mesin inti (`shared`)
      
      Implementasikan 6.1 sampai 6.10 beserta skema zod dan tipe. Tulis unit test termasuk semua vektor uji di 6.11.
      
      **Gate**: `npm test -w shared` lolos.
      
      ### Fase 2: Backend
      
      Skema Prisma dan migrasi, auth, seluruh rute di Bagian 7, `recompute` dan `snapshot`, middleware error dan validasi, cek kepemilikan, seed (Bagian 9). Tulis test integrasi Supertest dengan database SQLite terpisah untuk: auth, CRUD tugas, parent-child, FS dan SS dengan lag, penolakan siklus, kalender (weekend dan libur menggeser jadwal), assignment, analisis, dan akses lintas pengguna ditolak.
      
      **Gate**: typecheck dan test server lolos, `npm run db:seed` berhasil.
      
      ### Fase 3: Frontend
      
      Implementasikan Bagian 8 lengkap. Tulis beberapa test komponen: bar Gantt dan panah dependensi ter-render dari data seed, form tugas memvalidasi input, diagram PERT menandai node kritis.
      
      **Gate**: typecheck, test, dan build client lolos.
      
      ### Fase 4: Integrasi dan verifikasi
      
      1. Jalankan `npm install`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm test`, dan `npm run build`. Perbaiki semua error sampai semuanya lolos tanpa error.
      2. Jalankan `npm run dev`, pastikan `http://localhost:3001/api/health` dan `http://localhost:5173` merespons, lalu beri tahu manusia alamat URL aplikasi.
      3. Telusuri Acceptance Checklist (Bagian 12) dan tandai di `PROGRESS.md`. Item yang gagal diperbaiki dengan protokol Bagian 13.
      
      **Gate**: keempat perintah verifikasi lolos dan seluruh checklist tercentang.
      
      ### Fase 5: Rapikan sebelum push
      
      - `.gitignore` mengecualikan minimal: `node_modules`, `dist`, `.env`, `*.db`, `*.db-journal`, `coverage`.
      - `README.md` berisi: deskripsi dan daftar fitur, tech stack, prasyarat, cara install (`npm install`, salin `.env.example` ke `.env`, `npm run db:migrate`, `npm run db:seed`), cara menjalankan (`npm run dev`, alamat URL), cara test dan build, akun demo, ringkasan API, dan bagian 'Asumsi'.
      - Pindai repo untuk rahasia: tidak ada token, password nyata, atau file `.env` dan database yang ikut ter-track.
      
      **Gate**: `git status` bersih dari file yang seharusnya diabaikan dan README sesuai kenyataan.
      
      ### Fase 6: Push ke GitHub
      
      Perlu URL repository dari manusia, berbentuk `https://github.com/USERNAME/gantt-project-app.git`. Jika belum diberikan, tanyakan sekali. Jangan menebak USERNAME.
      
      ```
      git init
      git add .
      git commit -m "Initial commit: Gantt Chart project management app"
      git branch -M main
      git remote add origin <URL repository>
      git push -u origin main
      ```
      
      Saat push pertama, jendela browser untuk login GitHub mungkin muncul (Git Credential Manager). Beri tahu manusia agar login dan menekan Authorize. Jika identitas Git (`user.name` dan `user.email`) belum diatur, minta manusia mengaturnya. Jika push tidak bisa dijalankan agen, berikan perintah di atas kepada manusia untuk dijalankan manual.
      
      **Gate**: `git log` menunjukkan commit dan `git push` sukses.
      
      ## 11. Standar kualitas
      
      - TypeScript strict mode, tanpa `any` kecuali terpaksa dan diberi komentar.
      - Validasi semua input di server dengan zod. Jangan percaya data dari client.
      - Password di-hash dengan bcryptjs. `passwordHash` tidak pernah dikirim ke client.
      - Server memakai `helmet` dan `cors`. Rute login dan register dibatasi dengan rate limit.
      - Jangan menyalin logika penjadwalan ke tempat lain. Selalu impor dari `shared`.
      - Kode terbaca: nama jelas, fungsi kecil, tanpa kode mati.
      
      ## 12. Acceptance checklist
      
      Verifikasi lewat API (Supertest atau curl) dan antarmuka. Tandai di `PROGRESS.md`.
      
      **Tugas**
      
      - [ ] Tambah, ubah, dan hapus tugas berhasil
      - [ ] Durasi, tanggal mulai, prioritas, dan warna tersimpan
      - [ ] Sub-tugas (parent-child) bisa dibuat, dan induk menghitung rentang serta progres otomatis
      
      **Dependensi dan Gantt**
      
      - [ ] Predecessor FS dan SS dengan lag mengubah posisi bar sesuai 6.4 dan vektor uji 6.11
      - [ ] Bar Gantt tampil, ada panah dependensi (FS dan SS), dan bar bisa di-drag serta di-resize
      - [ ] Dependensi yang membentuk siklus ditolak dengan pesan jelas
      
      **Resource**
      
      - [ ] Tambah anggota tim (nama, email, peran, gaji) dan alokasikan ke tugas
      - [ ] Biaya dan peringatan over-alokasi muncul benar
      
      **Kalender**
      
      - [ ] Ubah akhir pekan dan tambah hari libur, lalu jadwal bergeser otomatis
      - [ ] Durasi tidak menghitung akhir pekan dan hari libur
      
      **Analisis**
      
      - [ ] Halaman Analisis menampilkan Critical Path dan diagram PERT, node kritis berwarna merah
      
      **Persistensi dan akun**
      
      - [ ] Data tidak hilang saat halaman di-refresh (data berasal dari database)
      - [ ] Register, login, dan logout berfungsi, dan pengguna A tidak bisa melihat data pengguna B
      
      **Repository**
      
      - [ ] Ada folder `src` di tiap workspace, `package.json`, dan `README.md`
      - [ ] `node_modules`, `.env`, dan file database tidak ikut ter-commit
      - [ ] `npm run typecheck`, `npm test`, dan `npm run build` lolos tanpa error
      
      ## 13. Protokol perbaikan bug
      
      Gunakan saat manusia melaporkan fitur rusak, misalnya 'saat saya klik tambah tugas, tidak ada yang muncul'.
      
      1. Ulangi masalahnya (test, curl, atau langkah di UI) dan pastikan terlihat.
      2. Tentukan lapisannya: mesin `shared`, API server, atau UI client.
      3. Tulis test yang gagal terlebih dahulu bila memungkinkan.
      4. Perbaiki penyebab utamanya dengan perubahan sekecil mungkin.
      5. Jalankan `typecheck`, `test`, dan `build` penuh untuk memastikan fitur lain tidak rusak, lalu cek ulang item terkait di Bagian 12.
      6. Laporkan penyebab dan perbaikan dalam maksimal tiga kalimat.
      
      **Contoh**: 'Panah dependensi tidak muncul dan durasi tidak melewati akhir pekan.' Periksa: (a) posisi x panah harus dihitung dari `scheduledStart` dan `scheduledEnd`, dan panah harus dirender untuk FS dan SS; (b) durasi harus memakai `shiftWorkingDays` dan `workingDaysBetween`, bukan selisih hari kalender; (c) jalankan vektor uji 6.11 untuk membuktikan perbaikan.
      
      Jika kamu berhenti di tengah pekerjaan, baca `PROGRESS.md` dan lanjutkan dari item pertama yang belum selesai.
      
      ## 14. Laporan akhir ke manusia
      
      Setelah Fase 6, kirim laporan singkat berisi:
      
      - Hasil keempat perintah verifikasi (install, typecheck, test, build)
      - URL aplikasi lokal dan akun demo
      - URL repository dan status push
      - Daftar asumsi atau batasan yang kamu ambil
      - Pengingat untuk manusia: uji manual dengan checklist Bagian 12, pastikan repository berstatus Public, lalu simpan chat history dan kumpulkan bersama URL repository sesuai instruksi dosen

> ```ts
> export interface SchedulerTaskInput {

> Algorithm:
> - leafTasks = tasks without children
> - parentTasks determined by parentId references
> - Build successor adjacency for leaves.
> - Kahn topo sort: indegree = number of predecessors.
> - For each leaf in topo order: compute bound = max over predecessors of dependencyBound; start = nextWorkingDay(max(startDate, bound)); end = shiftWorkingDays(start, duration-1).
>   - If no predecessors, bound = startDate.
>   - Note if lag negative, shiftWorkingDays can move earlier — need care: shiftWorkingDays requires the base date be a working day. pred.scheduledEnd is always a working day. shiftWorkingDays with negative n: e.g. -1 → previous working day.
> - Then roll up parents bottom-up: scheduledStart = min child start, end = max child end, durationDays = workingDaysBetween(start,end), progress = weighted avg by durationDays.
> 
> Cycle detection: Kahn's leftover nodes indicate cycle. Also validate on dependency creation.
> 
> Parent depth max 3 levels — validate.
> 
> Now let me also handle: "hanya tugas daun boleh punya dependensi dan assignment" - validate at API.
> 
> Cost: dailyRate = salary/22; taskCost = sum over assignments of dailyRate * duration * alloc/100. Total = sum over leaf tasks. Format IDR.
> 
> Over-allocation: for each resource, for each working day, sum allocationPercent over leaf tasks assigned that are active that day > 100 → warning.

> dates.ts:
> ```ts
> const DATE_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
> 
> export function isValidDateString(value: string): boolean {
>   if (!DATE_RE.test(value)) return false;
>   const d = toUTCDate(value);
>   return formatDate(d) === value;
> }
> 
> export function toUTCDate(value: string): Date {
>   const [y, m, d] = value.split('-').map(Number);
>   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
> }
> 
> export function formatDate(date: Date): string {
>   const y = date.getUTCFullYear();
>   const m = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
>   const d = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
>   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
> }
> 
> export function addDays(date: Date, days: number): Date {
>   const r = new Date(date.getTime());
>   r.setUTCDate(r.getUTCDate() + days);
>   return r;
> }
> 
> export function getDayOfWeek(date: Date): number { return date.getUTCDay(); }
> export function compare(d1,d2) 
> export function minDate, maxDate
> export function diffInDays(a,b) // calendar days
> ```
> 
> calendar.ts:
> ```ts
> export interface CalendarConfig { weekendDays: number[]; holidays: string[]; }
> 
> export const DEFAULT_WEEKEND_DAYS = [0, 6];
> 
> export function normalizeWeekendDays(value: string | number[]): number[]
> export function weekendDaysToString(days: number[]): string
> export function parseWeekendDays(value: string): number[]
> export function validateWeekendDays(days: number[]): string | null
> 
> export function isWeekend(date, config)
> export function isHoliday(date, config)
> export function isWorkingDay(date, config)
> export function nextWorkingDay(date, config)
> export function previousWorkingDay(date, config)
> export function shiftWorkingDays(date, n, config)
> export function workingDaysBetween(start, end, config) // inclusive; 0 if end < start
> export function workingDaysList(start, end, config)
> ```
> 
> shiftWorkingDays when n > 0: move forward n working days, d must be working day (if not, spec says d must be working day). Implementation: 
> ```
> let cur = date;
> let remaining = Math.abs(n);
> const step = n >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
> while (remaining > 0) { cur = addDays(cur, step); if (isWorkingDay(cur, config)) remaining--; }
> return cur;
> ```
> Wait if n>0 and d itself is a working day, then after loop we've advanced n working days beyond d. shiftWorkingDays(Fri23, 1) → Mon26 ✓. shiftWorkingDays(Fri23,2) → Tue27 ✓.
> If n<0: shiftWorkingDays(Mon26, -1) → Fri23 ✓.
> 
> nextWorkingDay(d): while (!isWorkingDay(d)) d = addDays(d,1); return d.
> previousWorkingDay similarly.
> 
> workingDaysBetween(start, end): if end < start → 0; count working days in [start, end] inclusive.
> 
> Now scheduler.ts. Note: dates as strings for storage but computation with Date. I'll convert internally.
> 
> ```ts
> export interface ScheduleTaskInput {
>   id: string;
>   parentId: string | null;
>   position: number;
>   durationDays: number;
>   startDate: string;
>   progress?: number;
> }
> export interface ScheduleDependencyInput { predecessorId; successorId; type; lagDays }
> export interface ScheduledTask { id; scheduledStart; scheduledEnd; durationDays; progress; isParent }
> export interface ScheduleOutput { tasks: ScheduledTask[]; byId: Map<string, ScheduledTask> }
> ```
> 
> Hmm ScheduleOutput with Map is not serializable but fine internally.
> 
> Algorithm details:
> 
> ```ts
> export function computeSchedule(tasks, dependencies, calendar): ScheduleOutput {
>   const byId = new Map(tasks.map(t => [t.id, t]));
>   const childCount = new Map<string, number>();
>   for (const t of tasks) if (t.parentId) childCount.set(t.parentId, (childCount.get(t.parentId) ?? 0) + 1);
>   const isParent = (t) => (childCount.get(t.id) ?? 0) > 0;
>   const leaves = tasks.filter(t => !isParent(t));
>   const deps = dependencies.filter(d => byId.has(d.predecessorId) && byId.has(d.successorId));
>   // also drop deps where either is parent
>   const adjacency = new Map<string, ScheduleDependencyInput[]>();
>   const indegree = new Map<string, number>();
>   for (const leaf of leaves) { adjacency.set(leaf.id, []); indegree.set(leaf.id, 0); }
>   for (const d of deps) {
>     if (!leafIds.has(d.predecessorId) || !leafIds.has(d.successorId)) continue;
>     adjacency.get(d.predecessorId)!.push(d);
>     indegree.set(d.successorId, indegree.get(d.successorId)! + 1);
>   }
>   // Kahn
>   const queue = leaves.filter(l => indegree.get(l.id) === 0).sort(byPosition).map(l=>l.id);
>   const order: string[] = [];
>   while (queue.length) {
>     const id = queue.shift()!;
>     order.push(id);
>     for (const dep of adjacency.get(id)!) {
>       const n = indegree.get(dep.successorId)! - 1;
>       indegree.set(dep.successorId, n);
>       if (n === 0) queue.push(dep.successorId);
>     }
>   }
>   if (order.length !== leaves.length) throw new CycleError...
> ```
> Hmm, queue order stability matters when multiple ready tasks; sort by position each time? Use a sorted insertion. Since schedule of each task only depends on its predecessors, order among independent tasks doesn't matter for correctness. Fine.
> 
> Then compute:
> ```ts
>   const scheduleById = new Map<string, ScheduledTask>();
>   for (const id of order) {
>     const task = byId.get(id)!;
>     let bound: Date | null = null;
>     for (const dep of predecessors of id) {
>       const pred = scheduleById.get(dep.predecessorId)!;
>       const b = dep.type === 'FS'
>         ? shiftWorkingDays(toUTCDate(pred.scheduledEnd), 1 + dep.lagDays, calendar)
>         : shiftWorkingDays(toUTCDate(pred.scheduledStart), dep.lagDays, calendar);
>       bound = bound === null || b > bound ? b : bound;
>     }
>     const earliest = toUTCDate(task.startDate);
>     const startDate = bound === null ? earliest : (bound > earliest ? bound : earliest);
>     const start = nextWorkingDay(startDate, calendar);
>     const end = shiftWorkingDays(start, Math.max(1, task.durationDays) - 1, calendar);
>     scheduleById.set(id, { id, scheduledStart: formatDate(start), scheduledEnd: formatDate(end), durationDays, progress: task.progress ?? 0, isParent: false });
>   }
> ```
> Need predecessors map.
> 
> Then roll up parents bottom-up (by depth descending):
> ```ts
>   const parents = tasks.filter(isParent);
>   const depth = ... computeDepth (0 = root)
>   sort parents by depth desc
>   for (const p of parents) {
>     const children = tasks.filter(t => t.parentId === p.id).map(t => scheduleById.get(t.id)!);
>     if (!children.length) continue; // shouldn't happen
>     const startD = min of child scheduledStart; endD = max child scheduledEnd;
>     duration = workingDaysBetween(startD, endD, calendar);
>     progress = weighted average: sum(childDuration * childProgress)/sum(childDuration) rounded.
>   }
> ```
> Note children of a parent may themselves be parents already computed (depth desc ensures children computed first ✓).
> 
> Weighted by child durationDays (the scheduled duration). Round to integer.
> 
> Cycle detection: also dependencies on parents are invalid; ignore? Spec: reject at API. In computeSchedule, I'll ignore deps involving parents (defensive) — but better to throw? I'll just ignore them and validate at API.
> 
> Also need `detectCycle(dependencies, tasks)` helper for API validation and `wouldCreateCycle`.

string;\n  name: string;\n  description: string | null;\n  startDate: string;\n  weekendDays: string;\n  createdAt: string;\n}\n\nexport interface HolidayDto {\n  id: string;\n  projectId: string;\n  date: string;\n  name: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskDto {\n  id: string;\n  projectId: string;\n  parentId: string | null;\n  position: number;\n  name: string;\n  description: string | null;\n  durationDays: number;\n  startDate: string;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n  priority: Priority;\n  color: string;\n  progress: number;\n  optimisticDays: number | null;\n  pessimisticDays: number | null;\n}\n\nexport interface DependencyDto {\n  id: string;\n  predecessorId: string;\n  successorId: string;\n  type: DependencyType;\n  lagDays: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ResourceDto {\n  id: string;\n  projectId: string;\n  name: string;\n  email: string;\n  role: string;\n  salary: number;\n}\n\nexport interface AssignmentDto {\n  id: string;\n  taskId: string;\n  resourceId: string;\n  allocationPercent: number;\n}\n\n/** Snapshot lengkap satu proyek (respons GET /api/projects/:id). */\nexport interface ProjectSnapshot {\n  project: ProjectDto;\n  tasks: TaskDto[];\n  dependencies: DependencyDto[];\n  resources: ResourceDto[];\n  assignments: AssignmentDto[];\n  holidays: HolidayDto[];\n}\n\nexport interface AuthResponse {\n  token: string;\n  user: UserDto;\n}\n\nexport interface ApiErrorBody {\n  error: {\n    code: string;\n    message: string;\n    details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  };\n}\n\n/** Respons standar setiap mutasi yang mengubah jadwal. */\nexport interface TasksResponse {\n  tasks: TaskDto[];\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "shared/src/dates.ts",
  "instructions": "UTC date helpers",
  "content": "/**\n * Utilitas tanggal berbasis UTC murni.\n * Semua perhitungan memakai UTC agar tidak ada pergeseran satu hari\n * akibat zona waktu lokal.\n */\n\nconst DATE_PATTERN = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/;\n\nexport const MS_PER_DAY = 86_400_000;\n\n/** Mengubah string `YYYY-MM-DD` menjadi `Date` pada tengah malam UTC. */\nexport function toUTCDate(value: string): Date {\n  const match = DATE_PATTERN.exec(value);\n  if (!match) {\n    throw new Error(`Format tanggal tidak valid: ${value}`);\n  }\n  const year = Number(match[1]);\n  const month = Number(match[2]);\n  const day = Number(match[3]);\n  return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));\n}\n\n/** Mengubah `Date` menjadi string `YYYY-MM-DD` (UTC). */\nexport function formatDate(date: Date): string {\n  const year = String(date.getUTCFullYear()).padStart(4, '0');\n  const month = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');\n  const day = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');\n  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n}\n\n/** Benar bila string adalah tanggal ISO valid (mis. 2026-02-30 ditolak). */\nexport function isValidDateString(value: string): boolean {\n  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !DATE_PATTERN.test(value)) return false;\n  try {\n    return formatDate(toUTCDate(value)) === value;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Menambah sejumlah hari kalender (boleh negatif). */\nexport function addDays(date: Date, days: number): Date {\n  const result = new Date(date.getTime());\n  result.setUTCDate(result.getUTCDate() + days);\n  return result;\n}\n\n/** Hari dalam seminggu UTC: 0 = Minggu ... 6 = Sabtu. */\nexport function getDayOfWeek(date: Date): number {\n  return date.getUTCDay();\n}\n\n/** Negatif bila a < b, 0 bila sama, positif bila a > b. */\nexport function compareDates(a: Date, b: Date): number {\n  return a.getTime() - b.getTime();\n}\n\n/** Selisih hari kalender antara dua tanggal. */\nexport function diffInCalendarDays(a: Date, b: Date): number {\n  return Math.round((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / MS_PER_DAY);\n}\n\nexport function minDate(a: Date, b: Date): Date {\n  return compareDates(a, b) <= 0 ? a : b;\n}\n\nexport function maxDate(a: Date, b: Date): Date {\n  return compareDates(a, b) >= 0 ? a : b;\n}\n\nexport const WEEKDAY_NAMES_ID = [\n  'Minggu',\n  'Senin',\n  'Selasa',\n  'Rabu',\n  'Kamis',\n  'Jumat',\n  'Sabtu',\n] as const;\n\nexport function weekdayName(day: number): string {\n  return WEEKDAY_NAMES_ID[day] ?? '';\n}\n\n/** Format tampilan pendek untuk UI, mis. `23 Okt 2026`. */\nconst MONTH_NAMES_ID = [\n  'Jan',\n  'Feb',\n  'Mar',\n  'Apr',\n  'Mei',\n  'Jun',\n  'Jul',\n  'Agu',\n  'Sep',\n  'Okt',\n  'Nov',\n  'Des',\n] as const;\n\nexport function formatDisplayDate(value: string): string {\n  const date = toUTCDate(value);\n  return `${date.getUTCDate()} ${MONTH_NAMES_ID[date.getUTCMonth()]} ${date.getUTCFullYear()}`;\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "shared/src/calendar.ts",
  "instructions": "Calendar logic (weekend days, holidays, working day math)",
  "content": "/**\n * Kalender proyek: akhir pekan dan hari libur.\n * Durasi tugas hanya menghitung hari kerja.\n */\n\nimport { addDays, compareDates, formatDate, getDayOfWeek, toUTCDate } from './dates.js';\n\nexport interface CalendarConfig {\n  /** 0 = Minggu ... 6 = Sabtu. */\n  weekendDays: number[];\n  /** Daftar tanggal libur `YYYY-MM-DD`. */\n  holidays: string[];\n}\n\nexport const DEFAULT_WEEKEND_DAYS: number[] = [0, 6];\n\nexport function weekendDaysToString(days: number[]): string {\n  return [...new Set(days)].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(',');\n}\n\nexport function parseWeekendDays(value: string | null | undefined): number[] {\n  if (!value) return [...DEFAULT_WEEKEND_DAYS];\n  const days = value\n    .split(',')\n    .map((part) => part.trim())\n    .filter((part) => part.length > 0)\n    .map((part) => Number(part))\n    .filter((day) => Number.isInteger(day) && day >= 0 && day <= 6);\n  return [...new Set(days)].sort((a, b) => a - b);\n}\n\n/**\n * Mengembalikan pesan kesalahan bila daftar akhir pekan tidak valid,\n * atau null bila valid. Kalender tanpa satu pun hari kerja ditolak.\n */\nexport function validateWeekendDays(days: number[]): string | null {\n  if (!Array.isArray(days) || days.length === 0) {\n    return 'Akhir pekan tidak boleh kosong.';\n  }\n  for (const day of days) {\n    if (!Number.isInteger(day) || day < 0 || day > 6) {\n      return 'Hari akhir pekan harus berupa angka 0 sampai 6.';\n    }\n  }\n  const unique = new Set(days);\n  if (unique.size !== days.length) {\n    return 'Hari akhir pekan tidak boleh duplikat.';\n  }\n  if (unique.size >= 7) {\n    return 'Akhir pekan tidak boleh berisi ketujuh hari.';\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function isWeekend(date: Date, calendar: CalendarConfig): boolean {\n  return calendar.weekendDays.includes(getDayOfWeek(date));\n}\n\nexport function isHoliday(date: Date, calendar: CalendarConfig): boolean {\n  return calendar.holidays.includes(formatDate(date));\n}\n\nexport function isWorkingDay(date: Date, calendar: CalendarConfig): boolean {\n  return !isWeekend(date, calendar) && !isHoliday(date, calendar);\n}\n\n/** Hari kerja pada atau setelah `date`. */\nexport function nextWorkingDay(date: Date, calendar: CalendarConfig): Date {\n  let cursor = date;\n  // Pengaman agar tidak terjadi infinite loop pada konfigurasi aneh.\n  for (let guard = 0; guard < 4000; guard += 1) {\n    if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) return cursor;\n    cursor = addDays(cursor, 1);\n  }\n  throw new Error('Tidak menemukan hari kerja dalam rentang yang wajar.');\n}\n\n/** Hari kerja pada atau sebelum `date`. */\nexport function previousWorkingDay(date: Date, calendar: CalendarConfig): Date {\n  let cursor = date;\n  for (let guard = 0; guard < 4000; guard += 1) {\n    if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) return cursor;\n    cursor = addDays(cursor, -1);\n  }\n  throw new Error('Tidak menemukan hari kerja dalam rentang yang wajar.');\n}\n\n/**\n * Menggeser hari kerja sebanyak `count` langkah (boleh negatif).\n * `date` harus merupakan hari kerja.\n */\nexport function shiftWorkingDays(\n  date: Date,\n  count: number,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): Date {\n  if (!Number.isInteger(count)) {\n    throw new Error('Jumlah pergeseran hari kerja harus bilangan bulat.');\n  }\n  if (count === 0) return date;\n  const step = count > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n  let remaining = Math.abs(count);\n  let cursor = date;\n  while (remaining > 0) {\n    cursor = addDays(cursor, step);\n    if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) remaining -= 1;\n  }\n  return cursor;\n}\n\n/** Jumlah hari kerja pada rentang `start`..`end` (inklusif). */\nexport function workingDaysBetween(\n  start: Date,\n  end: Date,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): number {\n  if (compareDates(start, end) > 0) return 0;\n  let count = 0;\n  let cursor = start;\n  while (compareDates(cursor, end) <= 0) {\n    if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) count += 1;\n    cursor = addDays(cursor, 1);\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\n/** Daftar tanggal hari kerja pada rentang `start`..`end` (inklusif). */\nexport function workingDaysList(\n  start: Date,\n  end: Date,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): Date[] {\n  const result: Date[] = [];\n  let cursor = start;\n  while (compareDates(cursor, end) <= 0) {\n    if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) result.push(cursor);\n    cursor = addDays(cursor, 1);\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\n/** Membuat konfigurasi kalender dari data proyek. */\nexport function createCalendar(\n  weekendDays: number[] | string,\n  holidays: string[] = [],\n): CalendarConfig {\n  const days = typeof weekendDays === 'string' ? parseWeekendDays(weekendDays) : weekendDays;\n  return { weekendDays: days, holidays: [...holidays] };\n}\n\nexport { toUTCDate };\n"
}

{
  "path": "shared/src/scheduler.ts",
  "instructions": "Pure scheduling engine: topo sort, dependency bounds, parent roll-up",
  "content": "/**\n * Mesin penjadwalan (bagian 6.4 - 6.6).\n * Fungsi murni: dipakai server sebagai sumber kebenaran dan client untuk pratinjau drag.\n */\n\nimport {\n  type CalendarConfig,\n  nextWorkingDay,\n  shiftWorkingDays,\n  workingDaysBetween,\n} from './calendar.js';\nimport { compareDates, formatDate, maxDate, toUTCDate } from './dates.js';\nimport type { DependencyType } from './types.js';\n\nexport interface SchedulableTask {\n  id: string;\n  parentId: string | null;\n  position: number;\n  durationDays: number;\n  startDate: string;\n  progress?: number;\n  priority?: string;\n  name?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface SchedulableDependency {\n  predecessorId: string;\n  successorId: string;\n  type: DependencyType;\n  lagDays: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ScheduledTask {\n  id: string;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n  durationDays: number;\n  progress: number;\n  isParent: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface ScheduleOutput {\n  tasks: ScheduledTask[];\n  byId: Map<string, ScheduledTask>;\n}\n\nexport const MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH = 3;\n\nexport class ScheduleCycleError extends Error {\n  readonly cycle: string[];\n\n  constructor(cycle: string[]) {\n    super(`Siklus dependensi terdeteksi: ${cycle.join(' -> ')}`);\n    this.name = 'ScheduleCycleError';\n    this.cycle = cycle;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Batas mulai penerus menurut satu dependensi (bagian 6.4). */\nexport function dependencyBound(\n  predecessor: { scheduledStart: string; scheduledEnd: string },\n  dependency: SchedulableDependency,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): Date {\n  if (dependency.type === 'FS') {\n    return shiftWorkingDays(\n      toUTCDate(predecessor.scheduledEnd),\n      1 + dependency.lagDays,\n      calendar,\n    );\n  }\n  return shiftWorkingDays(\n    toUTCDate(predecessor.scheduledStart),\n    dependency.lagDays,\n    calendar,\n  );\n}\n\n/** Parent (summary) adalah tugas yang memiliki minimal satu anak. */\nexport function getParentIds(tasks: SchedulableTask[]): Set<string> {\n  const parents = new Set<string>();\n  const ids = new Set(tasks.map((task) => task.id));\n  for (const task of tasks) {\n    if (task.parentId && ids.has(task.parentId)) parents.add(task.parentId);\n  }\n  return parents;\n}\n\n/** Hanya tugas daun yang boleh punya dependensi dan assignment. */\nexport function getLeafTaskIds(tasks: SchedulableTask[]): Set<string> {\n  const parents = getParentIds(tasks);\n  return new Set(tasks.filter((task) => !parents.has(task.id)).map((task) => task.id));\n}\n\n/** Kedalaman tugas, 1 untuk tugas akar. */\nexport function getTaskDepth(tasks: SchedulableTask[], taskId: string): number {\n  const byId = new Map(tasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  let depth = 1;\n  let current = byId.get(taskId);\n  const guard = new Set<string>();\n  while (current?.parentId) {\n    if (guard.has(current.id)) break;\n    guard.add(current.id);\n    const parent = byId.get(current.parentId);\n    if (!parent) break;\n    depth += 1;\n    current = parent;\n  }\n  return depth;\n}\n\n/** Jumlah level maksimal di bawah tugas (0 bila tidak punya anak). */\nexport function getSubtreeExtraDepth(tasks: SchedulableTask[], taskId: string): number {\n  const childrenOf = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const task of tasks) {\n    if (!task.parentId) continue;\n    const list = childrenOf.get(task.parentId) ?? [];\n    list.push(task.id);\n    childrenOf.set(task.parentId, list);\n  }\n  let extra = 0;\n  const stack: Array<{ id: string; level: number }> = [{ id: taskId, level: 0 }];\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    const node = stack.pop() as { id: string; level: number };\n    extra = Math.max(extra, node.level);\n    for (const childId of childrenOf.get(node.id) ?? []) {\n      stack.push({ id: childId, level: node.level + 1 });\n    }\n  }\n  return extra;\n}\n\n/** Semua keturunan sebuah tugas (termasuk dirinya sendiri). */\nexport function getSubtreeIds(tasks: SchedulableTask[], taskId: string): string[] {\n  const childrenOf = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const task of tasks) {\n    if (!task.parentId) continue;\n    const list = childrenOf.get(task.parentId) ?? [];\n    list.push(task.id);\n    childrenOf.set(task.parentId, list);\n  }\n  const result: string[] = [];\n  const stack = [taskId];\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    const id = stack.pop() as string;\n    result.push(id);\n    for (const childId of childrenOf.get(id) ?? []) stack.push(childId);\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\n/**\n * Mendeteksi siklus pada graf ketergantungan.\n * Mengembalikan lintasan siklus (mis. `[a, b, a]`) atau null bila bebas siklus.\n */\nexport function detectCycle(\n  dependencies: SchedulableDependency[],\n  taskIds?: Iterable<string>,\n): string[] | null {\n  const adjacency = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  const nodes = new Set<string>(taskIds ? [...taskIds] : []);\n  for (const dependency of dependencies) {\n    nodes.add(dependency.predecessorId);\n    nodes.add(dependency.successorId);\n    const list = adjacency.get(dependency.predecessorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency.successorId);\n    adjacency.set(dependency.predecessorId, list);\n  }\n\n  const WHITE = 0;\n  const GRAY = 1;\n  const BLACK = 2;\n  const color = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const node of nodes) color.set(node, WHITE);\n  const path: string[] = [];\n\n  const visit = (node: string): string[] | null => {\n    color.set(node, GRAY);\n    path.push(node);\n    for (const next of adjacency.get(node) ?? []) {\n      const state = color.get(next) ?? WHITE;\n      if (state === GRAY) {\n        const start = path.indexOf(next);\n        return [...path.slice(start), next];\n      }\n      if (state === WHITE) {\n        const found = visit(next);\n        if (found) return found;\n      }\n    }\n    path.pop();\n    color.set(node, BLACK);\n    return null;\n  };\n\n  for (const node of nodes) {\n    if ((color.get(node) ?? WHITE) === WHITE) {\n      const found = visit(node);\n      if (found) return found;\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Urutan topologis tugas daun memakai algoritma Kahn. */\nexport function topologicalOrder(\n  tasks: SchedulableTask[],\n  dependencies: SchedulableDependency[],\n): string[] {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  const byId = new Map(tasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const adjacency = new Map<string, SchedulableDependency[]>();\n  const indegree = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const id of leaves) {\n    adjacency.set(id, []);\n    indegree.set(id, 0);\n  }\n  for (const dependency of dependencies) {\n    if (!leaves.has(dependency.predecessorId) || !leaves.has(dependency.successorId)) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    adjacency.get(dependency.predecessorId)?.push(dependency);\n    indegree.set(dependency.successorId, (indegree.get(dependency.successorId) ?? 0) + 1);\n  }\n\n  const compareByPosition = (a: string, b: string): number => {\n    const taskA = byId.get(a);\n    const taskB = byId.get(b);\n    if (!taskA || !taskB) return 0;\n    if (taskA.position !== taskB.position) return taskA.position - taskB.position;\n    return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;\n  };\n\n  const ready = [...leaves].filter((id) => (indegree.get(id) ?? 0) === 0).sort(compareByPosition);\n  const order: string[] = [];\n  while (ready.length > 0) {\n    const id = ready.shift() as string;\n    order.push(id);\n    for (const dependency of adjacency.get(id) ?? []) {\n      const remaining = (indegree.get(dependency.successorId) ?? 0) - 1;\n      indegree.set(dependency.successorId, remaining);\n      if (remaining === 0) {\n        ready.push(dependency.successorId);\n        ready.sort(compareByPosition);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (order.length !== leaves.size) {\n    const cycle = detectCycle(dependencies, leaves) ?? [];\n    throw new ScheduleCycleError(cycle);\n  }\n  return order;\n}\n\n/**\n * Menghitung `scheduledStart` dan `scheduledEnd` seluruh tugas,\n * lalu menggulung (roll-up) tugas induk dari bawah ke atas.\n */\nexport function computeSchedule(\n  tasks: SchedulableTask[],\n  dependencies: SchedulableDependency[],\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): ScheduleOutput {\n  const byId = new Map(tasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const parents = getParentIds(tasks);\n  const order = topologicalOrder(tasks, dependencies);\n\n  const predecessorsOf = new Map<string, SchedulableDependency[]>();\n  for (const dependency of dependencies) {\n    if (parents.has(dependency.predecessorId) || parents.has(dependency.successorId)) continue;\n    const list = predecessorsOf.get(dependency.successorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency);\n    predecessorsOf.set(dependency.successorId, list);\n  }\n\n  const byIdResult = new Map<string, ScheduledTask>();\n\n  for (const id of order) {\n    const task = byId.get(id);\n    if (!task) continue;\n    let bound: Date | null = null;\n    for (const dependency of predecessorsOf.get(id) ?? []) {\n      const predecessor = byIdResult.get(dependency.predecessorId);\n      if (!predecessor) continue;\n      const candidate = dependencyBound(predecessor, dependency, calendar);\n      bound = bound === null ? candidate : maxDate(bound, candidate);\n    }\n    const earliestStart = toUTCDate(task.startDate);\n    const rawStart = bound === null ? earliestStart : maxDate(earliestStart, bound);\n    const start = nextWorkingDay(rawStart, calendar);\n    const durationDays = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays));\n    const end = shiftWorkingDays(start, durationDays - 1, calendar);\n    byIdResult.set(id, {\n      id,\n      scheduledStart: formatDate(start),\n      scheduledEnd: formatDate(end),\n      durationDays,\n      progress: clampProgress(task.progress),\n      isParent: false,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const parentsByDepth = [...parents]\n    .map((id) => ({ id, depth: getTaskDepth(tasks, id) }))\n    .sort((a, b) => b.depth - a.depth);\n\n  for (const { id } of parentsByDepth) {\n    const children = tasks\n      .filter((task) => task.parentId === id)\n      .map((task) => byIdResult.get(task.id))\n      .filter((task): task is ScheduledTask => Boolean(task));\n    if (children.length === 0) continue;\n    let start = toUTCDate(children[0].scheduledStart);\n    let end = toUTCDate(children[0].scheduledEnd);\n    for (const child of children) {\n      start = compareDates(start, toUTCDate(child.scheduledStart)) <= 0 ? start : toUTCDate(child.scheduledStart);\n      end = compareDates(end, toUTCDate(child.scheduledEnd)) >= 0 ? end : toUTCDate(child.scheduledEnd);\n    }\n    const totalDuration = children.reduce((sum, child) => sum + child.durationDays, 0);\n    const weightedProgress =\n      totalDuration > 0\n        ? children.reduce((sum, child) => sum + child.progress * child.durationDays, 0) /\n          totalDuration\n        : 0;\n    byIdResult.set(id, {\n      id,\n      scheduledStart: formatDate(start),\n      scheduledEnd: formatDate(end),\n      durationDays: workingDaysBetween(start, end, calendar),\n      progress: Math.round(weightedProgress),\n      isParent: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return { tasks: tasks.map((task) => byIdResult.get(task.id) as ScheduledTask), byId: byIdResult };\n}\n\nfunction clampProgress(value: number | undefined): number {\n  if (typeof value !== 'number' || Number.isNaN(value)) return 0;\n  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(value)));\n}\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Now cpm.ts.
> 
> Input: tasks (with id, name, durationDays, scheduledStart, scheduledEnd, parentId) and dependencies, calendar, projectStart.
> 
> Steps:
> - leaves only
> - ord(d) = workingDaysBetween(projectStart, d, calendar) - 1
> - ES = ord(scheduledStart); EF = ord(scheduledEnd)
> - PE = max EF
> - backward: LF for no-successor = PE; else min over successors.
>   - FS: LS_succ - 1 - lag
>   - SS: LS_succ - lag + dur_pred - 1
> - LS = LF - dur + 1; totalFloat = max(0, LS-ES)
> - critical = totalFloat === 0
> 
> Careful with lag in working days vs ordinal: LS_succ is an ordinal (working-day index relative to project start). So "LS_succ - 1" means one working day before. And "- lag" subtracts lag working days. Consistent.
> 
> But note: ord() computed from project start; if a task starts before project start (negative ordinal), ord could be negative. workingDaysBetween(projectStart, d) returns 0 if d < projectStart → ord = -1. Edge case, acceptable.
> 
> Projects' tasks all start >= project start since startDate >= project start normally. Our seed has project start = earliest task start.
> 
> criticalPaths: build from critical tasks.
> 
> Implementation for paths: 
> - criticalSet. Edges between critical tasks (using deps where both critical).
> - Find "starts": critical tasks with no critical predecessor.
> - DFS from each start following critical successors, collecting path; when multiple branches, produce multiple paths. Return all maximal paths.
> 
> Then sort by ES of first task.
> 
> Also compute project start/end/workingDays for analysis summary.
> 
> Let me write cpm.ts.
> 
> Also the blueprint says "Kritis jika totalFloat = 0" and totalFloat minimal 0.
> 
> For projectEnd date: max scheduledEnd among leaves.
> 
> Let me write.

{
  "path": "shared/src/cpm.ts",
  "instructions": "CPM critical path calculation with forward/backward pass",
  "content": "/**\n * Critical Path Method (bagian 6.8). Hanya tugas daun yang dihitung.\n */\n\nimport { type CalendarConfig, workingDaysBetween } from './calendar.js';\nimport { formatDate, toUTCDate } from './dates.js';\nimport { getLeafTaskIds } from './scheduler.js';\nimport type { DependencyType } from './types.js';\n\nexport interface CpmTaskInput {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  parentId: string | null;\n  durationDays: number;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmDependencyInput {\n  predecessorId: string;\n  successorId: string;\n  type: DependencyType;\n  lagDays: number;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmTask {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  durationDays: number;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n  es: number;\n  ef: number;\n  ls: number;\n  lf: number;\n  totalFloat: number;\n  critical: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmResult {\n  tasks: CpmTask[];\n  byId: Map<string, CpmTask>;\n  /** Ordinal hari kerja akhir proyek (PE). */\n  projectEndOrdinal: number;\n  projectStartDate: string;\n  projectEndDate: string;\n  totalWorkingDays: number;\n  criticalTasks: string[];\n  criticalPaths: string[][];\n}\n\n/** Ordinal hari kerja dari `projectStart` (inklusif) sampai `date`. */\nexport function workingDayOrdinal(\n  projectStart: string,\n  date: string,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): number {\n  return workingDaysBetween(toUTCDate(projectStart), toUTCDate(date), calendar) - 1;\n}\n\nexport function computeCpm(\n  tasks: CpmTaskInput[],\n  dependencies: CpmDependencyInput[],\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n  projectStart: string,\n): CpmResult {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  const leafTasks = tasks.filter((task) => leaves.has(task.id));\n  const leafIds = new Set(leafTasks.map((task) => task.id));\n\n  const deps = dependencies.filter(\n    (dependency) =>\n      leafIds.has(dependency.predecessorId) && leafIds.has(dependency.successorId),\n  );\n\n  const successorsOf = new Map<string, CpmDependencyInput[]>();\n  for (const dependency of deps) {\n    const list = successorsOf.get(dependency.predecessorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency);\n    successorsOf.set(dependency.predecessorId, list);\n  }\n\n  const es = new Map<string, number>();\n  const ef = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const task of leafTasks) {\n    const start = workingDayOrdinal(projectStart, task.scheduledStart, calendar);\n    const duration = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays));\n    es.set(task.id, start);\n    ef.set(task.id, start + duration - 1);\n  }\n\n  let projectEndOrdinal = 0;\n  for (const value of ef.values()) projectEndOrdinal = Math.max(projectEndOrdinal, value);\n\n  const ls = new Map<string, number>();\n  const lf = new Map<string, number>();\n\n  // Urutan backward: proses penerus lebih dulu. Cari urutan aman dengan\n  // mengurutkan menurut EF menurun (graf tanpa siklus -> penerus selalu EF lebih besar).\n  const backwardOrder = [...leafTasks].sort(\n    (a, b) => (ef.get(b.id) ?? 0) - (ef.get(a.id) ?? 0),\n  );\n\n  for (const task of backwardOrder) {\n    const successors = successorsOf.get(task.id) ?? [];\n    const duration = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays));\n    let latestFinish: number;\n    if (successors.length === 0) {\n      latestFinish = projectEndOrdinal;\n    } else {\n      latestFinish = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\n      for (const dependency of successors) {\n        const successorLs = ls.get(dependency.successorId);\n        if (successorLs === undefined) continue;\n        const candidate =\n          dependency.type === 'FS'\n            ? successorLs - 1 - dependency.lagDays\n            : successorLs - dependency.lagDays + duration - 1;\n        latestFinish = Math.min(latestFinish, candidate);\n      }\n      if (!Number.isFinite(latestFinish)) latestFinish = projectEndOrdinal;\n    }\n    lf.set(task.id, latestFinish);\n    ls.set(task.id, latestFinish - duration + 1);\n  }\n\n  const results: CpmTask[] = leafTasks.map((task) => {\n    const taskEs = es.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskEf = ef.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskLs = ls.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskLf = lf.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const totalFloat = Math.max(0, taskLs - taskEs);\n    return {\n      id: task.id,\n      name: task.name,\n      durationDays: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays)),\n      scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n      scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n      es: taskEs,\n      ef: taskEf,\n      ls: taskLs,\n      lf: taskLf,\n      totalFloat,\n      critical: totalFloat === 0,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const byId = new Map(results.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const criticalTasks = results\n    .filter((task) => task.critical)\n    .sort((a, b) => a.es - b.es)\n    .map((task) => task.id);\n\n  const criticalSet = new Set(criticalTasks);\n  const criticalSuccessors = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  const hasCriticalPredecessor = new Set<string>();\n  for (const dependency of deps) {\n    if (!criticalSet.has(dependency.predecessorId) || !criticalSet.has(dependency.successorId)) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const list = criticalSuccessors.get(dependency.predecessorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency.successorId);\n    criticalSuccessors.set(dependency.predecessorId, list);\n    hasCriticalPredecessor.add(dependency.successorId);\n  }\n\n  const pathStarts = criticalTasks.filter((id) => !hasCriticalPredecessor.has(id));\n  const criticalPaths: string[][] = [];\n  const walk = (current: string, path: string[], visited: Set<string>): void => {\n    const nextNodes = (criticalSuccessors.get(current) ?? []).filter((id) => !visited.has(id));\n    if (nextNodes.length === 0) {\n      criticalPaths.push([...path]);\n      return;\n    }\n    for (const next of nextNodes) {\n      visited.add(next);\n      path.push(next);\n      walk(next, path, visited);\n      path.pop();\n      visited.delete(next);\n    }\n  };\n  for (const start of pathStarts) {\n    walk(start, [start], new Set([start]));\n  }\n\n  criticalPaths.sort((a, b) => (byId.get(a[0])?.es ?? 0) - (byId.get(b[0])?.es ?? 0));\n\n  let projectEndDate = projectStart;\n  for (const task of leafTasks) {\n    if (task.scheduledEnd > projectEndDate) projectEndDate = task.scheduledEnd;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    tasks: results,\n    byId,\n    projectEndOrdinal,\n    projectStartDate: projectStart,\n    projectEndDate,\n    totalWorkingDays: workingDaysBetween(\n      toUTCDate(projectStart),\n      toUTCDate(projectEndDate),\n      calendar,\n    ),\n    criticalTasks,\n    criticalPaths,\n  };\n}\n\nexport { formatDate };\n"
}

{
  "path": "shared/src/pert.ts",
  "instructions": "PERT activity-on-node network builder",
  "content": "/**\n * Diagram PERT / activity-on-node (bagian 6.9).\n * Estimasi te dan variansi bersifat informasi tambahan, tidak mengubah jadwal.\n */\n\nimport type { CpmDependencyInput, CpmResult } from './cpm.js';\nimport type { DependencyType } from './types.js';\n\nexport interface PertTaskInput {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  optimisticDays?: number | null;\n  pessimisticDays?: number | null;\n}\n\nexport interface PertNode {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  durationDays: number;\n  es: number;\n  ef: number;\n  ls: number;\n  lf: number;\n  slack: number;\n  critical: boolean;\n  /** Kolom tata letak = kedalaman jalur terpanjang dari node awal. */\n  column: number;\n  optimisticDays: number | null;\n  pessimisticDays: number | null;\n  expectedDays: number | null;\n  variance: number | null;\n}\n\nexport interface PertEdge {\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  type: DependencyType;\n  lagDays: number;\n  critical: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface PertNetwork {\n  nodes: PertNode[];\n  edges: PertEdge[];\n  totalVariance: number | null;\n  stdDeviation: number | null;\n}\n\n/** Estimasi PERT: te = (o + 4m + p) / 6, variansi = ((p - o) / 6)^2. */\nexport function pertEstimate(\n  optimisticDays: number,\n  mostLikelyDays: number,\n  pessimisticDays: number,\n): { expectedDays: number; variance: number } {\n  const expectedDays = (optimisticDays + 4 * mostLikelyDays + pessimisticDays) / 6;\n  const variance = ((pessimisticDays - optimisticDays) / 6) ** 2;\n  return { expectedDays, variance };\n}\n\nexport function buildPertNetwork(\n  tasks: PertTaskInput[],\n  dependencies: CpmDependencyInput[],\n  cpm: CpmResult,\n): PertNetwork {\n  const taskById = new Map(tasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const cpmNodes = cpm.tasks;\n  const nodeIds = new Set(cpmNodes.map((task) => task.id));\n\n  const edges: PertEdge[] = dependencies\n    .filter((dependency) => nodeIds.has(dependency.predecessorId) && nodeIds.has(dependency.successorId))\n    .map((dependency) => ({\n      from: dependency.predecessorId,\n      to: dependency.successorId,\n      type: dependency.type,\n      lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n      critical:\n        (cpm.byId.get(dependency.predecessorId)?.critical ?? false) &&\n        (cpm.byId.get(dependency.successorId)?.critical ?? false),\n    }));\n\n  const predecessors = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const edge of edges) {\n    const list = predecessors.get(edge.to) ?? [];\n    list.push(edge.from);\n    predecessors.set(edge.to, list);\n  }\n\n  const columnCache = new Map<string, number>();\n  const columnOf = (id: string, guard = new Set<string>()): number => {\n    const cached = columnCache.get(id);\n    if (cached !== undefined) return cached;\n    if (guard.has(id)) return 0;\n    guard.add(id);\n    const preds = predecessors.get(id) ?? [];\n    const column = preds.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(...preds.map((pred) => columnOf(pred, guard) + 1));\n    guard.delete(id);\n    columnCache.set(id, column);\n    return column;\n  };\n\n  const nodes: PertNode[] = cpmNodes.map((task) => {\n    const input = taskById.get(task.id);\n    const optimistic = input?.optimisticDays ?? null;\n    const pessimistic = input?.pessimisticDays ?? null;\n    const hasEstimate =\n      typeof optimistic === 'number' &&\n      typeof pessimistic === 'number' &&\n      optimistic > 0 &&\n      pessimistic >= optimistic;\n    const estimate = hasEstimate\n      ? pertEstimate(optimistic, task.durationDays, pessimistic)\n      : null;\n    return {\n      id: task.id,\n      name: task.name,\n      durationDays: task.durationDays,\n      es: task.es,\n      ef: task.ef,\n      ls: task.ls,\n      lf: task.lf,\n      slack: task.totalFloat,\n      critical: task.critical,\n      column: columnOf(task.id),\n      optimisticDays: hasEstimate ? optimistic : null,\n      pessimisticDays: hasEstimate ? pessimistic : null,\n      expectedDays: estimate ? round(estimate.expectedDays, 3) : null,\n      variance: estimate ? round(estimate.variance, 4) : null,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const criticalNodes = nodes.filter((node) => node.critical);\n  const hasAnyEstimate = criticalNodes.some((node) => node.variance !== null);\n  const totalVariance = hasAnyEstimate\n    ? round(\n        criticalNodes.reduce((sum, node) => sum + (node.variance ?? 0), 0),\n        4,\n      )\n    : null;\n  const stdDeviation = totalVariance === null ? null : round(Math.sqrt(totalVariance), 4);\n\n  return { nodes, edges, totalVariance, stdDeviation };\n}\n\nfunction round(value: number, digits: number): number {\n  const factor = 10 ** digits;\n  return Math.round(value * factor) / factor;\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "shared/src/cost.ts",
  "instructions": "Cost calculation and overallocation detection",
  "content": "/**\n * Biaya dan beban kerja (bagian 6.10).\n * Tarif harian = gaji bulanan / 22 hari kerja.\n */\n\nimport { type CalendarConfig, isWorkingDay } from './calendar.js';\nimport { compareDates, toUTCDate } from './dates.js';\nimport { getLeafTaskIds } from './scheduler.js';\n\nexport const WORKING_DAYS_PER_MONTH = 22;\n\nexport interface CostTask {\n  id: string;\n  name?: string;\n  parentId: string | null;\n  durationDays: number;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CostAssignment {\n  id?: string;\n  taskId: string;\n  resourceId: string;\n  allocationPercent: number;\n}\n\nexport interface CostResource {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  salary: number;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskCost {\n  taskId: string;\n  cost: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ResourceCost {\n  resourceId: string;\n  totalCost: number;\n  totalWorkingDays: number;\n}\n\nexport interface OverallocationWarning {\n  resourceId: string;\n  date: string;\n  totalPercent: number;\n  taskIds: string[];\n}\n\n/** Tarif harian seorang anggota tim. */\nexport function dailyRate(salary: number): number {\n  return salary / WORKING_DAYS_PER_MONTH;\n}\n\n/** Biaya satu tugas = jumlah (tarif harian x durasi x persen alokasi). */\nexport function computeTaskCost(\n  task: CostTask,\n  assignments: CostAssignment[],\n  resources: CostResource[],\n): number {\n  const salaryById = new Map(resources.map((resource) => [resource.id, resource.salary]));\n  let total = 0;\n  for (const assignment of assignments) {\n    if (assignment.taskId !== task.id) continue;\n    const salary = salaryById.get(assignment.resourceId);\n    if (salary === undefined) continue;\n    const percent = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, assignment.allocationPercent));\n    total += dailyRate(salary) * task.durationDays * (percent / 100);\n  }\n  return total;\n}\n\n/** Biaya seluruh tugas daun (tugas induk tidak dihitung terpisah). */\nexport function computeTaskCosts(\n  tasks: CostTask[],\n  assignments: CostAssignment[],\n  resources: CostResource[],\n): TaskCost[] {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  return tasks\n    .filter((task) => leaves.has(task.id))\n    .map((task) => ({\n      taskId: task.id,\n      cost: computeTaskCost(task, assignments, resources),\n    }));\n}\n\nexport function totalProjectCost(\n  tasks: CostTask[],\n  assignments: CostAssignment[],\n  resources: CostResource[],\n): number {\n  return computeTaskCosts(tasks, assignments, resources).reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.cost, 0);\n}\n\n/** Total biaya per anggota tim beserta total hari kerja yang dialokasikan. */\nexport function computeResourceCosts(\n  tasks: CostTask[],\n  assignments: CostAssignment[],\n  resources: CostResource[],\n): ResourceCost[] {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  const leafTasks = tasks.filter((task) => leaves.has(task.id));\n  const taskById = new Map(leafTasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  return resources.map((resource) => {\n    let totalCost = 0;\n    let totalWorkingDays = 0;\n    for (const assignment of assignments) {\n      if (assignment.resourceId !== resource.id) continue;\n      const task = taskById.get(assignment.taskId);\n      if (!task) continue;\n      const percent = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, assignment.allocationPercent));\n      const proportion = percent / 100;\n      totalCost += dailyRate(resource.salary) * task.durationDays * proportion;\n      totalWorkingDays += task.durationDays * proportion;\n    }\n    return { resourceId: resource.id, totalCost, totalWorkingDays };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Peringatan over-alokasi: seorang anggota dialokasikan lebih dari 100%\n * pada satu hari kerja karena tugas yang saling tumpang tindih.\n */\nexport function findOverallocations(\n  tasks: CostTask[],\n  assignments: CostAssignment[],\n  resources: CostResource[],\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): OverallocationWarning[] {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  const leafTasks = tasks.filter((task) => leaves.has(task.id));\n  const taskById = new Map(leafTasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const relevant = assignments.filter((assignment) => taskById.has(assignment.taskId));\n  if (relevant.length === 0) return [];\n\n  const sorted = [...relevant].sort((a, b) => {\n    const taskA = taskById.get(a.taskId) as CostTask;\n    const taskB = taskById.get(b.taskId) as CostTask;\n    if (taskA.scheduledStart !== taskB.scheduledStart) {\n      return taskA.scheduledStart < taskB.scheduledStart ? -1 : 1;\n    }\n    return a.resourceId < b.resourceId ? -1 : a.resourceId > b.resourceId ? 1 : 0;\n  });\n\n  const warnings = new Map<string, OverallocationWarning>();\n  for (const assignment of sorted) {\n    const task = taskById.get(assignment.taskId) as CostTask;\n    const start = toUTCDate(task.scheduledStart);\n    const end = toUTCDate(task.scheduledEnd);\n    let cursor = start;\n    while (compareDates(cursor, end) <= 0) {\n      if (isWorkingDay(cursor, calendar)) {\n        const date = cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n        const key = `${assignment.resourceId}|${date}`;\n        const existing = warnings.get(key) ?? {\n          resourceId: assignment.resourceId,\n          date,\n          totalPercent: 0,\n          taskIds: [],\n        };\n        existing.totalPercent += assignment.allocationPercent;\n        if (!existing.taskIds.includes(assignment.taskId)) existing.taskIds.push(assignment.taskId);\n        warnings.set(key, existing);\n      }\n      cursor = new Date(cursor.getTime() + 86_400_000);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const resourceIds = new Set(resources.map((resource) => resource.id));\n  return [...warnings.values()]\n    .filter((warning) => warning.totalPercent > 100 && resourceIds.has(warning.resourceId))\n    .sort((a, b) => (a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0));\n}\n\nconst idrFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', {\n  style: 'currency',\n  currency: 'IDR',\n  maximumFractionDigits: 0,\n});\n\n/** Format rupiah, mis. `Rp 15.000.000`. */\nexport function formatIDR(value: number): string {\n  const safe = Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;\n  return idrFormatter.format(Math.round(safe)).replace(/\\u00a0/g, ' ');\n}\n"
}

Dipakai server (validasi input) dan client (validasi form).\n */\n\nimport { z } from 'zod';\nimport { isValidDateString } from './dates.js';\nimport { validateWeekendDays } from './calendar.js';\n\nexport const prioritySchema = z.enum(['LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH'], {\n  errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Prioritas harus LOW, MEDIUM, atau HIGH.' }),\n});\n\nexport const dependencyTypeSchema = z.enum(['FS', 'SS'], {\n  errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Tipe dependensi harus FS atau SS.' }),\n});\n\nexport const dateOnlySchema = z\n  .string({ required_error: 'Tanggal wajib diisi.' })\n  .refine(isValidDateString, { message: 'Tanggal harus berformat YYYY-MM-DD dan valid.' });\n\nexport const hexColorSchema = z\n  .string({ required_error: 'Warna wajib diisi.' })\n  .regex(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/, 'Warna harus berupa kode hex, mis. #2563eb.');\n\nexport const weekendDaysSchema = z\n  .array(z.number().int().min(0).max(6), {\n    required_error: 'Daftar akhir pekan But since the graph is a DAG, and we need successors computed before predecessors... Using `topologicalOrder` from scheduler would be more correct: reverse topo order. But topologicalOrder requires SchedulableTask with position/startDate. Hmm. Let me implement a simple topological sort based on the deps in cpm using Kahn on leaf ids with indegree/outdegree, then reverse. That's more robust than EF sorting.

> ```ts
> // Urutan topologis sederhana (Kahn), lalu diproses terbalik.
> const indegree = new Map<string, number>();
> for (const id of leafIds) indegree.set(id, 0);
> for (const d of deps) indegree.set(d.successorId, (indegree.get(d.successorId) ?? 0) + 1);

> const topo: string[] = [];
> while (queue.length) {
>   const id = queue.shift()!;
>   topo.push(id);

>     indegree.set(d.successorId, n);
>     if (n === 0) queue.push(d.successorId);
>   }
> }
> const backwardOrder = [...topo].reverse().map(id => taskById.get(id)!).filter(Boolean);
> ```
