{
  "path": "shared/src/cpm.ts",
  "instructions": "Rewrite CPM with topological backward pass and no duplicate exports",
  "content": "/**\n * Critical Path Method (bagian 6.8). Hanya tugas daun yang dihitung.\n */\n\nimport { type CalendarConfig, workingDaysBetween } from './calendar.js';\nimport { toUTCDate } from './dates.js';\nimport { getLeafTaskIds } from './scheduler.js';\nimport type { DependencyType } from './types.js';\n\nexport interface CpmTaskInput {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  parentId: string | null;\n  durationDays: number;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmDependencyInput {\n  predecessorId: string;\n  successorId: string;\n  type: DependencyType;\n  lagDays: number;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmTask {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  durationDays: number;\n  scheduledStart: string;\n  scheduledEnd: string;\n  es: number;\n  ef: number;\n  ls: number;\n  lf: number;\n  totalFloat: number;\n  critical: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface CpmResult {\n  tasks: CpmTask[];\n  byId: Map<string, CpmTask>;\n  /** Ordinal hari kerja akhir proyek (PE). */\n  projectEndOrdinal: number;\n  projectStartDate: string;\n  projectEndDate: string;\n  totalWorkingDays: number;\n  criticalTasks: string[];\n  criticalPaths: string[][];\n}\n\n/** Ordinal hari kerja dari `projectStart` (inklusif) sampai `date`. */\nexport function workingDayOrdinal(\n  projectStart: string,\n  date: string,\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n): number {\n  return workingDaysBetween(toUTCDate(projectStart), toUTCDate(date), calendar) - 1;\n}\n\nexport function computeCpm(\n  tasks: CpmTaskInput[],\n  dependencies: CpmDependencyInput[],\n  calendar: CalendarConfig,\n  projectStart: string,\n): CpmResult {\n  const leaves = getLeafTaskIds(tasks);\n  const leafTasks = tasks.filter((task) => leaves.has(task.id));\n  const leafIds = new Set(leafTasks.map((task) => task.id));\n  const taskById = new Map(leafTasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n\n  const deps = dependencies.filter(\n    (dependency) =>\n      leafIds.has(dependency.predecessorId) && leafIds.has(dependency.successorId),\n  );\n\n  const successorsOf = new Map<string, CpmDependencyInput[]>();\n  const indegree = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const id of leafIds) indegree.set(id, 0);\n  for (const dependency of deps) {\n    const list = successorsOf.get(dependency.predecessorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency);\n    successorsOf.set(dependency.predecessorId, list);\n    indegree.set(dependency.successorId, (indegree.get(dependency.successorId) ?? 0) + 1);\n  }\n\n  const es = new Map<string, number>();\n  const ef = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const task of leafTasks) {\n    const start = workingDayOrdinal(projectStart, task.scheduledStart, calendar);\n    const duration = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays));\n    es.set(task.id, start);\n    ef.set(task.id, start + duration - 1);\n  }\n\n  let projectEndOrdinal = 0;\n  for (const value of ef.values()) projectEndOrdinal = Math.max(projectEndOrdinal, value);\n\n  const ls = new Map<string, number>();\n  const lf = new Map<string, number>();\n\n  // Urutan topologis (Kahn), lalu diproses terbalik agar penerus selesai lebih dulu.\n  const ready = [...leafIds].filter((id) => (indegree.get(id) ?? 0) === 0);\n  const topological: string[] = [];\n  while (ready.length > 0) {\n    const id = ready.shift() as string;\n    topological.push(id);\n    for (const dependency of successorsOf.get(id) ?? []) {\n      const remaining = (indegree.get(dependency.successorId) ?? 0) - 1;\n      indegree.set(dependency.successorId, remaining);\n      if (remaining === 0) ready.push(dependency.successorId);\n    }\n  }\n  for (const id of leafIds) {\n    if (!topological.includes(id)) topological.push(id);\n  }\n\n  for (const id of [...topological].reverse()) {\n    const task = taskById.get(id);\n    if (!task) continue;\n    const duration = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays));\n    const successors = successorsOf.get(id) ?? [];\n    let latestFinish: number;\n    if (successors.length === 0) {\n      latestFinish = projectEndOrdinal;\n    } else {\n      latestFinish = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\n      for (const dependency of successors) {\n        const successorLs = ls.get(dependency.successorId);\n        if (successorLs === undefined) continue;\n        const candidate =\n          dependency.type === 'FS'\n            ? successorLs - 1 - dependency.lagDays\n            : successorLs - dependency.lagDays + duration - 1;\n        latestFinish = Math.min(latestFinish, candidate);\n      }\n      if (!Number.isFinite(latestFinish)) latestFinish = projectEndOrdinal;\n    }\n    lf.set(id, latestFinish);\n    ls.set(id, latestFinish - duration + 1);\n  }\n\n  const results: CpmTask[] = leafTasks.map((task) => {\n    const taskEs = es.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskEf = ef.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskLs = ls.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const taskLf = lf.get(task.id) ?? 0;\n    const totalFloat = Math.max(0, taskLs - taskEs);\n    return {\n      id: task.id,\n      name: task.name,\n      durationDays: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(task.durationDays)),\n      scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n      scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n      es: taskEs,\n      ef: taskEf,\n      ls: taskLs,\n      lf: taskLf,\n      totalFloat,\n      critical: totalFloat === 0,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const byId = new Map(results.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n  const criticalTasks = results\n    .filter((task) => task.critical)\n    .sort((a, b) => a.es - b.es)\n    .map((task) => task.id);\n\n  const criticalSet = new Set(criticalTasks);\n  const criticalSuccessors = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  const hasCriticalPredecessor = new Set<string>();\n  for (const dependency of deps) {\n    if (!criticalSet.has(dependency.predecessorId) || !criticalSet.has(dependency.successorId)) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const list = criticalSuccessors.get(dependency.predecessorId) ?? [];\n    list.push(dependency.successorId);\n    criticalSuccessors.set(dependency.predecessorId, list);\n    hasCriticalPredecessor.add(dependency.successorId);\n  }\n\n  const pathStarts = criticalTasks.filter((id) => !hasCriticalPredecessor.has(id));\n  const criticalPaths: string[][] = [];\n  const walk = (current: string, path: string[], visited: Set<string>): void => {\n    const nextNodes = (criticalSuccessors.get(current) ?? []).filter((id) => !visited.has(id));\n    if (nextNodes.length === 0) {\n      criticalPaths.push([...path]);\n      return;\n    }\n    for (const next of nextNodes) {\n      visited.add(next);\n      path.push(next);\n      walk(next, path, visited);\n      path.pop();\n      visited.delete(next);\n    }\n  };\n  for (const start of pathStarts) {\n    walk(start, [start], new Set([start]));\n  }\n  criticalPaths.sort((a, b) => (byId.get(a[0])?.es ?? 0) - (byId.get(b[0])?.es ?? 0));\n\n  let projectEndDate = projectStart;\n  for (const task of leafTasks) {\n    if (task.scheduledEnd > projectEndDate) projectEndDate = task.scheduledEnd;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    tasks: results,\n    byId,\n    projectEndOrdinal,\n    projectStartDate: projectStart,\n    projectEndDate,\n    totalWorkingDays: workingDaysBetween(\n      toUTCDate(projectStart),\n      toUTCDate(projectEndDate),\n      calendar,\n    ),\n    criticalTasks,\n    criticalPaths,\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "path": "shared/src/cpm.test.ts",
  "instructions": "CPM tests including the mandatory CPM vector 6.11",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { createCalendar } from './calendar.js';\nimport { computeCpm, workingDayOrdinal } from './cpm.js';\nimport { computeSchedule, type SchedulableTask } from './scheduler.js';\n\nconst calendar = createCalendar([0, 6]);\n\nfunction leaf(id: string, durationDays: number, startDate: string, name = id): SchedulableTask {\n  return { id, parentId: null, position: 0, durationDays, startDate, name };\n}\n\ndescribe('vektor CPM wajib (bagian 6.11)', () => {\n  const tasks = [\n    leaf('A', 3, '2026-10-19', 'A'),\n    leaf('B', 2, '2026-10-19', 'B'),\n    leaf('C', 5, '2026-10-19', 'C'),\n    leaf('D', 1, '2026-10-19', 'D'),\n  ];\n  const dependencies = [\n    { predecessorId: 'A', successorId: 'B', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n    { predecessorId: 'A', successorId: 'C', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n    { predecessorId: 'B', successorId: 'D', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n    { predecessorId: 'C', successorId: 'D', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n  ];\n\n  it('menandai A, C, D kritis, B punya float 3, dan PE = 8', () => {\n    const schedule = computeSchedule(tasks, dependencies, calendar);\n    const cpm = computeCpm(\n      tasks.map((task) => {\n        const scheduled = schedule.byId.get(task.id);\n        return {\n          id: task.id,\n          name: task.name ?? task.id,\n          parentId: task.parentId,\n          durationDays: task.durationDays,\n          scheduledStart: scheduled?.scheduledStart as string,\n          scheduledEnd: scheduled?.scheduledEnd as string,\n        };\n      }),\n      dependencies,\n      calendar,\n      '2026-10-19',\n    );\n\n    expect(cpm.projectEndOrdinal).toBe(8);\n    expect(cpm.byId.get('A')?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(cpm.byId.get('C')?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(cpm.byId.get('D')?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(cpm.byId.get('B')?.critical).toBe(false);\n    expect(cpm.byId.get('B')?.totalFloat).toBe(3);\n    expect(cpm.criticalPaths.map((path) => path.join('-'))).toEqual(['A-C-D']);\n  });\n\n  it('menghitung ordinal hari kerja', () => {\n    expect(workingDayOrdinal('2026-10-19', '2026-10-19', calendar)).toBe(0);\n    expect(workingDayOrdinal('2026-10-19', '2026-10-23', calendar)).toBe(4);\n    expect(workingDayOrdinal('2026-10-19', '2026-10-26', calendar)).toBe(5);\n  });\n});\n"
}

  "path": "shared/src/pert.test.ts",
  "instructions": "PERT network tests",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { createCalendar } from './calendar.js';\nimport { computeCpm } from './cpm.js';\nimport { buildPertNetwork, pertEstimate } from './pert.js';\nimport { computeSchedule, type SchedulableTask } from './scheduler.js';\n\nconst calendar = createCalendar([0, 6]);\n\nconst tasks: SchedulableTask[] = [\n  { id: 'A', parentId: null, position: 0, durationDays: 3, startDate: '2026-10-19', name: 'A' },\n  { id: 'B', parentId: null, position: 1, durationDays: 2, startDate: '2026-10-19', name: 'B' },\n  { id: 'C', parentId: null, position: 2, durationDays: 5, startDate: '2026-10-19', name: 'C' },\n  { id: 'D', parentId: null, position: 3, durationDays: 1, startDate: '2026-10-19', name: 'D' },\n];\nconst dependencies = [\n  { predecessorId: 'A', successorId: 'B', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n  { predecessorId: 'A', successorId: 'C', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n  { predecessorId: 'B', successorId: 'D', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n  { predecessorId: 'C', successorId: 'D', type: 'FS' as const, lagDays: 0 },\n];\n\ndescribe('diagram PERT', () => {\n  it('menghitung kolom berdasarkan jalur terpanjang dan menandai jalur kritis', () => {\n    const schedule = computeSchedule(tasks, dependencies, calendar);\n    const cpm = computeCpm(\n      tasks.map((task) => {\n        const scheduled = schedule.byId.get(task.id);\n        return {\n          id: task.id,\n          name: task.name ?? task.id,\n          parentId: task.parentId,\n          durationDays: task.durationDays,\n          scheduledStart: scheduled?.scheduledStart as string,\n          scheduledEnd: scheduled?.scheduledEnd as string,\n        };\n      }),\n      dependencies,\n      calendar,\n      '2026-10-19',\n    );\n    const network = buildPertNetwork(tasks, dependencies, cpm);\n    const columnOf = (id: string): number =>\n      network.nodes.find((node) => node.id === id)?.column ?? -1;\n\n    expect(columnOf('A')).toBe(0);\n    expect(columnOf('B')).toBe(1);\n    expect(columnOf('C')).toBe(1);\n    expect(columnOf('D')).toBe(2);\n    expect(network.nodes.find((node) => node.id === 'C')?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(network.nodes.find((node) => node.id === 'B')?.critical).toBe(false);\n    expect(network.edges).toHaveLength(4);\n    expect(network.edges.filter((edge) => edge.critical)).toHaveLength(2);\n  });\n\n  it('menghitung estimasi te dan variansi', () => {\n    const estimate = pertEstimate(8, 10, 15);\n    expect(estimate.expectedDays).toBeCloseTo(10.5, 3);\n    expect(estimate.variance).toBeCloseTo(1.3611, 3);\n  });\n});\n"
}
```

  "content": "import path from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { config as loadEnv } from 'dotenv';\n\n// Memuat server/.env tanpa bergantung pada current working directory,\n// sehingga `npm run dev` dan `npm run start` sama-sama bekerja.\nconst currentDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nloadEnv({ path: path.resolve(currentDir, '../.env') });\n\nfunction readPort(value: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {\n  const parsed = Number(value);\n  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : fallback;\n}\n\nconst jwtSecret = process.env.JWT_SECRET?.trim() ?? '';\nif (jwtSecret.length === 0) {\n  throw new Error(\n    'JWT_SECRET belum diatur. Salin server/.env.example menjadi server/.env lalu isi JWT_SECRET dengan string acak.',\n  );\n}\n\nexport const config = {\n  port: readPort(process.env.PORT, 3001),\n  jwtSecret,\n  databaseUrl: process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? '',\n  nodeEnv: process.env.NODE_ENV ?? 'development',\n  isTest: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test',\n} as const;\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/prisma/schema.prisma",
  "instructions": "Prisma schema for the Gantt app",
  "content": "// Skema database aplikasi manajemen proyek Gantt Chart.\n// Tanggal disimpan sebagai string ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` agar tidak terpengaruh zona waktu.\n\ngenerator client {\n  provider = \"prisma-client-js\"\n}\n\ndatasource db {\n  provider = \"sqlite\"\n  url      = env(\"DATABASE_URL\")\n}\n\nmodel User {\n  id           String    @id @default(cuid())\n  name         String\n  email        String    @unique\n  passwordHash String\n  createdAt    DateTime  @default(now())\n  projects     Project[]\n}\n\nmodel Project {\n  id          String    @id @default(cuid())\n  ownerId     String\n  name        String\n  description String?\n  startDate   String\n  weekendDays String    @default(\"0,6\")\n  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())\n  owner       User      @relation(fields: [ownerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  holidays    Holiday[]\n  tasks       Task[]\n  resources   Resource[]\n\n  @@index([ownerId])\n}\n\nmodel Holiday {\n  id        String  @id @default(cuid())\n  projectId String\n  date      String\n  name      String\n  project   Project @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  @@unique([projectId, date])\n  @@index([projectId])\n}\n\nmodel Task {\n  id              String       @id @default(cuid())\n  projectId       String\n  parentId        String?\n  position        Int          @default(0)\n  name            String\n  description     String?\n  durationDays    Int\n  startDate       String\n  scheduledStart  String\n  scheduledEnd    String\n  priority        String       @default(\"MEDIUM\")\n  color           String       @default(\"#2563eb\")\n  progress        Int          @default(0)\n  optimisticDays  Int?\n  pessimisticDays Int?\n  project         Project      @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  parent          Task?        @relation(\"TaskHierarchy\", fields: [parentId], references: [id], onDelete: NoAction, onUpdate: NoAction)\n  children        Task[]       @relation(\"TaskHierarchy\")\n  predecessors    Dependency[] @relation(\"DependencyPredecessor\")\n  successors      Dependency[] @relation(\"DependencySuccessor\")\n  assignments     Assignment[]\n\n  @@index([projectId])\n  @@index([parentId])\n}\n\nmodel Dependency {\n  id            String @id @default(cuid())\n  predecessorId String\n  successorId   String\n  type          String @default(\"FS\")\n  lagDays       Int    @default(0)\n\n  predecessor Task @relation(\"DependencyPredecessor\", fields: [predecessorId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  successor   Task @relation(\"DependencySuccessor\", fields: [successorId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  @@unique([predecessorId, successorId])\n  @@index([successorId])\n}\n\nmodel Resource {\n  id        String       @id @default(cuid())\n  projectId String\n  name      String\n  email     String\n  role      String\n  salary    Int\n  project   Project      @relation(fields: [projectId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  assignments Assignment[]\n\n  @@index([projectId])\n}\n\nmodel Assignment {\n  id                String   @id @default(cuid())\n  taskId            String\n  resourceId        String\n  allocationPercent Int      @default(100)\n  task              Task     @relation(fields: [taskId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  resource          Resource @relation(fields: [resourceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  @@unique([taskId, resourceId])\n  @@index([taskId])\n  @@index([resourceId])\n}\n"
}
```

> ```
> Note with express 4, throwing inside a sync middleware is caught by express ✓ (sync throws are handled). For async handlers I need asyncHandler wrapper.

> zod error details: r.error.flatten() → { fieldErrors, formErrors }. I'll use `r.error.issues.map(i => ({ path: i.path.join('.'), message: i.message }))`. details type: Record<string, unknown>. I'll set details = { issues: [...] }.

{
  "path": "server/src/middleware/error.ts",
  "instructions": "HTTP error helpers, async wrapper, 404 and error handlers",
  "content": "import type { NextFunction, Request, RequestHandler, Response } from 'express';\nimport { ZodError } from 'zod';\n\n/** Kesalahan HTTP dengan kode dan pesan bahasa Indonesia. */\nexport class HttpError extends Error {\n  readonly status: number;\n  readonly code: string;\n  readonly details: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n\n  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = 'HttpError';\n    this.status = status;\n    this.code = code;\n    this.details = details;\n  }\n}\n\nexport const badRequest = (message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>): HttpError =>\n  new HttpError(400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message, details);\n\nexport const unprocessable = (code: string, message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>): HttpError =>\n  new HttpError(422, code, message, details);\n\nexport const unauthorized = (message = 'Silakan masuk terlebih dahulu.'): HttpError =>\n  new HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', message);\n\nexport const notFound = (message = 'Data tidak ditemukan.'): HttpError =>\n  new HttpError(404, 'NOT_FOUND', message);\n\nexport const conflict = (code: string, message: string): HttpError =>\n  new HttpError(409, code, message);\n\nexport function zodDetails(error: ZodError): Record<string, unknown> {\n  return {\n    issues: error.issues.map((issue) => ({\n      path: issue.path.join('.'),\n      message: issue.message,\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\n/** Membungkus handler async agar error-nya diteruskan ke error handler Express. */\nexport function asyncHandler(\n  handler: (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => Promise<unknown>,\n): RequestHandler {\n  return (req, res, next) => {\n    handler(req, res, next).catch(next);\n  };\n}\n\nexport function notFoundHandler(req: Request, res: Response): void {\n  res.status(404).json({\n    error: {\n      code: 'NOT_FOUND',\n      message: `Rute ${req.method} ${req.originalUrl} tidak ditemukan.`,\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars\nexport function errorHandler(\n  error: unknown,\n  _req: Request,\n  res: Response,\n  _next: NextFunction,\n): void {\n  if (error instanceof HttpError) {\n    res.status(error.status).json({\n      error: { code: error.code, message: error.message, details: error.details ?? {} },\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (error instanceof ZodError) {\n    res.status(400).json({\n      error: {\n        code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',\n        message: 'Data yang dikirim tidak valid.',\n        details: zodDetails(error),\n      },\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Terjadi kesalahan tidak terduga.';\n  console.error('[error]', error);\n  res.status(500).json({\n    error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: `Terjadi kesalahan pada server: ${message}` },\n  });\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/middleware/validate.ts",
  "instructions": "zod body validation middleware",
  "content": "import type { RequestHandler } from 'express';\nimport type { ZodTypeAny } from 'zod';\nimport { badRequest, zodDetails } from './error.js';\n\n/** Memvalidasi body request dengan skema zod dan menggantinya dengan hasil parse. */\nexport function validateBody(schema: ZodTypeAny): RequestHandler {\n  return (req, _res, next) => {\n    const result = schema.safeParse(req.body);\n    if (!result.success) {\n      next(badRequest('Data yang dikirim tidak valid.', zodDetails(result.error)));\n      return;\n    }\n    req.body = result.data;\n    next();\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/middleware/auth.ts",
  "instructions": "JWT auth middleware",
  "content": "import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';\nimport jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';\nimport { config } from '../config.js';\nimport { unauthorized } from './error.js';\n\nexport interface AuthenticatedRequest extends Request {\n  userId: string;\n}\n\nconst TOKEN_TTL = '7d';\n\nexport function signToken(userId: string): string {\n  return jwt.sign({ sub: userId }, config.jwtSecret, { expiresIn: TOKEN_TTL });\n}\n\nfunction readBearerToken(req: Request): string | null {\n  const header = req.headers.authorization;\n  if (!header || !header.startsWith('Bearer ')) return null;\n  const token = header.slice('Bearer '.length).trim();\n  return token.length > 0 ? token : null;\n}\n\n/** Semua rute selain register, login, dan health memakai middleware ini. */\nexport function requireAuth(req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {\n  const token = readBearerToken(req);\n  if (!token) {\n    next(unauthorized('Header Authorization Bearer token wajib diisi.'));\n    return;\n  }\n  try {\n    const payload = jwt.verify(token, config.jwtSecret);\n    if (typeof payload === 'string' || typeof payload.sub !== 'string') {\n      next(unauthorized('Token tidak valid.'));\n      return;\n    }\n    (req as AuthenticatedRequest).userId = payload.sub;\n    next();\n  } catch {\n    next(unauthorized('Token tidak valid atau sudah kedaluwarsa.'));\n  }\n}\n\n/** Mengambil userId dari request yang sudah melewati requireAuth. */\nexport function currentUserId(req: Request): string {\n  const userId = (req as AuthenticatedRequest).userId;\n  if (!userId) throw unauthorized();\n  return userId;\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "path": "server/src/services/snapshot.ts",
  "instructions": "Serializers and full project snapshot builder",
  "content": "import type {\n  Assignment as PrismaAssignment,\n  Dependency as PrismaDependency,\n  Holiday as PrismaHoliday,\n  Project as PrismaProject,\n  Resource as PrismaResource,\n  Task as PrismaTask,\n  User as PrismaUser,\n} from '@prisma/client';\nimport type {\n  AssignmentDto,\n  DependencyDto,\n  HolidayDto,\n  Priority,\n  ProjectDto,\n  ProjectSnapshot,\n  ResourceDto,\n  TaskDto,\n  UserDto,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { notFound } from '../middleware/error.js';\n\nconst PRIORITIES: Priority[] = ['LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH'];\n\nexport function toPriority(value: string): Priority {\n  return PRIORITIES.includes(value as Priority) ? (value as Priority) : 'MEDIUM';\n}\n\nexport function toUserDto(user: PrismaUser): UserDto {\n  return {\n    id: user.id,\n    name: user.name,\n    email: user.email,\n    createdAt: user.createdAt.toISOString(),\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toProjectDto(project: PrismaProject): ProjectDto {\n  return {\n    id: project.id,\n    ownerId: project.ownerId,\n    name: project.name,\n    description: project.description,\n    startDate: project.startDate,\n    weekendDays: project.weekendDays,\n    createdAt: project.createdAt.toISOString(),\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toTaskDto(task: PrismaTask): TaskDto {\n  return {\n    id: task.id,\n    projectId: task.projectId,\n    parentId: task.parentId,\n    position: task.position,\n    name: task.name,\n    description: task.description,\n    durationDays: task.durationDays,\n    startDate: task.startDate,\n    scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n    scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n    priority: toPriority(task.priority),\n    color: task.color,\n    progress: task.progress,\n    optimisticDays: task.optimisticDays,\n    pessimisticDays: task.pessimisticDays,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toDependencyDto(dependency: PrismaDependency): DependencyDto {\n  return {\n    id: dependency.id,\n    predecessorId: dependency.predecessorId,\n    successorId: dependency.successorId,\n    type: dependency.type === 'SS' ? 'SS' : 'FS',\n    lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toResourceDto(resource: PrismaResource): ResourceDto {\n  return {\n    id: resource.id,\n    projectId: resource.projectId,\n    name: resource.name,\n    email: resource.email,\n    role: resource.role,\n    salary: resource.salary,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toAssignmentDto(assignment: PrismaAssignment): AssignmentDto {\n  return {\n    id: assignment.id,\n    taskId: assignment.taskId,\n    resourceId: assignment.resourceId,\n    allocationPercent: assignment.allocationPercent,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function toHolidayDto(holiday: PrismaHoliday): HolidayDto {\n  return {\n    id: holiday.id,\n    projectId: holiday.projectId,\n    date: holiday.date,\n    name: holiday.name,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Snapshot lengkap satu proyek beserta seluruh datanya. */\nexport async function buildSnapshot(projectId: string): Promise<ProjectSnapshot> {\n  const project = await prisma.project.findUnique({ where: { id: projectId } });\n  if (!project) throw notFound('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');\n\n  const [tasks, dependencies, resources, assignments, holidays] = await Promise.all([\n    prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId }, orderBy: [{ position: 'asc' }] }),\n    prisma.dependency.findMany({\n      where: { predecessor: { projectId } },\n      orderBy: [{ id: 'asc' }],\n    }),\n    prisma.resource.findMany({ where: { projectId }, orderBy: [{ name: 'asc' }] }),\n    prisma.assignment.findMany({ where: { task: { projectId } }, orderBy: [{ id: 'asc' }] }),\n    prisma.holiday.findMany({ where: { projectId }, orderBy: [{ date: 'asc' }] }),\n  ]);\n\n  return {\n    project: toProjectDto(project),\n    tasks: tasks.map(toTaskDto),\n    dependencies: dependencies.map(toDependencyDto),\n    resources: resources.map(toResourceDto),\n    assignments: assignments.map(toAssignmentDto),\n    holidays: holidays.map(toHolidayDto),\n  };\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "path": "server/src/middleware/access.ts",
  "instructions": "Ownership checks shared by the routes",
  "content": "import type {\n  Assignment,\n  Dependency,\n  Holiday,\n  Project,\n  Resource,\n  Task,\n} from '@prisma/client';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { notFound } from './error.js';\n\n/**\n * Setiap data dicek kepemilikannya: hanya pemilik proyek yang boleh mengakses.\n * Data milik pengguna lain dibalas 404 agar keberadaannya tidak terbocor.\n */\nexport async function requireOwnedProject(projectId: string, userId: string): Promise<Project> {\n  const project = await prisma.project.findFirst({ where: { id: projectId, ownerId: userId } });\n  if (!project) throw notFound('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');\n  return project;\n}\n\nexport async function requireOwnedTask(taskId: string, userId: string): Promise<Task> {\n  const task = await prisma.task.findFirst({\n    where: { id: taskId, project: { ownerId: userId } },\n  });\n  if (!task) throw notFound('Tugas tidak ditemukan.');\n  return task;\n}\n\nexport async function requireOwnedDependency(\n  dependencyId: string,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<Dependency> {\n  const dependency = await prisma.dependency.findFirst({\n    where: { id: dependencyId, predecessor: { project: { ownerId: userId } } },\n  });\n  if (!dependency) throw notFound('Dependensi tidak ditemukan.');\n  return dependency;\n}\n\nexport async function requireOwnedResource(\n  resourceId: string,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<Resource> {\n  const resource = await prisma.resource.findFirst({\n    where: { id: resourceId, project: { ownerId: userId } },\n  });\n  if (!resource) throw notFound('Anggota tim tidak ditemukan.');\n  return resource;\n}\n\nexport async function requireOwnedAssignment(\n  assignmentId: string,\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<Assignment> {\n  const assignment = await prisma.assignment.findFirst({\n    where: { id: assignmentId, task: { project: { ownerId: userId } } },\n  });\n  if (!assignment) throw notFound('Alokasi tidak ditemukan.');\n  return assignment;\n}\n\nexport async function requireOwnedHoliday(holidayId: string, userId: string): Promise<Holiday> {\n  const holiday = await prisma.holiday.findFirst({\n    where: { id: holidayId, project: { ownerId: userId } },\n  });\n  if (!holiday) throw notFound('Hari libur tidak ditemukan.');\n  return holiday;\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "path": "server/src/services/recompute.ts",
  "instructions": "Schedule recomputation service shared by all schedule-changing routes",
  "content": "import type { TaskDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { computeSchedule, createCalendar } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { toTaskDto } from './snapshot.js';\n\n/**\n * Menghitung ulang jadwal seluruh tugas proyek lalu menyimpannya.\n * Dipakai setiap kali tugas, dependensi, durasi, atau kalender berubah.\n */\nexport async function recomputeProject(projectId: string): Promise<TaskDto[]> {\n  const project = await prisma.project.findUnique({\n    where: { id: projectId },\n    include: { tasks: true, holidays: true, dependencies: true },\n  });\n  if (!project) return [];\n\n  const calendar = createCalendar(\n    project.weekendDays,\n    project.holidays.map((holiday) => holiday.date),\n  );\n\n  const schedule = computeSchedule(\n    project.tasks.map((task) => ({\n      id: task.id,\n      parentId: task.parentId,\n      position: task.position,\n      durationDays: task.durationDays,\n      startDate: task.startDate,\n      progress: task.progress,\n    })),\n    project.dependencies.map((dependency) => ({\n      predecessorId: dependency.predecessorId,\n      successorId: dependency.successorId,\n      type: dependency.type === 'SS' ? ('SS' as const) : ('FS' as const),\n      lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n    })),\n    calendar,\n  );\n\n  const updates = schedule.tasks\n    .filter((task) => {\n      const stored = project.tasks.find((item) => item.id === task.id);\n      if (!stored) return false;\n      return (\n        stored.scheduledStart !== task.scheduledStart ||\n        stored.scheduledEnd !== task.scheduledEnd ||\n        stored.durationDays !== task.durationDays ||\n        stored.progress !== task.progress\n      );\n    })\n    .map((task) =>\n      prisma.task.update({\n        where: { id: task.id },\n        data: {\n          scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n          scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n          durationDays: task.durationDays,\n          progress: task.progress,\n        },\n      }),\n    );\n\n  if (updates.length > 0) {\n    await prisma.$transaction(updates);\n  }\n\n  return listProjectTasks(projectId);\n}\n\n/** Daftar tugas proyek yang sudah dalam bentuk DTO. */\nexport async function listProjectTasks(projectId: string): Promise<TaskDto[]> {\n  const tasks = await prisma.task.findMany({\n    where: { projectId },\n    orderBy: [{ position: 'asc' }],\n  });\n  return tasks.map(toTaskDto);\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/routes/auth.ts",
  "instructions": "Authentication routes: register, login, me",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';\nimport rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';\nimport { loginSchema, registerSchema, type LoginInput, type RegisterInput } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { asyncHandler, conflict, unauthorized } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth, signToken } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { toUserDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nconst BCRYPT_ROUNDS = 10;\n\nconst authLimiter = rateLimit({\n  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,\n  limit: 100,\n  standardHeaders: true,\n  legacyHeaders: false,\n  message: {\n    error: {\n      code: 'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS',\n      message: 'Terlalu banyak percobaan. Silakan coba lagi beberapa saat lagi.',\n    },\n  },\n});\n\nexport const authRouter = Router();\n\nauthRouter.post(\n  '/register',\n  authLimiter,\n  validateBody(registerSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const input = req.body as RegisterInput;\n    const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: input.email } });\n    if (existing) {\n      throw conflict('EMAIL_TAKEN', 'Email sudah terdaftar. Silakan masuk atau pakai email lain.');\n    }\n    const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(input.password, BCRYPT_ROUNDS);\n    const user = await prisma.user.create({\n      data: { name: input.name, email: input.email, passwordHash },\n    });\n    res.status(201).json({ token: signToken(user.id), user: toUserDto(user) });\n  }),\n);\n\nauthRouter.post(\n  '/login',\n  authLimiter,\n  validateBody(loginSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const input = req.body as LoginInput;\n    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: input.email } });\n    if (!user) {\n      throw unauthorized('Email atau kata sandi salah.');\n    }\n    const matches = await bcrypt.compare(input.password, user.passwordHash);\n    if (!matches) {\n      throw unauthorized('Email atau kata sandi salah.');\n    }\n    res.json({ token: signToken(user.id), user: toUserDto(user) });\n  }),\n);\n\nauthRouter.get(\n  '/me',\n  requireAuth,\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: currentUserId(req) } });\n    if (!user) throw unauthorized('Sesi tidak valid.');\n    res.json({ user: toUserDto(user) });\n  }),\n);\n"
}

  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  DEFAULT_WEEKEND_DAYS,\n  projectCreateSchema,\n  projectUpdateSchema,\n  weekendDaysToString,\n  type ProjectCreateInput,\n  type ProjectUpdateInput,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedProject } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { buildSnapshot, toProjectDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nexport const projectRouter = Router();\n\nprojectRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nprojectRouter.get(\n  '/projects',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const userId = currentUserId(req);\n    const projects = await prisma.project.findMany({\n      where: { ownerId: userId },\n      orderBy: [{ createdAt: 'desc' }],\n    });\n    res.json({ projects: projects.map(toProjectDto) });\n  }),\n);\n\nprojectRouter.post(\n  '/projects',\n  validateBody(projectCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const userId = currentUserId(req);\n    const input = req.body as ProjectCreateInput;\n    const project = await prisma.project.create({\n      data: {\n        ownerId: userId,\n        name: input.name,\n        description: input.description ?? null,\n        startDate: input.startDate,\n        weekendDays: weekendDaysToString(input.weekendDays ?? DEFAULT_WEEKEND_DAYS),\n      },\n    });\n    res.status(201).json({ project: toProjectDto(project) });\n  }),\n);\n\nprojectRouter.get(\n  '/projects/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    res.json(await buildSnapshot(project.id));\n  }),\n);\n\nprojectRouter.patch(\n  '/projects/:id',\n  validateBody(projectUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as ProjectUpdateInput;\n    const updated = await prisma.project.update({\n      where: { id: project.id },\n      data: {\n        ...(input.name !== undefined ? { name: input.name } : {}),\n        ...(input.description !== undefined ? { description: input.description ?? null } : {}),\n        ...(input.startDate !== undefined ? { startDate: input.startDate } : {}),\n      },\n    });\n    res.json({ project: toProjectDto(updated) });\n  }),\n);\n\nprojectRouter.delete(\n  '/projects/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    await prisma.project.delete({ where: { id: project.id } });\n    res.status(204).send();\n  }),\n);\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/routes/tasks.ts",
  "instructions": "Task CRUD, delete subtree, and move routes",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH,\n  getSubtreeExtraDepth,\n  getSubtreeIds,\n  getTaskDepth,\n  taskCreateSchema,\n  taskMoveSchema,\n  taskUpdateSchema,\n  type TaskCreateInput,\n  type TaskMoveInput,\n  type TaskUpdateInput,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedProject, requireOwnedTask } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler, badRequest, conflict, notFound, unprocessable } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { listProjectTasks, recomputeProject } from '../services/recompute.js';\n\nexport const taskRouter = Router();\n\ntaskRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nconst PARENT_READ_ONLY_FIELDS = [\n  'durationDays',\n  'startDate',\n  'progress',\n  'optimisticDays',\n  'pessimisticDays',\n] as const;\n\nasync function assertParentCanHostChildren(taskIds: string[]): Promise<void> {\n  const [dependencyCount, assignmentCount] = await Promise.all([\n    prisma.dependency.count({\n      where: { OR: [{ predecessorId: { in: taskIds } }, { successorId: { in: taskIds } }] },\n    }),\n    prisma.assignment.count({ where: { taskId: { in: taskIds } } }),\n  ]);\n  if (dependencyCount > 0 || assignmentCount > 0) {\n    throw conflict(\n      'PARENT_HAS_RELATIONS',\n      'Tugas yang sudah punya dependensi atau alokasi tidak boleh dijadikan tugas induk.',\n    );\n  }\n}\n\ntaskRouter.post(\n  '/projects/:id/tasks',\n  validateBody(taskCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const userId = currentUserId(req);\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, userId);\n    const input = req.body as TaskCreateInput;\n    const tasks = await prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: project.id } });\n\n    const parentId = input.parentId ?? null;\n    if (parentId) {\n      const parent = tasks.find((task) => task.id === parentId);\n      if (!parent) throw notFound('Tugas induk tidak ditemukan.');\n      if (getTaskDepth(tasks, parentId) + 1 > MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH) {\n        throw unprocessable(\n          'MAX_DEPTH_EXCEEDED',\n          `Kedalaman sub-tugas maksimal ${MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH} level.`,\n        );\n      }\n      await assertParentCanHostChildren([parentId]);\n    }\n\n    const siblings = tasks.filter((task) => task.parentId === parentId);\n    const position =\n      input.position ??\n      (siblings.length > 0 ? Math.max(...siblings.map((task) => task.position)) + 1 : 0);\n\n    const created = await prisma.task.create({\n      data: {\n        projectId: project.id,\n        parentId,\n        position,\n        name: input.name,\n        description: input.description ?? null,\n        durationDays: input.durationDays,\n        startDate: input.startDate,\n        scheduledStart: input.startDate,\n        scheduledEnd: input.startDate,\n        priority: input.priority ?? 'MEDIUM',\n        color: input.color ?? '#2563eb',\n        progress: input.progress ?? 0,\n        optimisticDays: input.optimisticDays ?? null,\n        pessimisticDays: input.pessimisticDays ?? null,\n      },\n    });\n\n    const updated = await recomputeProject(project.id);\n    res.status(201).json({ tasks: updated, task: created.id });\n  }),\n);\n\ntaskRouter.patch(\n  '/tasks/:id',\n  validateBody(taskUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const task = await requireOwnedTask(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as TaskUpdateInput;\n\n    const childrenCount = await prisma.task.count({ where: { parentId: task.id } });\n    if (childrenCount > 0) {\n      const attempted = PARENT_READ_ONLY_FIELDS.filter((field) => input[field] !== undefined);\n      if (attempted.length > 0) {\n        throw conflict(\n          'PARENT_TASK_READ_ONLY',\n          'Tugas induk hanya boleh diubah nama, deskripsi, warna, dan prioritasnya.',\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    const optimisticDays =\n      input.optimisticDays !== undefined ? input.optimisticDays : task.optimisticDays;\n    const pessimisticDays =\n      input.pessimisticDays !== undefined ? input.pessimisticDays : task.pessimisticDays;\n    if (\n      typeof optimisticDays === 'number' &&\n      typeof pessimisticDays === 'number' &&\n      pessimisticDays < optimisticDays\n    ) {\n      throw badRequest('Estimasi pesimistis tidak boleh lebih kecil dari optimistis.', {\n        issues: [{ path: 'pessimisticDays', message: 'Nilai terlalu kecil.' }],\n      });\n    }\n\n    await prisma.task.update({\n      where: { id: task.id },\n      data: {\n        ...(input.name !== undefined ? { name: input.name } : {}),\n        ...(input.description !== undefined ? { description: input.description ?? null } : {}),\n        ...(input.durationDays !== undefined ? { durationDays: input.durationDays } : {}),\n        ...(input.startDate !== undefined ? { startDate: input.startDate } : {}),\n        ...(input.priority !== undefined ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),\n        ...(input.color !== undefined ? { color: input.color } : {}),\n        ...(input.progress !== undefined ? { progress: input.progress } : {}),\n        ...(input.optimisticDays !== undefined ? { optimisticDays: input.optimisticDays ?? null } : {}),\n        ...(input.pessimisticDays !== undefined\n          ? { pessimisticDays: input.pessimisticDays ?? null }\n          : {}),\n        ...(input.position !== undefined ? { position: input.position } : {}),\n      },\n    });\n\n    res.json({ tasks: await recomputeProject(task.projectId) });\n  }),\n);\n\ntaskRouter.delete(\n  '/tasks/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const task = await requireOwnedTask(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const all = await prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: task.projectId } });\n    const subtree = getSubtreeIds(all, task.id);\n    const ordered = subtree\n      .map((id) => ({ id, depth: getTaskDepth(all, id) }))\n      .sort((a, b) => b.depth - a.depth);\n\n    await prisma.$transaction(ordered.map((item) => prisma.task.delete({ where: { id: item.id } })));\n    res.json({ tasks: await recomputeProject(task.projectId) });\n  }),\n);\n\ntaskRouter.post(\n  '/tasks/:id/move',\n  validateBody(taskMoveSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const task = await requireOwnedTask(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as TaskMoveInput;\n    const all = await prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: task.projectId } });\n    const newParentId = input.parentId ?? null;\n\n    if (newParentId === task.id) {\n      throw unprocessable('INVALID_PARENT', 'Tugas tidak bisa dijadikan induk bagi dirinya sendiri.');\n    }\n\n    if (newParentId) {\n      const newParent = all.find((item) => item.id === newParentId);\n      if (!newParent) throw notFound('Tugas induk tidak ditemukan.');\n      const subtreeIds = getSubtreeIds(all, task.id);\n      if (subtreeIds.includes(newParentId)) {\n        throw unprocessable(\n          'INVALID_PARENT',\n          'Tugas tidak bisa dipindah ke dalam sub-tugasnya sendiri.',\n        );\n      }\n      const depth = getTaskDepth(all, newParentId) + 1 + getSubtreeExtraDepth(all, task.id);\n      if (depth > MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH) {\n        throw unprocessable(\n          'MAX_DEPTH_EXCEEDED',\n          `Kedalaman sub-tugas maksimal ${MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH} level.`,\n        );\n      }\n      await assertParentCanHostChildren([newParentId]);\n    }\n\n    const siblings = all\n      .filter((item) => item.parentId === newParentId && item.id !== task.id)\n      .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);\n    const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(input.position, siblings.length));\n    const orderedIds = [\n      ...siblings.slice(0, index).map((item) => item.id),\n      task.id,\n      ...siblings.slice(index).map((item) => item.id),\n    ];\n\n    await prisma.$transaction(\n      orderedIds.map((id, position) =>\n        prisma.task.update({\n          where: { id },\n          data: id === task.id ? { parentId: newParentId, position } : { position },\n        }),\n      ),\n    );\n\n    res.json({ tasks: await recomputeProject(task.projectId) });\n  }),\n);\n\nexport { listProjectTasks };\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/routes/dependencies.ts",
  "instructions": "Dependency create, update and delete routes with cycle detection",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  detectCycle,\n  getParentIds,\n  dependencyCreateSchema,\n  dependencyUpdateSchema,\n  type DependencyCreateInput,\n  type DependencyUpdateInput,\n  type DependencyType,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedDependency, requireOwnedTask } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler, notFound, unprocessable } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { recomputeProject } from '../services/recompute.js';\nimport { toDependencyDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nexport const dependencyRouter = Router();\n\ndependencyRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nfunction normalizedType(value: string): DependencyType {\n  return value === 'SS' ? 'SS' : 'FS';\n}\n\nasync function loadProjectDependencies(projectId: string) {\n  return prisma.dependency.findMany({ where: { predecessor: { projectId } } });\n}\n\ndependencyRouter.post(\n  '/tasks/:id/dependencies',\n  validateBody(dependencyCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const successor = await requireOwnedTask(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as DependencyCreateInput;\n\n    if (input.predecessorId === successor.id) {\n      throw unprocessable(\n        'SELF_DEPENDENCY',\n        'Sebuah tugas tidak boleh bergantung pada dirinya sendiri.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    const predecessor = await prisma.task.findFirst({\n      where: { id: input.predecessorId, projectId: successor.projectId },\n    });\n    if (!predecessor) throw notFound('Tugas predecessor tidak ditemukan.');\n\n    const projectTasks = await prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: successor.projectId } });\n    const parentIds = getParentIds(projectTasks);\n    if (parentIds.has(successor.id) || parentIds.has(predecessor.id)) {\n      throw unprocessable(\n        'DEPENDENCY_ON_PARENT_TASK',\n        'Dependensi hanya boleh ditambahkan pada tugas daun, bukan tugas induk.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    const duplicate = await prisma.dependency.findFirst({\n      where: { predecessorId: input.predecessorId, successorId: successor.id },\n    });\n    if (duplicate) {\n      throw unprocessable('DUPLICATE_DEPENDENCY', 'Dependensi pasangan tugas ini sudah ada.');\n    }\n\n    const existing = await loadProjectDependencies(successor.projectId);\n    const candidate = {\n      predecessorId: input.predecessorId,\n      successorId: successor.id,\n      type: normalizedType(input.type ?? 'FS'),\n      lagDays: input.lagDays ?? 0,\n    };\n    const cycle = detectCycle([\n      ...existing.map((dependency) => ({\n        predecessorId: dependency.predecessorId,\n        successorId: dependency.successorId,\n        type: normalizedType(dependency.type),\n        lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n      })),\n      candidate,\n    ]);\n    if (cycle) {\n      throw unprocessable(\n        'CYCLE_DETECTED',\n        `Dependensi ini akan membentuk siklus (${cycle.join(' → ')}) sehingga ditolak.`,\n        { cycle },\n      );\n    }\n\n    const dependency = await prisma.dependency.create({ data: candidate });\n    res.status(201).json({\n      tasks: await recomputeProject(successor.projectId),\n      dependency: toDependencyDto(dependency),\n    });\n  }),\n);\n\ndependencyRouter.patch(\n  '/dependencies/:id',\n  validateBody(dependencyUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const dependency = await requireOwnedDependency(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as DependencyUpdateInput;\n    const updated = await prisma.dependency.update({\n      where: { id: dependency.id },\n      data: {\n        ...(input.type !== undefined ? { type: input.type } : {}),\n        ...(input.lagDays !== undefined ? { lagDays: input.lagDays } : {}),\n      },\n    });\n    const predecessor = await prisma.task.findUniqueOrThrow({\n      where: { id: dependency.predecessorId },\n    });\n    res.json({\n      tasks: await recomputeProject(predecessor.projectId),\n      dependency: toDependencyDto(updated),\n    });\n  }),\n);\n\ndependencyRouter.delete(\n  '/dependencies/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const dependency = await requireOwnedDependency(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const predecessor = await prisma.task.findUniqueOrThrow({\n      where: { id: dependency.predecessorId },\n    });\n    await prisma.dependency.delete({ where: { id: dependency.id } });\n    res.json({ tasks: await recomputeProject(predecessor.projectId) });\n  }),\n);\n"
}

  "path": "server/src/routes/resources.ts",
  "instructions": "Team resource routes",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  resourceCreateSchema,\n  resourceUpdateSchema,\n  type ResourceCreateInput,\n  type ResourceUpdateInput,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedProject, requireOwnedResource } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { toResourceDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nexport const resourceRouter = Router();\n\nresourceRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nresourceRouter.get(\n  '/projects/:id/resources',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const resources = await prisma.resource.findMany({\n      where: { projectId: project.id },\n      orderBy: [{ name: 'asc' }],\n    });\n    res.json({ resources: resources.map(toResourceDto) });\n  }),\n);\n\nresourceRouter.post(\n  '/projects/:id/resources',\n  validateBody(resourceCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as ResourceCreateInput;\n    const resource = await prisma.resource.create({\n      data: {\n        projectId: project.id,\n        name: input.name,\n        email: input.email,\n        role: input.role,\n        salary: input.salary,\n      },\n    });\n    res.status(201).json({ resource: toResourceDto(resource) });\n  }),\n);\n\nresourceRouter.patch(\n  '/resources/:id',\n  validateBody(resourceUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const resource = await requireOwnedResource(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as ResourceUpdateInput;\n    const updated = await prisma.resource.update({\n      where: { id: resource.id },\n      data: {\n        ...(input.name !== undefined ? { name: input.name } : {}),\n        ...(input.email !== undefined ? { email: input.email } : {}),\n        ...(input.role !== undefined ? { role: input.role } : {}),\n        ...(input.salary !== undefined ? { salary: input.salary } : {}),\n      },\n    });\n    res.json({ resource: toResourceDto(updated) });\n  }),\n);\n\nresourceRouter.delete(\n  '/resources/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const resource = await requireOwnedResource(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    await prisma.resource.delete({ where: { id: resource.id } });\n    res.status(204).send();\n  }),\n);\n"
}

{
  "path": "server/src/routes/assignments.ts",
  "instructions": "Task assignment routes",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  assignmentCreateSchema,\n  assignmentUpdateSchema,\n  getParentIds,\n  type AssignmentCreateInput,\n  type AssignmentUpdateInput,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedAssignment, requireOwnedTask } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler, conflict, notFound, unprocessable } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { toAssignmentDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nexport const assignmentRouter = Router();\n\nassignmentRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nassignmentRouter.post(\n  '/tasks/:id/assignments',\n  validateBody(assignmentCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const task = await requireOwnedTask(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as AssignmentCreateInput;\n\n    const projectTasks = await prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: task.projectId } });\n    if (getParentIds(projectTasks).has(task.id)) {\n      throw unprocessable(\n        'ASSIGNMENT_ON_PARENT_TASK',\n        'Alokasi anggota hanya boleh diberikan pada tugas daun, bukan tugas induk.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    const resource = await prisma.resource.findFirst({\n      where: { id: input.resourceId, projectId: task.projectId },\n    });\n    if (!resource) throw notFound('Anggota tim tidak ditemukan pada proyek ini.');\n\n    const duplicate = await prisma.assignment.findFirst({\n      where: { taskId: task.id, resourceId: resource.id },\n    });\n    if (duplicate) {\n      throw conflict(\n        'ASSIGNMENT_EXISTS',\n        'Anggota ini sudah dialokasikan pada tugas tersebut. Silakan ubah persentase alokasinya.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    const assignment = await prisma.assignment.create({\n      data: {\n        taskId: task.id,\n        resourceId: resource.id,\n        allocationPercent: input.allocationPercent ?? 100,\n      },\n    });\n    res.status(201).json({ assignment: toAssignmentDto(assignment) });\n  }),\n);\n\nassignmentRouter.patch(\n  '/assignments/:id',\n  validateBody(assignmentUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const assignment = await requireOwnedAssignment(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as AssignmentUpdateInput;\n    const updated = await prisma.assignment.update({\n      where: { id: assignment.id },\n      data: { allocationPercent: input.allocationPercent },\n    });\n    res.json({ assignment: toAssignmentDto(updated) });\n  }),\n);\n\nassignmentRouter.delete(\n  '/assignments/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const assignment = await requireOwnedAssignment(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    await prisma.assignment.delete({ where: { id: assignment.id } });\n    res.status(204).send();\n  }),\n);\n"
}

'@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedHoliday, requireOwnedProject } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler, conflict } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\nimport { validateBody } from '../middleware/validate.js';\nimport { recomputeProject } from '../services/recompute.js';\nimport { toHolidayDto, toProjectDto } from '../services/snapshot.js';\n\nexport const calendarRouter = Router();\n\ncalendarRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\ncalendarRouter.put(\n  '/projects/:id/calendar',\n  validateBody(calendarUpdateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as CalendarUpdateInput;\n    const updated = await prisma.project.update({\n      where: { id: project.id },\n      data: { weekendDays: weekendDaysToString(input.weekendDays) },\n    });\n    res.json({\n      project: toProjectDto(updated),\n      tasks: await recomputeProject(project.id),\n    });\n  }),\n);\n\ncalendarRouter.post(\n  '/projects/:id/holidays',\n  validateBody(holidayCreateSchema),\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const input = req.body as HolidayCreateInput;\n    const duplicate = await prisma.holiday.findFirst({\n      where: { projectId: project.id, date: input.date },\n    });\n    if (duplicate) {\n      throw conflict('HOLIDAY_EXISTS', 'Hari libur pada tanggal tersebut sudah ada.');\n    }\n    const holiday = await prisma.holiday.create({\n      data: { projectId: project.id, date: input.date, name: input.name },\n    });\n    res.status(201).json({\n      holiday: toHolidayDto(holiday),\n      tasks: await recomputeProject(project.id),\n    });\n  }),\n);\n\ncalendarRouter.delete(\n  '/holidays/:id',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const holiday = await requireOwnedHoliday(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    await prisma.holiday.delete({ where: { id: holiday.id } });\n    res.json({ tasks: await recomputeProject(holiday.projectId) });\n  }),\n);\n"

  "path": "server/src/routes/analysis.ts",
  "instructions": "Project analysis route (CPM, PERT, costs, workloads)",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport {\n  buildPertNetwork,\n  computeCpm,\n  computeResourceCosts,\n  computeTaskCosts,\n  createCalendar,\n  findOverallocations,\n  type AnalysisResult,\n  type DependencyType,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { prisma } from '../db.js';\nimport { requireOwnedProject } from '../middleware/access.js';\nimport { asyncHandler } from '../middleware/error.js';\nimport { currentUserId, requireAuth } from '../middleware/auth.js';\n\nexport const analysisRouter = Router();\n\nanalysisRouter.use(requireAuth);\n\nanalysisRouter.get(\n  '/projects/:id/analysis',\n  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {\n    const project = await requireOwnedProject(req.params.id, currentUserId(req));\n    const [tasks, dependencies, resources, assignments, holidays] = await Promise.all([\n      prisma.task.findMany({ where: { projectId: project.id }, orderBy: { position: 'asc' } }),\n      prisma.dependency.findMany({ where: { predecessor: { projectId: project.id } } }),\n      prisma.resource.findMany({ where: { projectId: project.id }, orderBy: { name: 'asc' } }),\n      prisma.assignment.findMany({ where: { task: { projectId: project.id } } }),\n      prisma.holiday.findMany({ where: { projectId: project.id } }),\n    ]);\n\n    const calendar = createCalendar(\n      project.weekendDays,\n      holidays.map((holiday) => holiday.date),\n    );\n    const deps = dependencies.map((dependency) => ({\n      predecessorId: dependency.predecessorId,\n      successorId: dependency.successorId,\n      type: (dependency.type === 'SS' ? 'SS' : 'FS') as DependencyType,\n      lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n    }));\n\n    const cpm = computeCpm(\n      tasks.map((task) => ({\n        id: task.id,\n        name: task.name,\n        parentId: task.parentId,\n        durationDays: task.durationDays,\n        scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n        scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n      })),\n      deps,\n      calendar,\n      project.startDate,\n    );\n\n    const pert = buildPertNetwork(\n      tasks.map((task) => ({\n        id: task.id,\n        name: task.name,\n        optimisticDays: task.optimisticDays,\n        pessimisticDays: task.pessimisticDays,\n      })),\n      deps,\n      cpm,\n    );\n\n    const costTasks = tasks.map((task) => ({\n      id: task.id,\n      name: task.name,\n      parentId: task.parentId,\n      durationDays: task.durationDays,\n      scheduledStart: task.scheduledStart,\n      scheduledEnd: task.scheduledEnd,\n    }));\n    const costAssignments = assignments.map((assignment) => ({\n      id: assignment.id,\n      taskId: assignment.taskId,\n      resourceId: assignment.resourceId,\n      allocationPercent: assignment.allocationPercent,\n    }));\n    const costResources = resources.map((resource) => ({\n      id: resource.id,\n      name: resource.name,\n      salary: resource.salary,\n    }));\n\n    const taskCosts = computeTaskCosts(costTasks, costAssignments, costResources);\n    const resourceCosts = computeResourceCosts(costTasks, costAssignments, costResources);\n    const resourceById = new Map(resources.map((resource) => [resource.id, resource]));\n\n    const result: AnalysisResult = {\n      summary: {\n        startDate: cpm.projectStartDate,\n        endDate: cpm.projectEndDate,\n        totalWorkingDays: cpm.totalWorkingDays,\n        totalCost: taskCosts.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.cost, 0),\n        totalTasks: cpm.tasks.length,\n        criticalTaskCount: cpm.criticalTasks.length,\n      },\n      cpm: cpm.tasks,\n      criticalPaths: cpm.criticalPaths,\n      criticalPathNames: cpm.criticalPaths.map((path) =>\n        path.map((id) => cpm.byId.get(id)?.name ?? id),\n      ),\n      pert,\n      costs: {\n        total: taskCosts.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.cost, 0),\n        tasks: taskCosts,\n        resources: resourceCosts.map((cost) => {\n          const resource = resourceById.get(cost.resourceId);\n          return {\n            ...cost,\n            name: resource?.name ?? '',\n            email: resource?.email ?? '',\n            role: resource?.role ?? '',\n          };\n        }),\n      },\n      overallocations: findOverallocations(costTasks, costAssignments, costResources, calendar),\n    };\n\n    res.json(result);\n  }),\n);\n"
}

  "content": "import cors from 'cors';\nimport express, { type Express } from 'express';\nimport helmet from 'helmet';\nimport { errorHandler, notFoundHandler } from './middleware/error.js';\nimport { analysisRouter } from './routes/analysis.js';\nimport { assignmentRouter } from './routes/assignments.js';\nimport { authRouter } from './routes/auth.js';\nimport { calendarRouter } from './routes/calendar.js';\nimport { dependencyRouter } from './routes/dependencies.js';\nimport { projectRouter } from './routes/projects.js';\nimport { resourceRouter } from './routes/resources.js';\nimport { taskRouter } from './routes/tasks.js';\n\n/** Membangun aplikasi Express lengkap (dipakai server dan test integrasi). */\nexport function createApp(): Express {\n  const app = express();\n\n  app.use(helmet());\n  app.use(cors());\n  app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' }));\n\n  app.get('/api/health', (_req, res) => {\n    res.json({ status: 'ok', time: new Date().toISOString() });\n  });\n\n  app.use('/api/auth', authRouter);\n  app.use('/api', projectRouter);\n  app.use('/api', taskRouter);\n  app.use('/api', dependencyRouter);\n  app.use('/api', resourceRouter);\n  app.use('/api', assignmentRouter);\n  app.use('/api', calendarRouter);\n  app.use('/api', analysisRouter);\n\n  app.use(notFoundHandler);\n  app.use(errorHandler);\n\n  return app;\n}\n"
}

prisma.task.create({\n      data: {\n        projectId: project.id,\n        name: group.name,\n        position: groupIndex,\n        durationDays: 1,\n        startDate: project.startDate,\n taskIds.set(`group-${groupIndex}`, parent.id);\n\n    for (const [childIndex, child] of group.children.entries()) {\n      const created = await prisma.task.create({\n        data: {\n          projectId: project.id,\n          parentId: parent.id,\n          position: childIndex,\n          name: child.name,\n          durationDays: child.durationDays,\n          startDate: child.progress,\n          optimisticDays: child.optimisticDays ?? null,\n          pessimisticDays: child.pessimisticDays ?? null,\n        },\n      });\n      taskIds.set(child.key, created.id);\n    }\n  }\n\n  for (const group of GROUPS) {\n    for (const child of group.children) {\n      const successorId = taskIds.get(child.key) as string;\n      for (const dependency of child.dependencies ?? []) {\n        const predecessorId = taskIds.get(dependency.from);\n        if (!predecessorId) continue;\n        await prisma.dependency.create({\n          data: {\n            predecessorId,\n            successorId,\n            type: dependency.type,\n            lagDays: dependency.lagDays,\n          },\n        });\n      }\n      for (const assignment of child.assignments) {\n        const resourceId = resourceIds.get(assignment.resource);\n        if (!resourceId) continue;\n        await prisma.assignment.create({\n          data: { taskId: successorId, resourceId, allocationPercent: assignment.percent },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  await recomputeProject(project.id);\n\n  const [taskCount, dependencyCount, assignmentCount] = await Promise.all([\n    prisma.task.count({ where: { projectId: project.id } }),\n    prisma.dependency.count({ where: { predecessor: { projectId: project.id } } }),\n    prisma.assignment.count({ where: { task: { projectId: project.id } } }),\n  ]);\n\n  console.log('[seed] Selesai.');\n  console.log(`[seed] Akun demo: ${DEMO_EMAIL} / ${DEMO_PASSWORD}`);\n  console.log(`[seed] Proyek: ${project.name} (${project.id})`);\n  console.log(\n    `[seed] ${taskCount} tugas, ${dependencyCount} dependensi, ${assignmentCount} alokasi, ${RESOURCES.length} anggota tim.`,\n  );\n}\n\nmain()\n  .catch((error) => {\n    console.error('[seed] Gagal:', error);\n    process.exitCode = 1;\n  })\n  .finally(async () => {\n    await prisma.$disconnect();\n  });\n"

{
  "path": "server/tests/helpers.ts",
  "instructions": "Shared helpers for Supertest integration tests",
  "content": "import type { Express } from 'express';\nimport request from 'supertest';\nimport type { ProjectDto, ResourceDto, TaskDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { createApp } from '../src/app.js';\n\nlet sequence = 0;\n\nexport function makeApp(): Express {\n  return createApp();\n}\n\nexport function authHeader(token: string): Record<string, string> {\n  return { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };\n}\n\nexport function uniqueEmail(prefix = 'user'): string {\n  sequence += 1;\n  return `${prefix}${Date.now()}-${sequence}@example.com`;\n}\n\nexport interface TestUser {\n  token: string;\n  userId: string;\n  email: string;\n  password: string;\n}\n\nexport async function registerUser(app: Express, name = 'Pengguna Uji'): Promise<TestUser> {\n  const email = uniqueEmail();\n  const password = 'Password123';\n  const response = await request(app)\n    .post('/api/auth/register')\n    .send({ name, email, password })\n    .expect(201);\n  return { token: response.body.token, userId: response.body.user.id, email, password };\n}\n\nexport async function createProject(\n  app: Express,\n  token: string,\n  overrides: Partial<{ name: string; startDate: string; weekendDays: number[] }> = {},\n): Promise<ProjectDto> {\n  const response = await request(app)\n    .post('/api/projects')\n    .set(authHeader(token))\n    .send({\n      name: 'Proyek Uji',\n      startDate: '2026-10-19',\n      ...overrides,\n    })\n    .expect(201);\n  return response.body.project as ProjectDto;\n}\n\nexport interface CreateTaskOptions {\n  name: string;\n  durationDays: number;\n  startDate?: string;\n  parentId?: string | null;\n  priority?: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH';\n  color?: string;\n  progress?: number;\n  optimisticDays?: number;\n  pessimisticDays?: number;\n  position?: number;\n}\n\nexport async function createTask(\n  app: Express,\n  token: string,\n  projectId: string,\n  options: CreateTaskOptions,\n): Promise<TaskDto> {\n  const response = await request(app)\n    .post(`/api/projects/${projectId}/tasks`)\n    .set(authHeader(token))\n    .send({ startDate: '2026-10-19', ...options })\n    .expect(201);\n  return response.body.task as TaskDto;\n}\n\nexport async function createResource(\n  app: Express,\n  token: string,\n  projectId: string,\n  overrides: Partial<{ name: string; email: string; role: string; salary: number }> = {},\n): Promise<ResourceDto> {\n  const response = await request(app)\n    .post(`/api/projects/${projectId}/resources`)\n    .set(authHeader(token))\n    .send({\n      name: 'Anggota Uji',\n      email: uniqueEmail('anggota'),\n      role: 'Developer',\n      salary: 10_000_000,\n      ...overrides,\n    })\n    .expect(201);\n  return response.body.resource as ResourceDto;\n}\n\nexport function findTask(tasks: TaskDto[], name: string): TaskDto {\n  const task = tasks.find((item) => item.name === name);\n  if (!task) throw new Error(`Tugas ${name} tidak ditemukan dalam respons.`);\n  return task;\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\nimport request from 'supertest';\nimport type { AnalysisResult, ProjectDto, TaskDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport {\n  authHeader,\n  createProject,\n  createResource,\n  createTask,\n  findTask,\n  makeApp,\n  registerUser,\n  type TestUser,\n} from './helpers.js';\n\nconst app = makeApp();\nlet token: string;\nlet project: ProjectDto;\nconst ids: Record<string, string> = {};\n\nbeforeAll(async () => {\n  const user: TestUser = await registerUser(app);\n  token = user.token;\n  project = await createProject(app, token, { name: 'Proyek Analisis' });\n\n  const plan: Array<[string, number]> = [\n    ['A', 3],\n    ['B', 2],\n    ['C', 5],\n    ['D', 1],\n  ];\n  for (const [name, duration] of plan) {\n    const task = await createTask(app, token, project.id, {\n      name,\n      durationDays: duration,\n      startDate: '2026-10-19',\n    });\n    ids[name] = task.id;\n  }\n\n  const links: Array<[string, string]> = [\n    ['A', 'B'],\n    ['A', 'C'],\n    ['B', 'D'],\n    ['C', 'D'],\n  ];\n  for (const [predecessor, successor] of links) {\n    await request(app)\n      .post(`/api/tasks/${ids[successor]}/dependencies`)\n      .set(authHeader(token))\n      .send({ predecessorId: ids[predecessor], type: 'FS', lagDays: 0 })\n      .expect(201);\n  }\n});\n\nasync function fetchAnalysis(): Promise<AnalysisResult> {\n  const response = await request(app)\n    .get(`/api/projects/${project.id}/analysis`)\n    .set(authHeader(token))\n    .expect(200);\n  return response.body as AnalysisResult;\n}\n\ndescribe('analisis proyek', () => {\n  it('menghitung critical path: A, C, D kritis dan B punya float 3', async () => {\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    const byId = new Map(analysis.cpm.map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n\n    expect(analysis.summary.criticalTaskCount).toBe(3);\n    expect(byId.get(ids.A)?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(byId.get(ids.C)?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(byId.get(ids.D)?.critical).toBe(true);\n    expect(byId.get(ids.B)?.critical).toBe(false);\n    expect(byId.get(ids.B)?.totalFloat).toBe(3);\n    expect(analysis.criticalPathNames[0]).toEqual(['A', 'C', 'D']);\n  });\n\n  it('meringkas tanggal mulai, selesai, dan total hari kerja', async () => {\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    expect(analysis.summary.startDate).toBe('2026-10-19');\n    expect(analysis.summary.endDate).toBe('2026-10-29');\n    expect(analysis.summary.totalWorkingDays).toBe(9);\n  });\n\n  it('membangun jaringan PERT dengan node kritis bertanda', async () => {\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    const nodes = analysis.pert.nodes;\n    expect(nodes).toHaveLength(4);\n    expect(nodes.filter((node) => node.critical)).toHaveLength(3);\n    expect(nodes.find((node) => node.id === ids.D)?.column).toBe(2);\n    expect(analysis.pert.edges).toHaveLength(4);\n    expect(analysis.pert.edges.filter((edge) => edge.critical)).toHaveLength(2);\n  });\n\n  it('menghitung biaya proyek dari gaji anggota', async () => {\n    const resource = await createResource(app, token, project.id, {\n      name: 'Joko Susilo',\n      salary: 22_000_000,\n    });\n    await request(app)\n      .post(`/api/tasks/${ids.A}/assignments`)\n      .set(authHeader(token))\n      .send({ resourceId: resource.id, allocationPercent: 100 })\n      .expect(201);\n\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    // Tarif harian 1.000.000 x 3 hari kerja = 3.000.000\n    expect(analysis.costs.total).toBe(3_000_000);\n    expect(analysis.costs.resources[0].totalCost).toBe(3_000_000);\n  });\n\n  it('menampilkan peringatan over-alokasi', async () => {\n    const resource = await createResource(app, token, project.id, {\n      name: 'Kartika Sari',\n      salary: 9_000_000,\n    });\n    const first = await createTask(app, token, project.id, {\n      name: 'Over 1',\n      durationDays: 2,\n      startDate: '2026-10-19',\n    });\n    const second = await createTask(app, token, project.id, {\n      name: 'Over 2',\n      durationDays: 2,\n      startDate: '2026-10-19',\n    });\n    for (const task of [first, second]) {\n      await request(app)\n        .post(`/api/tasks/${task.id}/assignments`)\n        .set(authHeader(token))\n        .send({ resourceId: resource.id, allocationPercent: 60 })\n        .expect(201);\n    }\n\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    const warnings = analysis.overallocations.filter(\n      (warning) => warning.resourceId === resource.id,\n    );\n    expect(warnings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(warnings[0].totalPercent).toBe(120);\n  });\n\n  it('menolak analisis proyek milik pengguna lain', async () => {\n    const other = await registerUser(app);\n    await request(app)\n      .get(`/api/projects/${project.id}/analysis`)\n      .set(authHeader(other.token))\n      .expect(404);\n  });\n\n  it('menghitung estimasi PERT bila optimistic dan pessimistic diisi', async () => {\n    const estimated = await createTask(app, token, project.id, {\n      name: 'Estimasi PERT',\n      durationDays: 10,\n      optimisticDays: 8,\n      pessimisticDays: 15,\n    });\n    const analysis = await fetchAnalysis();\n    const node = analysis.pert.nodes.find((item) => item.id === estimated.id);\n    expect(node?.expectedDays).toBeCloseTo(10.5, 2);\n    expect(node?.variance).toBeCloseTo(1.3611, 3);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('konsistensi tugas setelah analisis', () => {\n  it('menyimpan jadwal hasil hitung pada database', async () => {\n    const snapshot = await request(app)\n      .get(`/api/projects/${project.id}`)\n      .set(authHeader(token))\n      .expect(200);\n    const taskD = findTask(snapshot.body.tasks as TaskDto[], 'D');\n    expect(taskD.scheduledStart).toBe('2026-10-29');\n    expect(taskD.scheduledEnd).toBe('2026-10-29');\n  });\n});\n"
}
