> Blueprint structure: components/ gantt/ pert/ tasks/ team/ calendar/ ui/, pages/ Login Register Projects Gantt Team Calendar Analysis, api/ hooks/ lib/ styles/. So layout can be components/ui/ or components/layout — I'll put AppLayout in components/ root.
> 
> Let me plan the Gantt page carefully since it's the most complex.
> 
> **Data flow**: `useProject(projectId)` → ProjectSnapshot via react-query. All mutations invalidate/update.
> 
> **Task tree**: build from tasks by parentId, sorted by position. Collapsible state in a Set of collapsed ids.
> 
> **Timeline**: 
> - zoom: 'day' | 'week' | 'month' → pixels per day: day=40, week=14, month=5? Let me define columnWidth per day: day: 36px, week: 12px, month: 4px.
> - Range: from min(scheduledStart) to max(scheduledEnd), padded.
> - x(date) = workingDays? No: "Bar tetap digambar menurut tanggal kalender, sehingga bar melintasi akhir pekan dan hari libur." And "posisi x berasal dari scheduledStart dan scheduledEnd (bukan hitungan hari kalender)". Hmm, contradictory? Let me interpret: x position = (calendarDaysBetween(rangeStart, date)) * dayWidth. The bar spans from scheduledStart to scheduledEnd inclusive → width = (diff+1)*dayWidth. So bars are drawn by calendar dates. That's consistent: "panah ... posisi x berasal dari scheduledStart dan scheduledEnd" just means use those fields (not computed durations).
> 
> So x(dateStr) = (diffInCalendarDays(rangeStart, date)) * dayWidth.
> 
> Header: for day zoom show each day; week zoom group by week (show week start date); month zoom group by month. Let me keep the header simple: render tick marks:
> - day: every day a narrow column with day number
> - week: every week a column of 7*dayWidth with label "dd Mmm"
> - month: label per month
> 
> Rows: each visible task gets a row of height 36px. Left table row height must match.
> 
> Scrolling sync: the right panel scrolls vertically and left table too; sync scrollTop via onScroll handlers with a guard.
> 
> Implementation approach: container with fixed height; two panes; left pane overflow-y hidden? Simplest: left pane has overflow hidden and its inner content is translated by -scrollTop; right pane scrolls both directions. Let me do: right pane scrolls (overflow: auto), and on scroll we set leftTableInnerRef.style.transform = `translateY(-${scrollTop}px)`.
> 
> Header row for the timeline should stay fixed vertically: put the timeline header in a separate div that scrolls horizontally in sync. Simpler approach: single right pane with `overflow: auto`; inside it, a sticky header (position: sticky; top: 0). SVG bars inside; the header is a separate div with position sticky top-0 and left-0. Combined with horizontal scroll, the sticky header needs `z-index`. Let me do: right pane contains a div (width = totalWidth) with:
> - header div: position sticky, top 0, height 32
> - body svg: width totalWidth, height rows*rowHeight
> 
> The svg draws everything (weekend shading, holidays, today line, bars, dependency arrows).
> 
> Actually easier: draw the whole timeline (header + grid + bars + arrows) in ONE SVG, with the header group translated to stay at the top when scrolling vertically. That's complex. Let me instead use two SVGs: header SVG (sticky) and body SVG. Since both are inside a horizontally+vertically scrollable container, horizontal scroll applies to both naturally (same width), and the header stays visible via position: sticky.
> 
> Let me structure:
> ```jsx
> <div className="flex">
>   <div className="w-[420px] shrink-0 border-r">   // left table
>      <div className="h-9 border-b ...">header</div>
>      <div className="overflow-hidden flex-1">    // viewport masked
>         <div ref={leftInnerRef}>{rows}</div>
>      </div>
>   </div>
>   <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto" ref={rightRef} onScroll={...}>
>      <div style={{width: totalWidth}}>
>         <div className="sticky top-0 z-10" style={{height:32}}><HeaderSvg/></div>
>         <svg width={totalWidth} height={rows*rowHeight}>...</svg>
>      </div>
>   </div>
> </div>
> ```
> The left table viewport height must equal the right pane height. Use a fixed height container (e.g., calc(100vh - 260px)) — with a flex-1 and min-h-0.
> 
> Drag & resize: implement with mouse events on SVG bar elements.
> - Drag: mousedown on bar body (leaf only) → track mousedown x, compute dayOffset = round((dx)/dayWidth) → preview startDate = shift by calendar days snap to working day → preview: set local state `dragState` and compute a preview schedule using computeSchedule from shared (with modified startDate) for the dragged task. Simpler: preview by shifting the dragged task's bar visually only (and show a tooltip), then on mouseup send PATCH with the new startDate, and use the server response.
> 
> The blueprint says: "Selama drag, client memakai mesin shared untuk pratinjau." So I should recompute the preview schedule using computeSchedule with the modified startDate, so dependent tasks preview their new positions. Let's do that: on drag, build a local tasks array with the dragged task's startDate changed, run computeSchedule(tasks, deps, calendar) → preview map. Render bars from the preview map while dragging.

{
  "path": "client/src/lib/taskTree.ts",
  "instructions": "Task tree helpers shared by the Gantt page and dialogs",
  "content": "import {\n  createCalendar,\n  getSubtreeIds,\n  type CalendarConfig,\n  type DependencyDto,\n  type HolidayDto,\n  type TaskDto,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\n\nexport interface TaskRow {\n  task: TaskDto;\n  depth: number;\n  hasChildren: boolean;\n  collapsed: boolean;\n  wbs: string;\n}\n\nexport function sortByPosition(tasks: TaskDto[]): TaskDto[] {\n  return [...tasks].sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);\n}\n\n/** Menyusun daftar baris (WBS) sesuai urutan dan status lipat. */\nexport function buildTaskRows(tasks: TaskDto[], collapsedIds: Set<string>): TaskRow[] {\n  const childrenOf = new Map<string | null, TaskDto[]>();\n  for (const task of tasks) {\n    const key = task.parentId ?? null;\n    const list = childrenOf.get(key) ?? [];\n    list.push(task);\n    childrenOf.set(key, list);\n  }\n\n  const rows: TaskRow[] = [];\n  const walk = (parentId: string | null, depth: number, prefix: string): void => {\n    const siblings = sortByPosition(childrenOf.get(parentId) ?? []);\n    siblings.forEach((task, index) => {\n      const wbs = prefix ? `${prefix}.${index + 1}` : `${index + 1}`;\n      const children = childrenOf.get(task.id) ?? [];\n      const collapsed = collapsedIds.has(task.id);\n      rows.push({ task, depth, hasChildren: children.length > 0, collapsed, wbs });\n      if (children.length > 0 && !collapsed) walk(task.id, depth + 1, wbs);\n    });\n  };\n  walk(null, 0, '');\n  return rows;\n}\n\n/** Semua id tugas yang punya anak. */\nexport function parentIdSet(tasks: TaskDto[]): Set<string> {\n  const ids = new Set(tasks.map((task) => task.id));\n  const parents = new Set<string>();\n  for (const task of tasks) {\n    if (task.parentId && ids.has(task.parentId)) parents.add(task.parentId);\n  }\n  return parents;\n}\n\nexport function isParentTask(tasks: TaskDto[], taskId: string): boolean {\n  return parentIdSet(tasks).has(taskId);\n}\n\n/** CalendarConfig dari data proyek. */\nexport function buildCalendar(\n  weekendDays: string | number[],\n  holidays: Array<HolidayDto | string>,\n): CalendarConfig {\n  const holidayDates = holidays.map((holiday) =>\n    typeof holiday === 'string' ? holiday : holiday.date,\n  );\n  return createCalendar(weekendDays, holidayDates);\n}\n\n/** Tugas yang tidak boleh menjadi induk bagi `taskId` (dirinya dan keturunannya). */\nexport function forbiddenParentIds(tasks: TaskDto[], taskId: string): Set<string> {\n  return new Set(getSubtreeIds(tasks, taskId));\n}\n\n/** Ringkasan milik tugas seperti \"2/5 selesei\". */\nexport function countCompleted(children: TaskDto[]): number {\n  return children.filter((child) => child.progress >= 100).length;\n}\n\nexport function dependenciesOf(dependencies: DependencyDto[], successorId: string): DependencyDto[] {\n  return dependencies.filter((dependency) => dependency.successorId === successorId);\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/api/client.ts",
  "instructions": "Fetch wrapper for the REST API",
  "content": "import type { ApiErrorBody } from '@gantt/shared';\n\nconst TOKEN_KEY = 'gantt-token';\nconst USER_KEY = 'gantt-user';\n\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  readonly status: number;\n  readonly code: string;\n  readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;\n\n  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string, details: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = 'ApiError';\n    this.status = status;\n    this.code = code;\n    this.details = details;\n  }\n\n  /** Daftar pesan validasi per field, mis. `{ name: 'Nama wajib diisi.' }`. */\n  get fieldErrors(): Record<string, string> {\n    const issues = this.details.issues;\n    if (!Array.isArray(issues)) return {};\n    const result: Record<string, string> = {};\n    for (const issue of issues as Array<{ path?: string; message?: string }>) {\n      if (issue.path && issue.message && !result[issue.path]) {\n        result[issue.path] = issue.message;\n      }\n    }\n    return result;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function getStoredToken(): string | null {\n  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);\n}\n\nexport function storeSession(token: string, user: unknown): void {\n  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);\n  localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));\n}\n\nexport function clearSession(): void {\n  localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);\n  localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);\n}\n\nexport function getStoredUser<T>(): T | null {\n  const raw = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);\n  if (!raw) return null;\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\ntype Method = 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE';\n\ninterface RequestOptions {\n  method?: Method;\n  body?: unknown;\n  signal?: AbortSignal;\n}\n\n/** Memanggil REST API dengan header Authorization bila token tersedia. */\nexport async function apiRequest<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {\n  const { method = 'GET', body, signal } = options;\n  const token = getStoredToken();\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};\n  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;\n  if (body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';\n\n  const response = await fetch(`/api${path}`, {\n    method,\n    headers,\n    signal,\n    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),\n  });\n\n  if (response.status === 204) {\n    return undefined as T;\n  }\n\n  const text = await response.text();\n  const payload = text.length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(text) as unknown) : null;\n\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const errorBody = payload as ApiErrorBody | null;\n    throw new ApiError(\n      response.status,\n      errorBody?.error?.code ?? 'UNKNOWN',\n      errorBody?.error?.message ?? 'Terjadi kesalahan saat menghubungi server.',\n      (errorBody?.error?.details as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},\n    );\n  }\n\n  return payload as T;\n}\n\nconst identity = <T>(value: T): T => value;\nexport const passthrough = identity;\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/hooks/useAuth.tsx",
  "instructions": "Authentication context provider",
  "content": "import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';\nimport type { AuthResponse, UserDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport {\n  apiRequest,\n  clearSession,\n  getStoredToken,\n  getStoredUser,\n  storeSession,\n} from '../api/client';\n\ninterface AuthContextValue {\n  user: UserDto | null;\n  ready: boolean;\n  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;\n  register: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;\n  logout: () => void;\n}\n\nconst AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);\n\nexport function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserDto | null>(() => getStoredUser<UserDto>());\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    const token = getStoredToken();\n    if (!token) {\n      setReady(true);\n      return;\n    }\n    apiRequest<{ user: UserDto }>('/auth/me')\n      .then((response) => {\n        if (!cancelled) setUser(response.user);\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        if (!cancelled) {\n          clearSession();\n          setUser(null);\n        }\n      })\n      .finally(() => {\n        if (!cancelled) setReady(true);\n      });\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const login = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {\n    const response = await apiRequest<AuthResponse>('/auth/login', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: { email, password },\n    });\n    storeSession(response.token, response.user);\n    setUser(response.user);\n  }, []);\n\n  const register = useCallback(async (name: string, email: string, password: string) => {\n    const response = await apiRequest<AuthResponse>('/auth/register', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: { name, email, password },\n    });\n    storeSession(response.token, response.user);\n    setUser(response.user);\n  }, []);\n\n  const logout = useCallback(() => {\n    clearSession();\n    setUser(null);\n  }, []);\n\n  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(\n    () => ({ user, ready, login, register, logout }),\n    [user, ready, login, register, logout],\n  );\n\n  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;\n}\n\nexport function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {\n  const context = useContext(AuthContext);\n  if (!context) throw new Error('useAuth harus dipakai di dalam AuthProvider.');\n  return context;\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport type { ProjectDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { apiRequest } from '../api/client';\n\nexport function useProjects() {\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: ['projects'],\n    queryFn: () => apiRequest<{ projects: ProjectDto[] }>('/projects'),\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useCreateProject() {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (input: { name: string; description?: string | null; startDate: string; weekendDays?: number[] }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ project: ProjectDto }>('/projects', { method: 'POST', body: input }),\n    onSuccess: () => {\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['projects'] });\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useDeleteProject() {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (projectId: string) => apiRequest<void>(`/projects/${projectId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: () => {\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['projects'] });\n    },\n  });\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/hooks/useProjectData.ts",
  "instructions": "Snapshot, analysis queries and all project mutations",
  "content": "import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient, type QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport type {\n  AnalysisResult,\n  AssignmentDto,\n  DependencyDto,\n  HolidayDto,\n  ProjectDto,\n  ProjectSnapshot,\n  ResourceDto,\n  TaskDto,\n} from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { apiRequest } from '../api/client';\n\nconst projectKey = (projectId: string) => ['project', projectId] as const;\nconst analysisKey = (projectId: string) => ['analysis', projectId] as const;\n\nexport function useProjectSnapshot(projectId: string) {\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: projectKey(projectId),\n    queryFn: () => apiRequest<ProjectSnapshot>(`/projects/${projectId}`),\n    enabled: projectId.length > 0,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useAnalysis(projectId: string, enabled = true) {\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: analysisKey(projectId),\n    queryFn: () => apiRequest<AnalysisResult>(`/projects/${projectId}/analysis`),\n    enabled: projectId.length > 0 && enabled,\n  });\n}\n\n/** Menerapkan daftar tugas terbaru dari server ke cache tanpa refetch penuh. */\nfunction applyTasks(queryClient: QueryClient, projectId: string, tasks: TaskDto[]): void {\n  queryClient.setQueryData<ProjectSnapshot>(projectKey(projectId), (old) =>\n    old ? { ...old, tasks } : old,\n  );\n  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: analysisKey(projectId) });\n}\n\nfunction refreshSnapshot(queryClient: QueryClient, projectId: string): void {\n  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: projectKey(projectId) });\n  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: analysisKey(projectId) });\n}\n\nexport function useProjectActions(projectId: string) {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  const updateProject = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (input: { name?: string; description?: string | null; startDate?: string }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ project: ProjectDto }>(`/projects/${projectId}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: input }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.setQueryData<ProjectSnapshot>(projectKey(projectId), (old) =>\n        old ? { ...old, project: data.project } : old,\n      );\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['projects'] });\n    },\n  });\n\n  const createTask = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (input: Record<string, unknown>) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[]; task: TaskDto | null }>(`/projects/${projectId}/tasks`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks),\n  });\n\n  const updateTask = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ taskId, input }: { taskId: string; input: Record<string, unknown> }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/tasks/${taskId}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: input }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks),\n  });\n\n  const deleteTask = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (taskId: string) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/tasks/${taskId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks),\n  });\n\n  const moveTask = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ taskId, parentId, position }: { taskId: string; parentId: string | null; position: number }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/tasks/${taskId}/move`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: { parentId, position },\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks),\n  });\n\n  const addDependency = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ taskId, input }: { taskId: string; input: { predecessorId: string; type: 'FS' | 'SS'; lagDays: number } }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[]; dependency: DependencyDto }>(`/tasks/${taskId}/dependencies`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks);\n      refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId);\n    },\n  });\n\n  const updateDependency = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ dependencyId, input }: { dependencyId: string; input: { type?: 'FS' | 'SS'; lagDays?: number } }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[]; dependency: DependencyDto }>(`/dependencies/${dependencyId}`, {\n        method: 'PATCH',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks);\n      refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId);\n    },\n  });\n\n  const deleteDependency = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (dependencyId: string) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/dependencies/${dependencyId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks);\n      refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId);\n    },\n  });\n\n  const createResource = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (input: { name: string; email: string; role: string; salary: number }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ resource: ResourceDto }>(`/projects/${projectId}/resources`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const updateResource = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ resourceId, input }: { resourceId: string; input: Record<string, unknown> }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ resource: ResourceDto }>(`/resources/${resourceId}`, {\n        method: 'PATCH',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const deleteResource = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (resourceId: string) =>\n      apiRequest<void>(`/resources/${resourceId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const addAssignment = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ taskId, input }: { taskId: string; input: { resourceId: string; allocationPercent: number } }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ assignment: AssignmentDto }>(`/tasks/${taskId}/assignments`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const updateAssignment = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: ({ assignmentId, input }: { assignmentId: string; input: { allocationPercent: number } }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ assignment: AssignmentDto }>(`/assignments/${assignmentId}`, {\n        method: 'PATCH',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const deleteAssignment = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (assignmentId: string) =>\n      apiRequest<void>(`/assignments/${assignmentId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: () => refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId),\n  });\n\n  const updateCalendar = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (weekendDays: number[]) =>\n      apiRequest<{ project: ProjectDto; tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/projects/${projectId}/calendar`, {\n        method: 'PUT',\n        body: { weekendDays },\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.setQueryData<ProjectSnapshot>(projectKey(projectId), (old) =>\n        old ? { ...old, project: data.project, tasks: data.tasks } : old,\n      );\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: analysisKey(projectId) });\n    },\n  });\n\n  const addHoliday = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (input: { date: string; name: string }) =>\n      apiRequest<{ holiday: HolidayDto; tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/projects/${projectId}/holidays`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: input,\n      }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks);\n      refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId);\n    },\n  });\n\n  const deleteHoliday = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: (holidayId: string) =>\n      apiRequest<{ tasks: TaskDto[] }>(`/holidays/${holidayId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      applyTasks(queryClient, projectId, data.tasks);\n      refreshSnapshot(queryClient, projectId);\n    },\n  });\n\n  return {\n    updateProject,\n    createTask,\n    updateTask,\n    deleteTask,\n    moveTask,\n    addDependency,\n    updateDependency,\n    deleteDependency,\n    createResource,\n    updateResource,\n    deleteResource,\n    addAssignment,\n    updateAssignment,\n    deleteAssignment,\n    updateCalendar,\n    addHoliday,\n    deleteHoliday,\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/components/ui/Toast.tsx",
  "instructions": "Toast provider and hook",
  "content": "import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';\n\nexport type ToastKind = 'success' | 'error' | 'info';\n\ninterface ToastItem {\n  id: number;\n  kind: ToastKind;\n  message: string;\n}\n\ninterface ToastContextValue {\n  success: (message: string) => void;\n  error: (message: string) => void;\n  info: (message: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | null>(null);\n\nconst KIND_CLASSES: Record<ToastKind, string> = {\n  success: 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800',\n  error: 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700',\n  info: 'border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800',\n};\n\nexport function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastItem[]>([]);\n  const counter = useRef(0);\n\n  const push = useCallback((kind: ToastKind, message: string) => {\n    counter.current += 1;\n    const id = counter.current;\n    setToasts((current) => [...current, { id, kind, message }]);\n    window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setToasts((current) => current.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id));\n    }, 5000);\n  }, []);\n\n  const value = useMemo<ToastContextValue>(\n    () => ({\n      success: (message) => push('success', message),\n      error: (message) => push('error', message),\n      info: (message) => push('info', message),\n    }),\n    [push],\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <ToastContext.Provider value={value}>\n      {children}\n      <div className=\"pointer-events-none fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex w-80 flex-col gap-2\">\n        {toasts.map((toast) => (\n          <div\n            key={toast.id}\n            role=\"status\"\n            data-testid=\"toast\"\n            className={`pointer-events-auto rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-sm shadow-sm ${KIND_CLASSES[toast.kind]}`}\n          >\n            {toast.message}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </ToastContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function useToast(): ToastContextValue {\n  const context = useContext(ToastContext);\n  if (!context) throw new Error('useToast harus dipakai di dalam ToastProvider.');\n  return context;\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "path": "client/src/components/ui/Modal.tsx",
  "instructions": "Reusable modal dialog",
  "content": "import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';\n\ninterface ModalProps {\n  open: boolean;\n  title: string;\n  description?: string;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  children: ReactNode;\n  footer?: ReactNode;\n  size?: 'md' | 'lg';\n}\n\nexport function Modal({ open, title, description, onClose, children, footer, size = 'md' }: ModalProps) {\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return undefined;\n    const handler = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {\n      if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose();\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);\n  }, [open, onClose]);\n\n  if (!open) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900/40 p-4\">\n      <div\n        role=\"dialog\"\n        aria-modal=\"true\"\n        aria-label={title}\n        className={`mt-10 w-full ${size === 'lg' ? 'max-w-3xl' : 'max-w-lg'} rounded-lg bg-white shadow-xl`}\n      >\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3\">\n          <div>\n            <h2 className=\"text-base font-semibold text-slate-900\">{title}</h2>\n            {description ? <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500\">{description}</p> : null}\n          </div>\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1\" onClick={onClose} aria-label=\"Tutup\">\n            ✕\n          </button>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4\">{children}</div>\n        {footer ? (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 px-4 py-3\">{footer}</div>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

(\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary mt-3\" onClick={onRetry}>\n          Coba lagi\n        </button>\n      ) : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function EmptyState({\n  title,\n  description,\n  action,\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  description?: string;\n  action?: ReactNode;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-8 text-center\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm font-medium text-slate-700\">{title}</p>\n      {description ? <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">{description}</p> : null}\n      {action}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

'Memproses…' : confirmLabel}\n          </button>\n        </>\n      }\n    >\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-600\">{message}</p>\n    </Modal>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Now the Gantt components — the biggest chunk. Let me write:
> 
> 1. components/gantt/TaskTable.tsx — presentational table of rows.
> 2. components/gantt/GanttChart.tsx — timeline SVG + drag/resize + composite with TaskTable + scroll sync.
> 3. components/gantt/TaskDialog.tsx — task create/edit form with predecessors and assignees.
> 
> Let me carefully design GanttChart.
> 
> ```tsx
> interface GanttChartProps {
>   tasks: TaskDto[];
>   dependencies: DependencyDto[];
>   holidays: HolidayDto[];
>   weekendDays: string;
>   projectStart: string;
>   showCriticalPath: boolean;
>   onRequestEdit: (task: TaskDto) => void;
>   onResize: (taskId: string, durationDays: number) => void;
>   onDrag: (taskId: string, startDate: string) => void;
>   onMoveTask: (taskId: string) => void;  // maybe not needed
> }
> ```
> 
> Rows computed inside using buildTaskRows + collapsed state internal.
> 
> Timeline computations:
> ```ts
> const dayWidth = zoom === 'day' ? 36 : zoom === 'week' ? 14 : 5;
> const calendar = buildCalendar(weekendDays, holidays);
> const range = useMemo(() => {
>   const starts = tasks.map(t => t.scheduledStart);
>   const ends = tasks.map(t => t.scheduledEnd);
>   const min = starts.length ? starts.reduce((a,b) => a<b?a:b) : projectStart;
>   const max = ends.length ? ends.reduce((a,b) => a>b?a:b) : projectStart;
>   const from = formatDate(addDays(toUTCDate(min), -3));
>   const to = formatDate(addDays(toUTCDate(max), 5));
>   return { from, to, days: diffInCalendarDays(toUTCDate(from), toUTCDate(to)) + 1 };
> }, [tasks, projectStart]);
> const xOf = (date: string) => diffInCalendarDays(toUTCDate(range.from), toUTCDate(date)) * dayWidth;
> ```
> 
> Total width = range.days * dayWidth.
> 
> Header:
> - day zoom: a tick per day, width dayWidth, with the day number; label the month at the first day of the month or every Monday. For clarity, render two header rows: top row = months, bottom row = days/weeks. Let me keep it simple: one header row of height 32:
>   - day: each day shows day-of-month; the first working day of a week shows "d Mmm"? Too cramped at 36px. Let me show the day number and mark weekends with a different text color.
>   - week: columns of 7 days (each 7*dayWidth) with label "d Mmm".
>   - month: columns per month with label "Mmm yyyy".
> 
> Simplify: build header segments:
> ```ts
> type Segment = { start: string; days: number; label: string };
> function buildHeaderSegments(range, zoom): Segment[]
> ```
> - for 'day': segments of 1 day each, label = String(dayOfMonth); maybe add weekday initial when zoom==='day' and there's room (36px is enough for "Jum"? 36px with text-[10px] maybe). Let me label day zoom segments with the day number and, above... ok single row: `${dayOfMonth}` and a small weekday abbreviation under? Just day number with weekend styling, plus a month separator line.
>   Actually let me include the weekday abbreviation in a second line inside the 32px header: font-size 9px. Doable: `<text y=12>{weekday short}</text><text y=24>{dayOfMonth}</text>`.
> - for 'week': segment per 7 days starting from the range start, label = formatDisplayDate(segmentStart) shortened to "23 Okt".
> - for 'month': segment per month, label = "Okt 2026".
> 
> Good.
> 
> Body SVG height = rows.length * ROW_HEIGHT (36).
> 
> Draw in body SVG:
> 1. working-day/weekend/holiday background: for each day in the range, a rect (width dayWidth, height full) with class: weekend → fill #f1f5f9; holiday → fill #fee2e2; else white. At month zoom dayWidth=5, drawing thousands of rects is OK for a project of ~100 days.
> 2. Vertical grid lines.
> 3. Today line (if today within range): red vertical line + label.
> 4. Rows separator lines.
> 5. Bars: for each row, bar x = xOf(scheduledStart), width = (diff+1)*dayWidth - 2 (small gap), rounded rect, fill = task.color (leaf) or bracket style for parents, progress overlay rect (darker) width = width * progress/100, label text to the right of the bar (task name) or inside if wide enough. Blueprint: table on the left has names, so the bar labels are optional. I'll add the progress % text inside wide bars.
>    Parent bars: "bergaya bracket" → draw a bracket: a horizontal line with two vertical end caps (like ▐▬▬▌). I'll render a rect with lower opacity + end caps.
> 6. Dependency arrows: computed from the *visible* rows (skip if either endpoint is hidden).
> 7. Drag handles for leaf tasks (right edge 6px wide rect, cursor col-resize).
> 
> Critical highlight: if showCriticalPath, critical tasks get stroke red + fill override to #dc2626? "Toggle 'Sorot jalur kritis'" — I'll set stroke to red 2px and fill to a red shade for critical tasks.
> 
> Interactions:
> - onPointerDown on bar body (leaf only, and not on handle) → start drag: record startX = e.clientX, and the original task. Set state { type: 'drag', taskId, startDate, delta }.
> - onPointerMove (on the SVG or window) → delta = Math.round((e.clientX - startX)/dayWidth); requested start = addDays(originalStart, delta) formatted; then compute preview schedule.
> - onPointerUp → commit: onDrag(taskId, requestedStart) (only if delta !== 0); clear state.
> 
> Preview: when drag/resize state is active, compute preview tasks with useMemo:
> ```ts
> const previewTasks = useMemo(() => {
>   if (!interaction) return tasks;
>   const modified = tasks.map(t => t.id === interaction.taskId ? { ...t, startDate: interaction.requestedStart /* or durationDays */ } : t);
>   const schedule = computeSchedule(modified.map(toSchedulable), dependencies.map(...), calendar);
>   return tasks.map(t => { const s = schedule.byId.get(t.id); return s ? { ...t, scheduledStart: s.scheduledStart, scheduledEnd: s.scheduledEnd, durationDays: s.durationDays, progress: s.progress } : t; });
> }, [tasks, dependencies, calendar, interaction]);
> ```
> And the "clamped" indicator: if the preview's scheduledStart for the dragged task != requestedStart → show a tooltip "Dikembalikan ke batas predecessor: <date>". I'll pass a flag via callback: when committing, if preview was clamped, GanttPage shows the info toast. Let me have the callback signature onDrag(taskId, startDate, clamped: boolean) → the page shows a toast if clamped.
> 
> Better: the page can compute it since it has the data. But GanttChart knows the preview. I'll pass `clamped` in the callback.
> 
> For resize: requested duration = workingDaysBetween(originalStart, addDays(originalEnd, delta)) min 1. Preview the same way. Commit onResize(taskId, duration).
> 
> Implementation of pointer events: use React state + window listeners attached in useEffect while interaction is active. Simpler: handle onPointerMove on the wrapper div and onPointerUp on window. Let me attach window listeners in an effect when interaction != null.
> 
> Note: e.clientX with delta in px → days = Math.round(dx / dayWidth) for day zoom; for week/month zoom, dayWidth is small (14/5) so a 1-day granularity is still fine (round to days). Maybe snap to weeks at week zoom? Blueprint says snap to working days. Keep days.
> 
> Tests: I need data-testid attributes:
> - bar: `data-testid={`gantt-bar-${task.id}`}`
> - dependency arrow: `data-testid={`gantt-dependency-${dep.id}`}`
> - row: `data-testid={`gantt-row-${task.id}`}` (in table)
> 
> Now TaskTable:
> ```tsx
> interface TaskTableProps { rows: TaskRow[]; selectedId: string | null; onSelect(id): void; onToggle(id): void; innerRef: Ref<HTMLDivElement>; }
> ```
> Columns: WBS, Nama (indent + collapse triangle), Durasi, Mulai, Selesai, Prioritas, Penanggung jawab, Progres.

allocationPercent: assignment.allocationPercent,\n        }))\n    : [];\n\n  const {\n    register,\n    control,\n    handleSubmit,\n    watch,\n    formState: { errors },\n  } = '',\n      description: task?.description ?? '',\n      parentId: task ? (task.parentId ?? '') : (defaultParentId ?? className=\"space-y-4\" noValidate>\n      <Field label=\"Nama tugas\" htmlFor=\"task-name\" error={errors.name?.message}>\n        <input id=\"task-name\" className=\"input\" {...register('name')} />\n      </Field>\n\n      <Field label=\"Deskripsi\" htmlFor=\"task-description\" error={errors.description?.message}>\n        <textarea id=\"task-description\" rows={2} className=\"input\" {...register('description')} />\n      </Field>\n\n      <div className=\"grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2\">\n        <Field label=\"Tugas induk\" htmlFor=\"task-parent\" error={errors.parentId?.message}>\n              </option>\n            ))}\n          </select>\n        </Field>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"rounded-md bg-slate-50 p-3\">\n        <div className=\"grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2\">\n          <Field\n            label=\"Durasi (hari kerja)\"\n            htmlFor=\"task-duration\"\n            error={errors.durationDays?.message}\n            hint={isParent ? > Remove the pointless hidden mode input and hidden submit button? The hidden submit button is useful for Enter-key submission in the form. Keep it but remove the `value={mode}` hidden input (mode prop then unused → noUnusedLocals? No, it's a destructured prop, and unused destructured props... `noUnusedLocals` flags unused destructured variables? Yes, TS flags unused destructured properties from parameters? Actually TS's noUnusedLocals does report unused destructured elements in parameters only with noUnusedParameters for parameters. For destructured object parameters, TS reports "'mode' is declared but its value is never read" under noUnusedLocals? I believe destructured parameters are checked by noUnusedParameters, and it does report unused destructured bindings.

> ```ts
> async function syncRelations(taskId: string, values, dependencies, assignments) {
>   const currentDeps = dependencies.filter(d => d.successorId === taskId);
>   const incoming = values.predecessors.filter(p => p.predecessorId);
>   // delete removed
>   for (const dep of currentDeps) {
>     if (!incoming.some(p => p.predecessorId === dep.predecessorId)) await actions.deleteDependency.mutateAsync(dep.id);
>   }
>   for (const p of incoming) {
>     const existing = currentDeps.find(d => d.predecessorId === p.predecessorId);
>     if (!existing) await actions.addDependency.mutateAsync({ taskId, input: { predecessorId: p.predecessorId, type: p.type, lagDays: p.lagDays } });
>     else if (existing.type !== p.type || existing.lagDays !== p.lagDays) await actions.updateDependency.mutateAsync({ dependencyId: existing.id, input: { type: p.type, lagDays: p.lagDays } });
>   }
>   // same for assignees
> }
> ```

  "instructions": "Modal wrapper around the task form",
  "content": "import type { AssignmentDto, DependencyDto, ResourceDto, TaskDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';\nimport { TaskForm, type TaskFormValues } from '../tasks/TaskForm';\n\ninterface TaskDialogProps {\n  open: boolean;\n  mode: 'create' | 'edit';\n  task: TaskDto | null;\n  defaultParentId: string | null;\n  tasks: TaskDto[];\n  dependencies: DependencyDto[];\n  resources: ResourceDto[];\n  assignments: AssignmentDto[];\n  submitting: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  onSubmit: (values: TaskFormValues) => void;\n}\n\nexport function TaskDialog({\n  open,\n  mode,\n  task,\n  defaultParentId,\n  tasks,\n  dependencies,\n  resources,\n  assignments,\n  submitting,\n  onClose,\n  onSubmit,\n}: TaskDialogProps) {\n  return (\n    <Modal\n      open={open}\n      size=\"lg\"\n      title={mode === 'create' ? 'Tugas baru' : `Ubah tugas: ${task?.name ?? ''}`}\n      description=\"Durasi dihitung dalam hari kerja sesuai kalender proyek.\"\n      onClose={onClose}\n      footer={\n        <>\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary\" onClick={onClose}>\n            Batal\n          </button>\n          <button type=\"submit\" form=\"task-form\" className=\"btn-primary\" disabled={submitting}>\n            {submitting ? 'Menyimpan…' : 'Simpan'}\n          </button>\n        </>\n      }\n    >\n      <TaskForm\n        formId=\"task-form\"\n        mode={mode}\n        task={task}\n        defaultParentId={defaultParentId}\n        tasks={tasks}\n        dependencies={dependencies}\n        resources={resources}\n        assignments={assignments}\n        submitting={submitting}\n        onSubmit={onSubmit}\n      />\n    </Modal>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { Link, Navigate, Outlet, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';\n\nexport function ProtectedLayout() {\n  const { user, ready } = useAuth();\n  const location = useLocation();\n\n  if (!ready) {\n    return <div className=\"p-8 text-sm text-slate-500\">Memuat sesi…</div>;\n  }\n  if (!user) {\n    return <Navigate to=\"/login\" replace state={{ from: location.pathname }} />;\n  }\n  return <Outlet />;\n}\n\nexport function AppLayout() {\n  const { user, logout } = useAuth();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex min-h-full flex-col\">\n      <header className=\"border-b border-slate-200 bg-white\">\n        <div className=\"mx-auto flex max-w-screen-2xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-3\">\n          <Link to=\"/projects\" className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <span className=\"flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-brand-600 text-sm font-bold text-white\">\n              G\n            </span>\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Manajemen Proyek Gantt</span>\n          </Link>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm\">\n            {user ? <span className=\"hidden text-slate-600 sm:inline\">{user.name}</span> : null}\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary\" onClick={logout}>\n              Keluar\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n      <main className=\"min-h-0 flex-1\">\n        <Outlet />\n      </main>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nconst TABS = [\n  { key: 'gantt', label: 'Gantt' },\n  { key: 'team', label: 'Tim' },\n  { key: 'calendar', label: 'Kalender' },\n  { key: 'analysis', label: 'Analisis' },\n] as const;\n\nexport function ProjectLayout() {\n  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();\n  const location = useLocation();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex min-h-full flex-col\">\n      <nav className=\"border-b border-slate-200 bg-white\">\n        <div className=\"mx-auto flex max-w-screen-2xl gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-4\">\n          {TABS.map((tab) => {\n            const active = location.pathname.includes(`/${tab.key}`);\n            return (\n              <Link\n                key={tab.key}\n                to={`/projects/${projectId}/${tab.key}`}\n                className={`border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium ${\n                  active\n                    ? 'border-brand-600 text-brand-700'\n                    : 'border-transparent text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'\n                }`}\n              >\n                {tab.label}\n              </Link>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </nav>\n      <div className=\"min-h-0 flex-1\">\n        <Outlet />\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Now the pages: Login, Register, Projects, Gantt, Team, Calendar, Analysis. Plus PertDiagram, ResourceDialog, MiniCalendar.
> 
> Let me write PertDiagram first (used by AnalysisPage).
> 
> PertDiagram props: { network: PertNetwork }.
> Layout: nodes positioned by column and order within column.
> - columnWidth = 200, nodeWidth = 170, nodeHeight = 84, rowGap = 16.
> - Group nodes by column, order by es then name.
> - x = column * columnWidth
> - y = index in column * (nodeHeight + rowGap)
> - Total width/height computed.
> - Edges: from right edge of predecessor to left edge of successor (or elbow if overlapping columns).
> - Critical: red stroke.
> - Node content: name, durasi, ES/EF/LS/LF, slack, and te if available.
> - Zoom: scale state (0.6–1.4) applied via a wrapper div with transform scale; container scrollable.
> 
> Use a foreignObject? Simpler to draw with SVG text elements.
> 
> Node box: rect + texts:
> - name (truncate: slice to ~24 chars)
> - "Durasi: 3" 
> - "ES/EF: 0/2"
> - "LS/LF: 0/2"
> - "Slack: 0"
> 
> Test requirement: "diagram PERT menandai node kritis" → add data-testid={`pert-node-${id}`} and data-critical attribute, plus a red fill.

> Let me write it.

```json
{
  "path": "client/src/components/pert/PertDiagram.tsx",
  "instructions": "Scrollable and zoomable PERT activity-on-node diagram",
  "content": "import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport type { PertNetwork, PertNode } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { EmptyState } from '../ui/States';\n\nconst NODE_WIDTH = 176;\nconst NODE_HEIGHT = 92;\nconst COLUMN_GAP = 60;\nconst ROW_GAP = 18;\nconst PADDING = 24;\n\ninterface PositionedNode {\n  node: PertNode;\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n}\n\ninterface PertDiagramProps {\n  network: PertNetwork;\n}\n\nexport function PertDiagram({ network }: PertDiagramProps) {\n  const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);\n\n  const { nodes, width, height } = useMemo(() => {\n    const byColumn = new Map<number, PertNode[]>();\n    for (const node of network.nodes) {\n      const list = byColumn.get(node.column) ?? [];\n      list.push(node);\n      byColumn.set(node.column, list);\n    }\n    const positioned: PositionedNode[] = [];\n    let maxRows = 1;\n    for (const [column, list] of [...byColumn.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {\n      const ordered = [...list].sort((a, b) => a.es - b.es || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));\n      maxRows = Math.max(maxRows, ordered.length);\n      ordered.forEach((node, index) => {\n        positioned.push({\n          node,\n          x: PADDING + column * (NODE_WIDTH + COLUMN_GAP),\n          y: PADDING + index * (NODE_HEIGHT + ROW_GAP),\n        });\n      });\n    }\n    const columns = byColumn.size;\n    return {\n      nodes: positioned,\n      width: PADDING * 2 + columns * NODE_WIDTH + Math.max(0, columns - 1) * COLUMN_GAP,\n      height: PADDING * 2 + maxRows * NODE_HEIGHT + Math.max(0, maxRows - 1) * ROW_GAP,\n    };\n  }, [network]);\n\n  const positionById = useMemo(\n    () => new Map(nodes.map((item) => [item.node.id, item])),\n    [nodes],\n  );\n\n  if (network.nodes.length === 0) {\n    return <EmptyState title=\"Belum ada tugas daun\" description=\"Tambahkan tugas untuk melihat diagram PERT.\" />;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600\">\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1\" onClick={() => setScale((s) => Math.max(0.5, s - 0.1))}>\n          Perkecil\n        </button>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1\" onClick={() => setScale((s) => Math.min(1.6, s + 0.1))}>\n          Perbesar\n        </button>\n        <span>{Math.round(scale * 100)}%</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1\">\n          <span className=\"inline-block h-3 w-3 rounded-sm border border-red-600 bg-red-100\" /> Node kritis\n        </span>\n        {network.totalVariance !== null ? (\n          <span className=\"ml-auto\">\n            Total variansi jalur kritis: {network.totalVariance} · Simpangan baku: {network.stdDeviation}\n          </span>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"max-h-[520px] overflow-auto rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50\">\n        <svg\n          width={width * scale}\n          height={height * scale}\n          viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}\n          role=\"img\"\n          aria-label=\"Diagram PERT\"\n        >\n          <defs>\n            <marker id=\"pert-arrow\" viewBox=\"0 0 10 10\" refX={9} refY={5} markerWidth={6} markerHeight={6} orient=\"auto\">\n              <path d=\"M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z\" fill=\"#64748b\" />\n            </marker>\n            <marker id=\"pert-arrow-critical\" viewBox=\"0 0 10 10\" refX={9} refY={5} markerWidth={6} markerHeight={6} orient=\"auto\">\n              <path d=\"M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z\" fill=\"#dc2626\" />\n            </marker>\n          </defs>\n\n          {network.edges.map((edge, index) => {\n            const from = positionById.get(edge.from);\n            const to = positionById.get(edge.to);\n            if (!from || !to) return null;\n            const startX = from.x + NODE_WIDTH;\n            const startY = from.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const endX = to.x;\n            const endY = to.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const midX = (startX + endX) / 2;\n            const path = `M ${startX} ${startY} H ${midX} V ${endY} H ${endX}`;\n            return (\n              <path\n                key={`${edge.from}-${edge.to}-${index}`}\n                d={path}\n                fill=\"none\"\n                stroke={edge.critical ? '#dc2626' : '#94a3b8'}\n                strokeWidth={edge.critical ? 2 : 1.25}\n                markerEnd={edge.critical ? 'url(#pert-arrow-critical)' : 'url(#pert-arrow)'}\n                data-testid={`pert-edge-${edge.from}-${edge.to}`}\n              />\n            );\n          })}\n\n          {nodes.map(({ node, x, y }) => (\n            <g\n              key={node.id}\n              data-testid={`pert-node-${node.id}`}\n              data-critical={node.critical ? 'true' : 'false'}\n            >\n              <rect\n                x={x}\n                y={y}\n                width={NODE_WIDTH}\n                height={NODE_HEIGHT}\n                rx={6}\n                fill={node.critical ? '#fee2e2' : '#ffffff'}\n                stroke={node.critical ? '#dc2626' : '#cbd5e1'}\n                strokeWidth={node.critical ? 2 : 1}\n              />\n              <text x={x + 8} y={y + 16} fontSize={11} fontWeight={600} fill={node.critical ? '#991b1b' : '#0f172a'}>\n                {node.name.length > 24 ? `${node.name.slice(0, 23)}…` : node.name}\n              </text>\n              <text x={x + 8} y={y + 32} fontSize={10} fill=\"#475569\">\n                Durasi: {node.durationDays} hari\n              </text>\n              <text x={x + 8} y={y + 46} fontSize={10} fill=\"#475569\">\n                ES/EF: {node.es} / {node.ef}\n              </text>\n              <text x={x + 8} y={y + 60} fontSize={10} fill=\"#475569\">\n                LS/LF: {node.ls} / {node.lf}\n              </text>\n              <text x={x + 8} y={y + 74} fontSize={10} fill=\"#475569\">\n                Slack: {node.slack}\n              </text>\n              {node.expectedDays !== null ? (\n                <text x={x + 8} y={y + 87} fontSize={10} fill=\"#7c3aed\">\n                  te: {node.expectedDays} hari\n                </text>\n              ) : null}\n            </g>\n          ))}\n        </svg>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "import { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link, Navigate, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';\nimport { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';\nimport { loginSchema, type LoginInput } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';\nimport { Field } from '../components/ui/Field';\n\nexport function LoginPage() {\n  const { login, user } = useAuth();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const {\n    register,\n    handleSubmit,\n    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },\n  } = useForm<LoginInput>({\n    resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema),\n    defaultValues: { email: '', password: '' },\n  });\n\n  if (user) return <Navigate to=\"/projects\" replace />;\n\n  const onSubmit = async (values: LoginInput): Promise<void> => {\n    setServerError(null);\n    try {\n      await login(values.email, values.password);\n      navigate('/projects', { replace: true });\n    } catch (error) {\n      setServerError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal masuk.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto flex min-h-screen max-w-md flex-col justify-center px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">Masuk</h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500\">\n          Kelola proyek, jadwal, tim, dan analisis dalam satu tempat.\n        </p>\n        <form className=\"mt-4 space-y-4\" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>\n          <Field label=\"Email\" htmlFor=\"email\" error={errors.email?.message}>\n            <input id=\"email\" type=\"email\" className=\"input\" autoComplete=\"email\" {...register('email')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Kata sandi\" htmlFor=\"password\" error={errors.password?.message}>\n            <input\n              id=\"password\"\n              type=\"password\"\n              className=\"input\"\n              autoComplete=\"current-password\"\n              {...register('password')}\n            />\n          </Field>\n          {serverError ? (\n            <p className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700\" role=\"alert\">\n              {serverError}\n            </p>\n          ) : null}\n          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn-primary w-full\" disabled={isSubmitting}>\n            {isSubmitting ? 'Memproses…' : 'Masuk'}\n          </button>\n        </form>\n        <p className=\"mt-4 text-center text-sm text-slate-600\">\n          Belum punya akun?{' '}\n          <Link to=\"/register\" className=\"font-medium text-brand-700 hover:underline\">\n            Daftar\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n        <p className=\"mt-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-500\">\n          Akun demo: demo@example.com / Demo12345\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

error.message : 'Gagal mendaftar.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto flex min-h-screen max-w-md flex-col justify-center px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">Daftar akun</h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500\">Kata sandi minimal 8 karakter.</p>\n        <form className=\"mt-4 space-y-4\" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>\n          <Field label=\"Nama\" htmlFor=\"name\" error={errors.name?.message}>\n            <input id=\"name\" className=\"input\" autoComplete=\"name\" {...register('name')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Email\" htmlFor=\"email\" error={errors.email?.message}>\n            <input id=\"email\" type=\"email\" className=\"input\" autoComplete=\"email\" {...register('email')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Kata sandi\" htmlFor=\"password\" error={errors.password?.message}>\n            <input\n              id=\"password\"\n              type=\"password\"\n              className=\"input\"\n              autoComplete=\"new-password\"\n              {...register('password')}\n            />\n          </Field>\n          {serverError ? (\n            <p className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700\" role=\"alert\">\n              {serverError}\n            </p>\n          ) : null}\n          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn-primary w-full\" disabled={isSubmitting}>\n            {isSubmitting ? 'Memproses…' : 'Daftar'}\n          </button>\n        </form>\n        <p className=\"mt-4 text-center text-sm text-slate-600\">\n          Sudah punya akun?{' '}\n          <Link to=\"/login\" className=\"font-medium text-brand-700 hover:underline\">\n            Masuk\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

null,\n        startDate: values.startDate,\n      });\n      toast.success('Proyek berhasil dibuat.');\n      setDialogOpen(false);\n      reset();\n      navigate(`/projects/${response.project.id}/gantt`);\n    } catch (submitError) {\n      toast.error(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Gagal membuat proyek.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const confirmDelete = async (): Promise<void> => {\n    if (!deleteTarget) return;\n    try {\n      await deleteProject.mutateAsync(deleteTarget.id);\n      toast.success('Proyek dihapus.');\n      setDeleteTarget(null);\n    } catch (deleteError) {\n      toast.error(deleteError instanceof Error ? deleteError.message : 'Gagal menghapus proyek.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-screen-lg px-4 py-6\">\n      <div className=\"mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3\">\n        <div>\n          <h1 className=\"text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900\">Proyek saya</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-500\">Pilih proyek untuk membuka Gantt, tim, kalender, dan analisis.</p>\n        </div>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-primary\" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(true)}>\n          + Proyek baru\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {isLoading ? <LoadingState /> : null}\n      {error ? (\n        <ErrorState\n          message={error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal memuat proyek.'}\n          onRetry={() => void refetch()}\n        />\n      ) : null}\n\n      {data && data.projects.length === 0 ? (\n        <EmptyState\n          title=\"Belum ada proyek\"\n          description=\"Buat proyek pertama Anda untuk mulai menyusun jadwal.\"\n          action={\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-primary mt-2\" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(true)}>\n              + Proyek baru\n            </button>\n          }\n        />\n      ) : null}\n\n      <div className=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2\">\n        {(data?.projects ?? []).map((project) => (\n          <div key={project.id} className=\"card flex flex-col justify-between\">\n            <div>\n              <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900\">{project.name}</h2>\n              <p className=\"mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-500\">\n                {project.description ?? 'Tanpa deskripsi'}\n              </p>\n              <p className=\"mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500\">Mulai {formatDisplayDate(project.startDate)}</p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mt-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <Link to={`/projects/${project.id}/gantt`} className=\"btn-primary px-3 py-1 text-xs\">\n                Buka\n              </Link>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"btn-danger px-3 py-1 text-xs\"\n                onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(project)}\n              >\n                Hapus\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <Modal\n        open={dialogOpen}\n        title=\"Proyek baru\"\n        onClose={() => setDialogOpen(false)}\n        footer={\n          <>\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary\" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(false)}>\n              Batal\n            </button>\n            <button type=\"submit\" form=\"project-form\" className=\"btn-primary\" disabled={createProject.isPending}>\n              {createProject.isPending ? 'Menyimpan…' : 'Simpan'}\n            </button>\n          </>\n        }\n      >\n        <form id=\"project-form\" className=\"space-y-4\" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>\n          <Field label=\"Nama proyek\" htmlFor=\"project-name\" error={errors.name?.message}>\n            <input id=\"project-name\" className=\"input\" {...register('name')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Deskripsi\" htmlFor=\"project-description\" error={errors.description?.message}>\n            <textarea id=\"project-description\" rows={3} className=\"input\" {...register('description')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Tanggal mulai proyek\" htmlFor=\"project-start\" error={errors.startDate?.message}>\n            <input id=\"project-start\" type=\"date\" className=\"input\" {...register('startDate')} />\n          </Field>\n        </form>\n      </Modal>\n\n      <ConfirmDialog\n        open={deleteTarget !== null}\n        title=\"Hapus proyek\"\n        message={`Proyek \"${deleteTarget?.name ?? ''}\" beserta seluruh tugas, dependensi, dan timnya akan dihapus permanen.`}\n        busy={deleteProject.isPending}\n        onCancel={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}\n        onConfirm={() => void confirmDelete()}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

[]).map((resource) => [resource.id, resource.name]));\n    const assignments = data?.assignments ?? [];\n    return (taskId: string): string => {\n      const names = assignments\n        .filter((assignment) => assignment.taskId === taskId)\n        .map(\n          (assignment) =>\n            `${resources.get(assignment.resourceId) ?? 'Anggota'} (${assignment.allocationPercent}%)`,\n        );\n      return names.length > 0 ? names.join(', ') : 'Belum ada anggota';\n    };\n  }, [data]);\n\n  const syncRelations = async (taskId: string, values: TaskFormValues): Promise<void> => {\n    const existingDependencies = (data?.dependencies ?? []).filter(\n      (dependency) => dependency.successorId === taskId,\n    );\n    const incoming = values.predecessors.filter((row) => row.predecessorId.length > 0);\n\n    for (const dependency of existingDependencies) {\n      if (!incoming.some((row) => row.predecessorId === dependency.predecessorId)) {\n        await actions.deleteDependency.mutateAsync(dependency.id);\n      }\n    }\n    for (const row of incoming) {\n      const existing = existingDependencies.find(\n        (dependency) => dependency.predecessorId === row.predecessorId,\n      );\n      if (!existing) {\n        await actions.addDependency.mutateAsync({\n          taskId,\n          input: { predecessorId: row.predecessorId, type: row.type, lagDays: row.lagDays },\n        });\n      } else if (existing.type !== row.type || existing.lagDays !== row.lagDays) {\n        await actions.updateDependency.mutateAsync({\n          dependencyId: existing.id,\n          input: { type: row.type, lagDays: row.lagDays },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    const existingAssignments = (data?.assignments ?? []).filter(\n      (assignment) => assignment.taskId === taskId,\n    );\n    const incomingAssignees = values.assignees.filter((row) => row.resourceId.length > 0);\n\n    for (const assignment of existingAssignments) {\n      if (!incomingAssignees.some((row) => row.resourceId === assignment.resourceId)) {\n        await actions.deleteAssignment.mutateAsync(assignment.id);\n      }\n    }\n    for (const row of incomingAssignees) {\n      const existing = existingAssignments.find(\n        (assignment) => assignment.resourceId === row.resourceId,\n      );\n      if (!existing) {\n        await actions.addAssignment.mutateAsync({\n          taskId,\n          input: { resourceId: row.resourceId, allocationPercent: row.allocationPercent },\n        });\n      } else if (existing.allocationPercent !== row.allocationPercent) {\n        await actions.updateAssignment.mutateAsync({\n          assignmentId: existing.id,\n          input: { allocationPercent: row.allocationPercent },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (values: TaskFormValues): Promise<void> => {\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const isParent = dialog.task ? isParentTask(tasks, dialog.task.id) : false;\n      const basePayload = {\n        name: values.name,\n        description: values.description?.trim() ? values.description : null,\n        priority: values.priority,\n        color: values.color,\n      };\n      const fullPayload = {\n        ...basePayload,\n        durationDays: values.durationDays,\n        startDate: values.startDate,\n        progress: values.progress,\n        optimisticDays: values.optimisticDays ?? null,\n        pessimisticDays: values.pessimisticDays ?? null,\n      };\n\n      let taskId = '';\n      if (dialog.mode === 'create') {\n        const response = await actions.createTask.mutateAsync({\n          ...fullPayload,\n          parentId: values.parentId && values.parentId.length > 0 ? values.parentId : null,\n        });\n        taskId = response.task?.id ?? '';\n        toast.success('Tugas ditambahkan.');\n      } else if (dialog.task) {\n        taskId = dialog.task.id;\n        await actions.updateTask.mutateAsync({\n          taskId,\n          input: isParent ? basePayload : fullPayload,\n        });\n        const newParentId = values.parentId && values.parentId.length > 0 ? values.parentId : null;\n        if ((dialog.task.parentId ?? null) !== newParentId) {\n          await actions.moveTask.mutateAsync({ taskId, parentId: newParentId, position: 0 });\n        }\n        toast.success('Tugas diperbarui.');\n      }\n\n      await syncRelations(taskId, values);\n      setDialog((current) => ({ ...current, open: false }));\n      setSelectedId(taskId);\n    } catch (submitError) {\n      toast.error(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Gagal menyimpan tugas.');\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleDragTask = async (task: TaskDto, startDate: string, clamped: boolean): Promise<void> => {\n    try {\n      await actions.updateTask.mutateAsync({ taskId: task.id, input: { startDate } });\n      if (clamped) {\n        toast.info(`Bar \"${task.name}\" dikembalikan ke batas predecessor karena lebih awal.`);\n      }\n    } catch (dragError) {\n      toast.error(dragError instanceof Error ? dragError.message : 'Gagal memindahkan tugas.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleResizeTask = async (task: TaskDto, durationDays: number): Promise<void> => {\n    try {\n      await actions.updateTask.mutateAsync({ taskId: task.id, input: { durationDays } });\n    } catch (resizeError) {\n      toast.error(resizeError instanceof Error ? resizeError.message : 'Gagal mengubah durasi.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const confirmDelete = async (): Promise<void> => {\n    if (!deleteTarget) return;\n    try {\n      await actions.deleteTask.mutateAsync(deleteTarget.id);\n      toast.success('Tugas dihapus.');\n      if (selectedId === deleteTarget.id) setSelectedId(null);\n      setDeleteTarget(null);\n    } catch (deleteError) {\n      toast.error(deleteError instanceof Error ? deleteError.message : 'Gagal menghapus tugas.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (isLoading) return <LoadingState label=\"Memuat proyek…\" />;\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"p-4\">\n        <ErrorState\n          message={error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal memuat proyek.'}\n          onRetry={() => void refetch()}\n        />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (!data) return <LoadingState />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">{data.project.name}</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">\n            {tasks.length} tugas · akhir pekan: {data.project.weekendDays.split(',').length} hari ·\n            mulai {data.project.startDate}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"btn-primary\"\n            onClick={() => setDialog({ open: true, mode: 'create', task: null, parentId: null })}\n          >\n            + Tugas baru\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"btn-secondary\"\n            disabled={!selectedTask}\n            onClick={() =>\n              setDialog({ open: true, mode: 'create', task: null, parentId: selectedId })\n            }\n          >\n            + Sub-tugas\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"btn-secondary\"\n            disabled={!selectedTask}\n            onClick={() =>\n              setDialog({ open: true, mode: 'edit', task: selectedTask, parentId: null })\n            }\n          >\n            Ubah\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"btn-danger\"\n            disabled={!selectedTask}\n            onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(selectedTask)}\n          >\n            Hapus\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"min-h-0 flex-1\">\n        <GanttChart\n          tasks={tasks}\n          dependencies={data.dependencies}\n          holidays={data.holidays}\n          weekendDays={data.project.weekendDays}\n          projectStart={data.project.startDate}\n          showCriticalPath={showCriticalPath}\n          onToggleCriticalPath={() => setShowCriticalPath((value) => !value)}\n          assigneeLabel={assigneeLabel}\n          onSelectTask={(task) => setSelectedId(task.id)}\n          onOpenTask={(task) =>\n            setDialog({ open: true, mode: 'edit', task, parentId: null })\n          }\n          onDragTask={(task, startDate, clamped) => void handleDragTask(task, startDate, clamped)}\n          onResizeTask={(task, durationDays) => void handleResizeTask(task, {
  "path": "client/src/components/team/ResourceDialog.tsx",
  "instructions": "Resource create/edit dialog",
  "content": "import { useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';\nimport { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';\nimport { resourceCreateSchema, type ResourceCreateInput, type ResourceDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';\nimport { Field } from '../ui/Field';\n\ninterface ResourceDialogProps {\n  open: boolean;\n  resource: ResourceDto | null;\n  submitting: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  onSubmit: (values: ResourceCreateInput) => void;\n}\n\nexport function ResourceDialog({ open, resource, submitting, onClose, onSubmit }: ResourceDialogProps) {\n  const {\n    register,\n    handleSubmit,\n    reset,\n    formState: { errors },\n  } = useForm<ResourceCreateInput>({\n    resolver: zodResolver(resourceCreateSchema),\n    defaultValues: { name: '', email: '', role: '', salary: 0 },\n  });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return;\n    reset({\n      name: resource?.name ?? '',\n      email: resource?.email ?? '',\n      role: resource?.role ?? '',\n      salary: resource?.salary ?? 0,\n    });\n  }, [open, resource, reset]);\n\n  return (\n    <Modal\n      open={open}\n      title={resource ? `Ubah anggota: ${resource.name}` : 'Anggota tim baru'}\n      onClose={onClose}\n      footer={\n        <>\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary\" onClick={onClose}>\n            Batal\n          </button>\n          <button type=\"submit\" form=\"resource-form\" className=\"btn-primary\" disabled={submitting}>\n            {submitting ? 'Menyimpan…' : 'Simpan'}\n          </button>\n        </>\n      }\n    >\n      <form id=\"resource-form\" className=\"space-y-4\" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate>\n        <Field label=\"Nama\" htmlFor=\"resource-name\" error={errors.name?.message}>\n          <input id=\"resource-name\" className=\"input\" {...register('name')} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Email\" htmlFor=\"resource-email\" error={errors.email?.message}>\n          <input id=\"resource-email\" type=\"email\" className=\"input\" {...register('email')} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Peran\" htmlFor=\"resource-role\" error={errors.role?.message}>\n          <input id=\"resource-role\" className=\"input\" {...register('role')} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field\n          label=\"Gaji per bulan (rupiah)\"\n          htmlFor=\"resource-salary\"\n          error={errors.salary?.message}\n          hint=\"Tarif harian = gaji / 22 hari kerja.\"\n        >\n          <input\n            id=\"resource-salary\"\n            type=\"number\"\n            min={0}\n            step={100000}\n            className=\"input\"\n            {...register('salary', { valueAsNumber: true })}\n          />\n        </Field>\n      </form>\n    </Modal>\n  );\n}\n"
}
