{
  "path": "client/src/pages/TeamPage.tsx",
  "instructions": "Team page with resources, allocations, costs and overallocation warnings",
  "content": "import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport type { ResourceCreateInput, ResourceDto } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { ResourceDialog } from '../components/team/ResourceDialog';\nimport { ConfirmDialog } from '../components/ui/Field';\nimport { EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingState } from '../components/ui/States';\nimport { useToast } from '../components/ui/Toast';\nimport { useAnalysis, useProjectActions, useProjectSnapshot } from '../hooks/useProjectData';\nimport { formatDisplayDate, formatIDR } from '../lib/format';\n\nexport function TeamPage() {\n  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();\n  const toast = useToast();\n  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useProjectSnapshot(projectId);\n  const analysis = useAnalysis(projectId, true);\n  const actions = useProjectActions(projectId);\n\n  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<{ open: boolean; resource: ResourceDto | null }>({\n    open: false,\n    resource: null,\n  });\n  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<ResourceDto | null>(null);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  const costByResource = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const item of analysis.data?.costs.resources ?? []) {\n      map.set(item.resourceId, item.totalCost);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [analysis.data]);\n\n  const tasksByResource = useMemo(() => {\n    const taskById = new Map((data?.tasks ?? []).map((task) => [task.id, task]));\n    const map = new Map<string, Array<{ id: string; name: string; percent: number }>>();\n    for (const assignment of data?.assignments ?? []) {\n      const task = taskById.get(assignment.taskId);\n      if (!task) continue;\n      const list = map.get(assignment.resourceId) ?? [];\n      list.push({ id: task.id, name: task.name, percent: assignment.allocationPercent });\n      map.set(assignment.resourceId, list);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [data]);\n\n  const warningsByResource = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, Array<{ date: string; totalPercent: number }>>();\n    for (const warning of analysis.data?.overallocations ?? []) {\n      const list = map.get(warning.resourceId) ?? [];\n      list.push({ date: warning.date, totalPercent: warning.totalPercent });\n      map.set(warning.resourceId, list);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [analysis.data]);\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (values: ResourceCreateInput): Promise<void> => {\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      if (dialog.resource) {\n        await actions.updateResource.mutateAsync({ resourceId: dialog.resource.id, input: values });\n        toast.success('Data anggota diperbarui.');\n      } else {\n        await actions.createResource.mutateAsync(values);\n        toast.success('Anggota tim ditambahkan.');\n      }\n      setDialog({ open: false, resource: null });\n    } catch (submitError) {\n      toast.error(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Gagal menyimpan anggota.');\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const confirmDelete = async (): Promise<void> => {\n    if (!deleteTarget) return;\n    try {\n      await actions.deleteResource.mutateAsync(deleteTarget.id);\n      toast.success('Anggota dihapus.');\n      setDeleteTarget(null);\n    } catch (deleteError) {\n      toast.error(deleteError instanceof Error ? deleteError.message : 'Gagal menghapus anggota.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (isLoading) return <LoadingState label=\"Memuat tim…\" />;\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"p-4\">\n        <ErrorState\n          message={error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal memuat tim.'}\n          onRetry={() => void refetch()}\n        />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (!data) return <LoadingState />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-screen-xl space-y-4 px-4 py-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n        <div>\n          <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">Tim proyek</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">\n            Total biaya proyek: {formatIDR(analysis.data?.costs.total ?? 0)}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"btn-primary\"\n          onClick={() => setDialog({ open: true, resource: null })}\n        >\n          + Anggota baru\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {data.resources.length === 0 ? (\n        <EmptyState\n          title=\"Belum ada anggota tim\"\n          description=\"Tambahkan anggota untuk menghitung biaya dan beban kerja.\"\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white\">\n          <table className=\"min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-200 text-sm\">\n            <thead className=\"bg-slate-50 text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500\">\n              <tr>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Nama</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Peran</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Email</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Gaji / bulan</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Tugas dialokasikan</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Total biaya</th>\n                <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Aksi</th>\n              </tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody className=\"divide-y divide-slate-100\">\n              {data.resources.map((resource) => {\n                const warnings = warningsByResource.get(resource.id) ?? [];\n                return (\n                  <tr key={resource.id} data-testid={`team-row-${resource.id}`}>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 font-medium text-slate-800\">{resource.name}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-600\">{resource.role}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-600\">{resource.email}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{formatIDR(resource.salary)}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-600\">\n                      {(tasksByResource.get(resource.id) ?? []).length === 0 ? (\n                        <span className=\"text-xs text-slate-400\">Belum dialokasikan</span>\n                      ) : (\n                        <ul className=\"space-y-0.5 text-xs\">\n                          {(tasksByResource.get(resource.id) ?? []).map((task) => (\n                            <li key={task.id}>\n                              {task.name} — {task.percent}%\n                            </li>\n                          ))}\n                        </ul>\n                      )}\n                      {warnings.length > 0 ? (\n                        <p className=\"mt-1 rounded bg-amber-50 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-amber-700\">\n                          ⚠ Over-alokasi: {warnings.length} hari kerja melebihi 100% (contoh{' '}\n                          {formatDisplayDate(warnings[0].date)} — {warnings[0].totalPercent}%).\n                        </p>\n                      ) : null}\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right font-medium text-slate-800\">\n                      {formatIDR(costByResource.get(resource.id) ?? 0)}\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">\n                      <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-1\">\n                        <button\n                          type=\"button\"\n                          className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs\"\n                          onClick={() => setDialog({ open: true, resource })}\n                        >\n                          Ubah\n                        </button>\n                        <button\n                          type=\"button\"\n                          className=\"btn-danger px-2 py-1 text-xs\"\n                          onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(resource)}\n                        >\n                          Hapus\n                        </button>\n                      </div>\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                );\n              })}\n            </tbody>\n          </table>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {(analysis.data?.overallocations.length ?? 0) > 0 ? (\n        <div className=\"card border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-sm text-amber-800\">\n          <p className=\"font-medium\">Peringatan over-alokasi</p>\n          <ul className=\"mt-1 list-inside list-disc space-y-0.5 text-xs\">\n            {(analysis.data?.overallocations ?? []).slice(0, 12).map((warning) => (\n              <li key={`${warning.resourceId}-${warning.date}`}>\n                {data.resources.find((resource) => resource.id === warning.resourceId)?.name ?? 'Anggota'} ·{' '}\n                {formatDisplayDate(warning.date)} · total {warning.totalPercent}%\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <ResourceDialog\n        open={dialog.open}\n        resource={dialog.resource}\n        submitting={submitting}\n        onClose={() => setDialog({ open: false, resource: null })}\n        onSubmit={(values) => void handleSubmit(values)}\n      />\n\n      <ConfirmDialog\n        open={deleteTarget !== null}\n        title=\"Hapus anggota\"\n        message={`Anggota \"${deleteTarget?.name ?? ''}\" beserta seluruh alokasinya akan dihapus.`}\n        busy={actions.deleteResource.isPending}\n        onCancel={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}\n        onConfirm={() => void confirmDelete()}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> ```
> Grid: compute first day of month, number of days, offset by weekday (0=Sunday).

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "client/src/components/calendar/MiniCalendar.tsx",
  "instructions": "Mini month calendar marking non-working days",
  "content": "import { addDays, formatDate, getDayOfWeek, toUTCDate } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { WEEKDAY_LABELS } from '../../lib/format';\n\ninterface MiniCalendarProps {\n  month: string;\n  weekendDays: number[];\n  holidays: string[];\n  onChangeMonth: (month: string) => void;\n}\n\nexport function MiniCalendar({ month, weekendDays, holidays, onChangeMonth }: MiniCalendarProps) {\n  const first = toUTCDate(month);\n  const monthLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {\n    month: 'long',\n    year: 'numeric',\n    timeZone: 'UTC',\n  }).format(first);\n  const offset = getDayOfWeek(first);\n  const daysInMonth = new Date(Date.UTC(first.getUTCFullYear(), first.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();\n  const holidaySet = new Set(holidays);\n\n  const shift = (delta: number): void => {\n    const next = new Date(Date.UTC(first.getUTCFullYear(), first.getUTCMonth() + delta, 1));\n    onChangeMonth(formatDate(next));\n  };\n\n  const cells: Array<{ key: string; date: string | null; day: number }> = [];\n  for (let index = 0; index < offset; index += 1) {\n    cells.push({ key: `empty-${index}`, date: null, day: 0 });\n  }\n  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day += 1) {\n    const date = formatDate(addDays(first, day - 1));\n    cells.push({ key: date, date, day });\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3\">\n      <div className=\"mb-2 flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs\" onClick={() => shift(-1)}>\n          ‹\n        </button>\n        <span className=\"text-sm font-medium capitalize text-slate-800\">{monthLabel}</span>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs\" onClick={() => shift(1)}>\n          ›\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 text-center text-[10px] text-slate-500\">\n        {WEEKDAY_LABELS.map((label) => (\n          <span key={label}>{label}</span>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"mt-1 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1\">\n        {cells.map((cell) => {\n          if (!cell.date) return <span key={cell.key} />;\n          const date = toUTCDate(cell.date);\n          const isHoliday = holidaySet.has(cell.date);\n          const isWeekend = weekendDays.includes(getDayOfWeek(date));\n          const className = isHoliday\n            ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 font-semibold'\n            : isWeekend\n              ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'\n              : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border border-slate-200';\n          return (\n            <span\n              key={cell.key}\n              title={isHoliday ? 'Hari libur' : isWeekend ? 'Akhir pekan' : 'Hari kerja'}\n              data-testid={`mini-calendar-${cell.date}`}\n              className={`flex h-7 items-center justify-center rounded text-xs ${className}`}\n            >\n              {cell.day}\n            </span>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"mt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500\">\n        Abu-abu = akhir pekan, merah = hari libur.\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/pages/CalendarPage.tsx",
  "instructions": "Calendar page with weekend checkboxes and holiday management",
  "content": "import { useState } from 'react';\nimport { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';\nimport { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';\nimport { holidayCreateSchema, type HolidayCreateInput } from '@gantt/shared';\nimport { MiniCalendar } from '../components/calendar/MiniCalendar';\nimport { ConfirmDialog } from '../components/ui/Field';\nimport { Field } from '../components/ui/Field';\nimport { ErrorState, LoadingState } from '../components/ui/States';\nimport { useToast } from '../components/ui/Toast';\nimport { useProjectActions, useProjectSnapshot } from '../hooks/useProjectData';\nimport { WEEKDAY_LABELS, formatDisplayDate } from '../lib/format';\n\nexport function CalendarPage() {\n  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();\n  const toast = useToast();\n  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useProjectSnapshot(projectId);\n  const actions = useProjectActions(projectId);\n  const [month, setMonth] = useState<string>('');\n  const [deleteHolidayId, setDeleteHolidayId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const {\n    register,\n    handleSubmit,\n    reset,\n    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },\n  } = useForm<HolidayCreateInput>({\n    resolver: zodResolver(holidayCreateSchema),\n    defaultValues: { date: '', name: '' },\n  });\n\n  if (isLoading) return <LoadingState label=\"Memuat kalender…\" />;\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"p-4\">\n        <ErrorState\n          message={error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal memuat kalender.'}\n          onRetry={() => void refetch()}\n        />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (!data) return <LoadingState />;\n\n  const weekendDays = data.project.weekendDays\n    .split(',')\n    .map((part) => Number(part.trim()))\n    .filter((day) => Number.isInteger(day));\n  const currentMonth = month.length > 0 ? month : `${data.project.startDate.slice(0, 7)}-01`;\n  const holidays = data.holidays.map((holiday) => holiday.date);\n\n  const toggleWeekend = async (day: number): Promise<void> => {\n    const next = weekendDays.includes(day)\n      ? weekendDays.filter((item) => item !== day)\n      : [...weekendDays, day].sort((a, b) => a - b);\n    if (next.length === 0) {\n      toast.error('Minimal satu hari harus tetap dihitung sebagai hari kerja.');\n      return;\n    }\n    if (next.length >= 7) {\n      toast.error('Akhir pekan tidak boleh berisi ketujuh hari.');\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await actions.updateCalendar.mutateAsync(next);\n      toast.success('Jadwal diperbarui.');\n    } catch (updateError) {\n      toast.error(updateError instanceof Error ? updateError.message : 'Gagal memperbarui kalender.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const onSubmitHoliday = async (values: HolidayCreateInput): Promise<void> => {\n    try {\n      await actions.addHoliday.mutateAsync(values);\n      toast.success('Jadwal diperbarui.');\n      reset({ date: '', name: '' });\n    } catch (submitError) {\n      toast.error(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Gagal menambah hari libur.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const confirmDeleteHoliday = async (): Promise<void> => {\n    if (!deleteHolidayId) return;\n    try {\n      await actions.deleteHoliday.mutateAsync(deleteHolidayId);\n      toast.success('Jadwal diperbarui.');\n      setDeleteHolidayId(null);\n    } catch (deleteError) {\n      toast.error(deleteError instanceof Error ? deleteError.message : 'Gagal menghapus hari libur.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-screen-xl space-y-4 px-4 py-4\">\n      <div>\n        <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">Kalender proyek</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">\n          Durasi tugas hanya menghitung hari kerja. Perubahan di halaman ini langsung menggeser jadwal.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2\">\n        <section className=\"card\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Akhir pekan</h2>\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500\">\n            Centang hari yang tidak dihitung sebagai hari kerja.\n          </p>\n          <div className=\"mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4\">\n            {WEEKDAY_LABELS.map((label, day) => (\n              <label\n                key={label}\n                className=\"flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-slate-200 px-2 py-1.5 text-sm\"\n              >\n                <input\n                  type=\"checkbox\"\n                  checked={weekendDays.includes(day)}\n                  disabled={actions.updateCalendar.isPending}\n                  onChange={() => void toggleWeekend(day)}\n                />\n                {label}\n              </label>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500\">\n            Akhir pekan aktif: {weekendDays.map((day) => WEEKDAY_LABELS[day]).join(', ') || 'tidak ada'}\n          </p>\n        </section>\n\n        <MiniCalendar\n          month={currentMonth}\n          weekendDays={weekendDays}\n          holidays={holidays}\n          onChangeMonth={setMonth}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      <section className=\"card\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Hari libur khusus</h2>\n        <form\n          className=\"mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[180px_1fr_auto]\"\n          onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmitHoliday)}\n          noValidate\n        >\n          <Field label=\"Tanggal\" htmlFor=\"holiday-date\" error={errors.date?.message}>\n            <input id=\"holiday-date\" type=\"date\" className=\"input\" {...register('date')} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Nama hari libur\" htmlFor=\"holiday-name\" error={errors.name?.message}>\n            <input id=\"holiday-name\" className=\"input\" {...register('name')} />\n          </Field>\n          <div className=\"flex items-end\">\n            <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn-primary\" disabled={isSubmitting}>\n              {isSubmitting ? 'Menyimpan…' : '+ Tambah'}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n\n        {data.holidays.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500\">Belum ada hari libur khusus.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"mt-3 divide-y divide-slate-100\">\n            {data.holidays.map((holiday) => (\n              <li key={holiday.id} className=\"flex items-center justify-between py-2 text-sm\">\n                <span className=\"text-slate-700\">\n                  {formatDisplayDate(holiday.date)} — {holiday.name}\n                </span>\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  className=\"btn-danger px-2 py-1 text-xs\"\n                  onClick={() => setDeleteHolidayId(holiday.id)}\n                >\n                  Hapus\n                </button>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      <ConfirmDialog\n        open={deleteHolidayId !== null}\n        title=\"Hapus hari libur\"\n        message=\"Hari ini akan kembali dihitung sebagai hari kerja dan jadwal bergeser otomatis.\"\n        busy={actions.deleteHoliday.isPending}\n        onCancel={() => setDeleteHolidayId(null)}\n        onConfirm={() => void confirmDeleteHoliday()}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "client/src/pages/AnalysisPage.tsx",
  "instructions": "Analysis page with summary, CPM table, critical paths and PERT diagram",
  "content": "import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { PertDiagram } from '../components/pert/PertDiagram';\nimport { EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingState } from '../components/ui/States';\nimport { useAnalysis } from '../hooks/useProjectData';\nimport { formatDisplayDate, formatIDR } from '../lib/format';\n\nexport function AnalysisPage() {\n  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();\n  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useAnalysis(projectId);\n\n  if (isLoading) return <LoadingState label=\"Menghitung analisis…\" />;\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"p-4\">\n        <ErrorState\n          message={error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal memuat analisis.'}\n          onRetry={() => void refetch()}\n        />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (!data) return <LoadingState />;\n\n  const summaryCards = [\n    { label: 'Tanggal mulai', value: formatDisplayDate(data.summary.startDate) },\n    { label: 'Tanggal selesai', value: formatDisplayDate(data.summary.endDate) },\n    { label: 'Total hari kerja', value: `${data.summary.totalWorkingDays} hari` },\n    { label: 'Total biaya', value: formatIDR(data.summary.totalCost) },\n    { label: 'Tugas kritis', value: `${data.summary.criticalTaskCount} dari ${data.summary.totalTasks}` },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-screen-xl space-y-5 px-4 py-4\">\n      <div>\n        <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900\">Analisis proyek</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">\n          Critical Path Method dan diagram PERT dihitung dari tugas daun yang sudah dijadwalkan.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5\">\n        {summaryCards.map((card) => (\n          <div key={card.label} className=\"card\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">{card.label}</p>\n            <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900\">{card.value}</p>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <section className=\"card\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Jalur kritis</h2>\n        {data.criticalPathNames.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500\">Tidak ada jalur kritis (belum ada tugas daun).</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700\">\n            {data.criticalPathNames.map((path, index) => (\n              <li key={path.join('-')} data-testid={`critical-path-${index}`}>\n                {path.join(' → ')}\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      <section className=\"card\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Tabel CPM</h2>\n        {data.cpm.length === 0 ? (\n          <EmptyState title=\"Belum ada tugas daun\" description=\"Tambahkan tugas pada halaman Gantt.\" />\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"mt-3 overflow-x-auto\">\n            <table className=\"min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-200 text-sm\">\n              <thead className=\"bg-slate-50 text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500\">\n                <tr>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Tugas</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Durasi</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">ES</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">EF</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">LS</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">LF</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Float</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Kritis</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody className=\"divide-y divide-slate-100\">\n                {data.cpm.map((task) => (\n                  <tr key={task.id} data-testid={`cpm-row-${task.id}`} data-critical={task.critical ? 'true' : 'false'}>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-800\">{task.name}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.durationDays}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.es}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.ef}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.ls}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.lf}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">{task.totalFloat}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2\">\n                      {task.critical ? (\n                        <span className=\"badge bg-red-100 text-red-700\">Kritis</span>\n                      ) : (\n                        <span className=\"badge bg-slate-100 text-slate-600\">Tidak</span>\n                      )}\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      <section className=\"card\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Diagram PERT</h2>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500\">\n          Kolom menunjukkan kedalaman jalur terpanjang. Node dan panah merah berada di jalur kritis.\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"mt-3\">\n          <PertDiagram network={data.pert} />\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <section className=\"card\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">Beban dan biaya per anggota</h2>\n        {data.costs.resources.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500\">Belum ada anggota tim dengan alokasi.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"mt-3 overflow-x-auto\">\n            <table className=\"min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-200 text-sm\">\n              <thead className=\"bg-slate-50 text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500\">\n                <tr>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Anggota</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2\">Peran</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Hari kerja dialokasikan</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right\">Total biaya</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody className=\"divide-y divide-slate-100\">\n                {data.costs.resources.map((cost) => (\n                  <tr key={cost.resourceId}>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-800\">{cost.name}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-slate-600\">{cost.role}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-600\">\n                      {cost.totalWorkingDays.toFixed(1)}\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-right font-medium text-slate-800\">\n                      {formatIDR(cost.totalCost)}\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        )}\n        {data.overallocations.length > 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-3 rounded-md bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-800\">\n            {data.overallocations.length} hari kerja memiliki alokasi melebihi 100%. Lihat detail di\n            halaman Tim.\n          </p>\n        ) : null}\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

'./pages/AnalysisPage';\nimport { CalendarPage } from './pages/CalendarPage';\nimport { GanttPage } from './pages/GanttPage';\nimport { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';\nimport { ProjectsPage } from './pages/ProjectsPage';\nimport { RegisterPage } from './pages/RegisterPage';\nimport { TeamPage } from './pages/TeamPage';\n\nconst queryClient = new QueryClient({\n  defaultOptions: {\n    queries: {\n      retry: 1,\n      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,\n      staleTime: 5_000,\n    },\n  },\n});\n\nexport function App() {\n  return (\n    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>\n      <ToastProvider>\n        <AuthProvider>\n          <BrowserRouter>\n            <Routes>\n              <Route path=\"/login\" element={<LoginPage />} />\n              <Route path=\"/register\" element={<RegisterPage />} />\n              <Route element={<ProtectedLayout />}>\n                <Route element={<AppLayout />}>\n                  <Route path=\"/projects\" element={<ProjectsPage />} />\n                  <Route path=\"/projects/:projectId\" element={<ProjectLayout />}>\n                    <Route path=\"gantt\" element={<GanttPage />} />\n                    <Route path=\"team\" element={<TeamPage />} />\n                    <Route path=\"calendar\" element={<CalendarPage />} />\n                    <Route path=\"analysis\" element={<AnalysisPage />} />\n                  </Route>\n                </Route>\n              </Route>\n              <Route path=\"*\" element={<Navigate to=\"/projects\" replace />} />\n            </Routes>\n          </BrowserRouter>\n        </AuthProvider>\n      </ToastProvider>\n    </QueryClientProvider>\n  );\n}\n"
}

dependencies={snapshot.dependencies}\n      holidays={snapshot.holidays}\n      weekendDays={snapshot.project.weekendDays}\n      projectStart={snapshot.project.startDate}\n      showCriticalPath={showCriticalPath}\n      onToggleCriticalPath={vi.fn()}\n      assigneeLabel={() => 'Andi Pratama (100%)'}\n      onSelectTask={vi.fn()}\n      onOpenTask={vi.fn()}\n      onDragTask={vi.fn()}\n      onResizeTask={vi.fn()}\n    />,\n  );\n}\n\ndescribe('GanttChart', () => {\n  it('menampilkan bar tugas daun dan bar pengganti untuk tugas induk', () => {\n    renderChart();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-1.1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-5.2')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-g3')).toHaveAttribute('data-parent', 'true');\n    expect(screen.getAllByTestId(/^gantt-bar-/)).toHaveLength(snapshot.tasks.length);\n  });\n\n  it('menggambar panah dependensi FS dan SS dari data seed', () => {\n    renderChart();\n    const fs = snapshot.dependencies.find((dependency) => dependency.type === 'FS');\n    const ss = snapshot.dependencies.find((dependency) => dependency.type === 'SS');\n    expect(fs).toBeTruthy();\n    expect(ss).toBeTruthy();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId(`gantt-dependency-${fs?.id}`)).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId(`gantt-dependency-${ss?.id}`)).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getAllByTestId(/^gantt-dependency-/)).toHaveLength(snapshot.dependencies.length);\n  });\n\n  it('menandai bar tugas kritis saat sorot jalur kritis diaktifkan', () => {\n    renderChart(true);\n    // 5.2 selalu berada di jalur kritis pada data seed.\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-5.2')).toHaveAttribute('data-critical', 'true');\n    // 4.1 (Skenario uji) memiliki float sehingga tidak kritis.\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-4.1')).toHaveAttribute('data-critical', 'false');\n  });\n\n  it('menampilkan baris tabel sebelah kiri untuk setiap tugas', () => {\n    renderChart();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-row-1.1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('Kickoff dan kebutuhan')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('Skenario uji')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('menyediakan pegangan resize pada tugas daun', () => {\n    renderChart();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-resize-1.1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}

}

> _Reasoning_
> Multiple failures: because the tests render multiple times without cleanup? No — RTL auto-cleanup requires globals or explicit cleanup; vitest with globals:false doesn't auto-cleanup unless `@testing-library/react` detects `afterEach` globally. With globals: false, RTL's auto-cleanup doesn't run → DOM accumulates across tests → "multiple elements found".
> 
> Fix: either set `globals: true` in vitest config (RTL auto-cleanup works when `afterEach` is global) or add explicit cleanup in setup.ts:
> ```ts
> import { afterEach } from 'vitest';
> import { cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
> afterEach(cleanup);
> ```
> Let me add that to setup.ts. Cleaner and keeps globals false.
> 
> Let me see the other failures too. Let me add cleanup and rerun to see the full list.

}

  "command": "cat > /tmp/verify.mjs <<'EOF'\nconst API = 'http://localhost:3001/api';\nconst post = (path, body, token) => fetch(API + path, {\n  method: 'POST',\n  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}) },\n  body: JSON.stringify(body),\n});\nconst get = (path, token) => fetch(API + path, { headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {} });\n\nconst login = await post('/auth/login', { email: 'demo@example.com', password: 'Demo12345' });\nconst { token, user } = await login.json();\nconsole.log('login:', login.status, user.email);\n\nconst projects = await (await get('/projects', token)).json();\nconst project = projects.projects[0];\nconsole.log('project:', project.name, '| start', project.startDate, '| weekend', project.weekendDays);\n\nconst snapshot = await (await get(`/projects/${project.id}`, token)).json();\nconst byName = (n) => snapshot.tasks.find((t) => t.name === n);\nconsole.log('tasks:', snapshot.tasks.length, '| deps:', snapshot.dependencies.length, '| assignments:', snapshot.assignments.length, '| holidays:', snapshot.holidays.map((h) => h.date).join(','));\n\nconst leaf = (n) => { const t = byName(n); return `${n}: ${t.scheduledStart} -> ${t.scheduledEnd} (${t.durationDays}h, ${t.progress}%)`; };\nconsole.log(leaf('Kickoff dan kebutuhan'));\nconsole.log(leaf('Penyusunan spesifikasi'));\nconsole.log(leaf('Implementasi backend'));\nconsole.log(leaf('Skenario uji'));\nconsole.log('Induk Pengembangan:', JSON.stringify(byName('Pengembangan')));\n\nconst analysis = await (await get(`/projects/${project.id}/analysis`, token)).json();\nconst name = (id) => analysis.cpm.find((t) => t.id === id)?.name;\nconsole.log('summary:', JSON.stringify(analysis.summary));\nconsole.log('critical paths:', analysis.criticalPathNames.map((p) => p.join(' -> ')).join(' | '));\nconsole.log('float Skenario uji:', analysis.cpm.find((t) => t.name === 'Skenario uji')?.totalFloat);\nconsole.log('PERT te:', analysis.pert.nodes.filter((n) => n.expectedDays !== null).map((n) => `${n.name} te=${n.expectedDays} var=${n.variance}`).join(', '));\nconsole.log('cost total:', analysis.costs.total, '| per anggota:', analysis.costs.resources.map((r) => `${r.name}=${Math.round(r.totalCost)}`).join(', '));\nconsole.log('overalloc:', analysis.overallocations.length);\n\nconst created = await post(`/projects/${project.id}/tasks`, { name: 'Tugas Verifikasi', durationDays: 3, startDate: '2026-10-23', priority: 'HIGH', color: '#111827' }, token);\nconst createdBody = await created.json();\nconst newTask = createdBody.task;\nconsole.log('create task:', created.status, newTask.scheduledStart, '->', newTask.scheduledEnd);\n\nconst dep = await post(`/tasks/${newTask.id}/dependencies`, { predecessorId: byName('Kickoff dan kebutuhan').id, type: 'FS', lagDays: 0 }, token);\nconsole.log('add dependency:', dep.status);\nconst del = await fetch(`${API}/tasks/${newTask.id}`, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });\nconsole.log('delete task:', del.status);\nEOF\nnode /tmp/verify.mjs",

}

=> {\n    localStorage.clear();\n    vi.unstubAllGlobals();\n  });\n\n  it('memuat data proyek dari API dan menampilkan Gantt', async () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    expect(screen.getByText('Memuat proyek…')).toBeInTheDocument();\n\n    await waitFor(() => {\n      expect(screen.getByText(snapshot.project.name)).toBeInTheDocument();\n    });\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-1.1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByTestId('gantt-bar-g1')).toHaveAttribute('data-parent', 'true');\n    expect(screen.getAllByTestId(/^gantt-dependency-/)).toHaveLength(snapshot.dependencies.length);\n\n    const fetchMock = globalThis.fetch as unknown as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;\n    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n      `/api/projects/${snapshot.project.id}`,\n      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'GET' }),\n    );\n  });\n});\n"
}

**🛠 Write File**

````json
{
  "path": "README.md",
  "instructions": "Project README per blueprint section 10 phase 5",
  "content": "# Aplikasi Manajemen Proyek Gantt Chart\n\nAplikasi web manajemen proyek berbasis Gantt Chart, multi-pengguna, dengan data tersimpan di\ndatabase melalui REST API (bukan `localStorage`). Antarmuka berbahasa Indonesia.\n\n## Daftar fitur\n\n1. **Manajemen tugas** — tambah/ubah/hapus tugas, durasi dalam hari kerja, tanggal mulai paling\n   awal, prioritas (Low/Medium/High), warna indikator, sub-tugas (parent-child maksimal 3 level,\n   rentang dan progres tugas induk dihitung otomatis).\n2. **Dependensi dan linimasa** — hubungan Finish-to-Start (FS) dan Start-to-Start (SS) dengan jeda\n   hari kerja (lag); Gantt SVG dengan bar, panah dependensi, drag, dan resize.\n3. **Manajemen sumber daya** — anggota tim (nama, email, peran, gaji) dan alokasi anggota ke tugas\n   beserta persentasenya.\n4. **Kalender proyek** — pengaturan hari akhir pekan dan hari libur khusus; durasi hanya menghitung\n   hari kerja.\n5. **Analisis proyek** — Critical Path Method (CPM) dan diagram PERT, plus biaya serta peringatan\n   over-alokasi.\n\nDi luar cakupan: kolaborasi real-time, ekspor PDF, notifikasi email, multi-bahasa, deployment cloud.\n\n## Tech stack\n\n| Lapisan | Teknologi |\n| --- | --- |\n| Frontend | React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, React Router, TanStack Query, react-hook-form, zod |\n| Backend | Node.js, Express, TypeScript (tsx untuk dev), zod, jsonwebtoken, bcryptjs, helmet, cors, express-rate-limit |\n| Database | SQLite dengan Prisma ORM (`server/prisma/dev.db`, tidak ikut di-commit) |\n| Logika inti | Paket `shared` (TypeScript murni, dipakai server dan client) |\n| Gantt dan PERT | Digambar sendiri dengan SVG tanpa library Gantt eksternal |\n| Test | Vitest, Supertest, Testing Library |\n| Tooling | npm workspaces, concurrently |\n\nStruktur: `shared/src` (mesin penjadwalan, CPM, PERT, biaya, skema zod), `server` (REST API +\nPrisma), `client` (antarmuka). Mesin penjadwalan dipakai bersama supaya hasil di server dan\npratinjau di client identik.\n\n## Prasyarat\n\n- Node.js 20 atau lebih baru\n- npm 10 atau lebih baru\n\n## Cara install\n\n```bash\nnpm install                     # memasang seluruh dependensi workspace\ncp server/.env.example server/.env\n# isi JWT_SECRET dengan string acak, contoh:\n#   node -e \"console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('hex'))\"\nnpm run db:migrate              # membuat skema database (server/prisma/dev.db)\nnpm run db:seed                 # data contoh (aman dijalankan berulang)\n```\n\nPerintah berguna lain:\n\n```bash\nnpm run db:reset                # hapus dan buat ulang database + jalankan seed\nnpm run build                   # build shared, server, dan client\nnpm run typecheck               # tsc --noEmit di semua workspace\nnpm test                        # Vitest di semua workspace\n```\n\n## Cara menjalankan\n\n```bash\nnpm run dev\n```\n\n- Aplikasi (client): **http://localhost:5173**\n- REST API (server): **http://localhost:3001/api** — cek `http://localhost:3001/api/health`\n\nVite mem-proxy `/api` ke `http://localhost:3001`, jadi cukup membuka alamat client.\n\n### Akun demo\n\n| Email | Kata sandi |\n| --- | --- |\n| `demo@example.com` | `Demo12345` |\n\n## Ringkasan API\n\nSemua rute memakai prefiks `/api`. Rute selain `auth/register`, `auth/login`, dan `health`\nmemerlukan header `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Setiap data dicek kepemilikannya: pengguna lain\nmendapat balasan 404.\n\n| Method dan path | Fungsi |\n| --- | --- |\n| `GET /health` | Cek server hidup |\n| `POST /auth/register` · `POST /auth/login` | Daftar dan masuk, mengembalikan token + user |\n| `GET /auth/me` | Data pengguna saat ini |\n| `GET, POST /projects` | Daftar dan buat proyek |\n| `GET, PATCH, DELETE /projects/:id` | Snapshot lengkap proyek, ubah, hapus |\n| `POST /projects/:id/tasks` | Buat tugas (bisa dengan `parentId`) |\n| `PATCH, DELETE /tasks/:id` | Ubah tugas, hapus tugas beserta anaknya |\n| `POST /tasks/:id/move` | Ubah induk dan posisi tugas |\n| `POST /tasks/:id/dependencies` | Tambah predecessor (`predecessorId`, `type`, `lagDays`) |\n| `PATCH, DELETE /dependencies/:id` | Ubah atau hapus dependensi |\n| `GET, POST /projects/:id/resources` | Daftar dan tambah anggota tim |\n| `PATCH, DELETE /resources/:id` | Ubah atau hapus anggota |\n| `POST /tasks/:id/assignments` · `PATCH, DELETE /assignments/:id` | Alokasi anggota ke tugas |\n| `PUT /projects/:id/calendar` | Atur hari akhir pekan (jadwal dihitung ulang) |\n| `POST /projects/:id/holidays` · `DELETE /holidays/:id` | Kelola hari libur khusus |\n| `GET /projects/:id/analysis` | Hasil CPM, jalur kritis, data PERT, biaya, dan beban resource |\n\nSetiap mutasi yang mengubah jadwal mengembalikan `{ \"tasks\": [...] }` berisi seluruh tugas terbaru.\n\nFormat error: `{ \"error\": { \"code\": \"STRING\", \"message\": \"pesan bahasa Indonesia\", \"details\": {} } }`\ndengan status 400/422 (validasi), 401 (belum login), 404 (tidak ditemukan), 409 (konflik).\n\n## Test\n\n```bash\nnpm test                 # seluruh workspace: shared (32), server (51), client (13)\nnpm test -w shared       # mesin penjadwalan termasuk seluruh vektor uji blueprint 6.11\nnpm test -w server       # integrasi Supertest memakai database SQLite terpisah (server/prisma/test.db)\nnpm test -w client       # test komponen: bar Gantt, panah dependensi, PERT, form tugas\n```\n\n## Asumsi\n\n1. **Lokasi `.env`** — berkas `.env` diletakkan di `server/.env` (contoh di `server/.env.example`)\n   agar Prisma CLI dan server menemukannya tanpa bergantung pada direktori kerja. Server gagal start\n   dengan pesan jelas bila `JWT_SECRET` kosong.\n2. **Urutan build `shared`** — skrip root `typecheck` dan `test` membangun paket `shared` lebih dulu\n   karena server dan client mengimpor hasil `shared/dist`.\n3. **Helper kepemilikan** — pengecekan kepemilikan data diletakkan di\n   `server/src/middleware/access.ts` (tambahan kecil di luar daftar berkas blueprint).\n4. **Ordinal CPM** — `project.startDate` dipakai sebagai ordinal 0 perhitungan CPM, sedangkan\n   `startDate` tiap tugas tetap menjadi batas \"mulai paling awal\" pada penjadwalan.\n5. **Respond an alokasi** — alokasi anggota dan data tim tidak mengubah jadwal, sehingga responsnya\n   mengembalikan objek terkait (bukan `{ tasks }`); mutasi tugas/dependensi/kalender selalu\n   mengembalikan seluruh daftar tugas.\n6. **Tugas induk** — kolom yang dihitung otomatis (durasi, tanggal mulai, progres, estimasi)\n   bersifat read-only; server menolak perubahannya dengan 409 dan pesan jelas.\n7. **Estimasi optimistis/pesimistis** hanya menambah informasi `te` dan variansi pada diagram PERT,\n   tidak mengubah jadwal.\n8. **Rate limit** — rute `auth/register` dan `auth/login` dibatasi 100 permintaan per 15 menit per IP.\n9. **Zoom Gantt** (Hari/Minggu/Bulan) disimpan sebagai state antarmuka, tidak dipersistensi.\n10. **Warna dan prioritas** divalidasi sebagai kode hex `#rrggbb` dan enum LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH.\n"
}
````

> _Reasoning_
> The drag/drag tests fail: resize got duration 1 (meaning delta was computed as a large negative, or... "expected 1 to be 4" — duration 1 means workingDaysBetween(start, end+delta) clamped at min 1, i.e., delta was very negative). And the drag test presumably got startDate wrong.
> 
> Cause: `clientX` may not be set on the event → `event.clientX` is undefined → delta = Math.round((undefined - 200)/36) = NaN → request... For resize, delta NaN → `Math.max(1, workingDaysBetween(start, addDays(end, NaN)))` → invalid date → workingDaysBetween returns 0 → duration 1 ✓ matches "expected 1 to be 4".
> 
> So clientX isn't reaching the handler. jsdom doesn't implement PointerEvent, and testing-library's createEvent for pointer events: it uses `PointerEvent` constructor if available; jsdom 25 doesn't have PointerEvent, so it falls back... Let me check testing-library's createEvent implementation:
> 
> ```js
> const EventConstructor = window[EventType] || window.Event
> event = new EventConstructor(eventName, eventInit)
> // then assign extra props
> ```
> Actually in `createEvent`, `eventInit` properties that aren't settable on the event get assigned via `Object.defineProperty`? Let me look: 
>

>   ...
>   const eventInit = {...defaultInit, ...init}
>   const {target: {value, files, ...targetProperties} = {}} = eventInit
>   ...
>   const event = new EventConstructor(eventName, eventInit)
>   // Copy properties from eventInit to the event object
>   Object.keys(eventInit).forEach(key => { ... })
> }
> ```
> Hmm, I recall testing-library does:
> ```js
>   // Spread the eventInit onto the event for properties not supported by the constructor
>   const event = new window[EventType](eventName, eventInit)
>   // ...
>   Object.keys(eventInit).forEach(key => {
>     if (key !== 'bubbles' && ... ) event[key] = eventInit[key]  // ???
>   })
> ```
> I'm not certain. Given the observed NaN, clientX isn't set.
> 
> Alternative approach: dispatch a native MouseEvent with pointer type? Simplest robust fix in the component: read `clientX` from the event but fall back to `(event as PointerEvent).clientX ?? event.nativeEvent...`. That doesn't help.
> 
> Better approach for the test: use `new MouseEvent('pointerdown', { clientX: 100, bubbles: true })` and dispatch it manually — MouseEvent supports clientX in jsdom ✓, and React listens for 'pointerdown' by name so it will handle the MouseEvent as if it were a pointer event (React dispatches based on event type name).
> 
> Let me change the test to:
> ```ts

> };

> React's synthetic pointerdown handler reads event.clientX from the native event ✓. For window listeners, my component adds window.addEventListener('pointermove', ...) — dispatching MouseEvent('pointermove') on window works ✓.
> 
> But careful: React 18 attaches listeners at the root container with capture; a MouseEvent with bubbles: true dispatched on the SVG child will propagate to the container and React will call the onPointerDown handler with a SyntheticEvent whose clientX comes from the native event ✓.

> _Reasoning_
> Now onDragTask isn't called at all. Possible causes:
> 1. React didn't handle the pointerdown (MouseEvent with type 'pointerdown' — React 18 does register 'pointerdown' listeners; dispatching a MouseEvent with that type should still trigger React's listener since it maps native event types to plugin events by type name; but React's synthetic event constructor for pointerdown is SyntheticPointerEvent which reads properties from the native event — should be OK).
> 
> Hmm, but maybe React's event system ignores the event if `event.pointerType`/nature? React checks `isPointerEvent`... In `getEventTarget`/`extractEvents`, React looks up the plugin by the event type from the top-level listener map; the SimpleEventPlugin has a list of event types including pointerdown ✓. Then `SyntheticPointerEvent` is created with the native event ✓.
> 
> 2. The issue could be that `renderChart(false, {onDragTask})` passes `handlers.onDragTask ?? vi.fn()` cast as never — that's fine.
> 
> 3. Maybe the interaction state updates but the effect that attaches window listeners hasn't run before I dispatch pointermove synchronously. React state update from the pointerdown handler is batched; the effect runs after the re-render, which happens... in React 18 with `fireEvent`/dispatchEvent, updates are batched and flushed synchronously (legacy mode + `act`?). With React Testing Library, `fireEvent` wraps in act() so state flushes and effects run. But my custom `dispatchEvent` isn't wrapped in act → the state update may not be flushed before I dispatch pointermove, so the window listener isn't attached yet, and by the time of pointerup the interaction exists but delta is 0 → commit does nothing (delta===0 → no callback) ✓ that explains 0 calls.
> 
> Fix: make the helper wrap dispatch in `act` from @testing-library/react:
> ```ts
> import { act } from '@testing-library/react';
> function pointerEvent(target, type, clientX) {
>   act(() => { target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(type, {clientX, bubbles:true, cancelable:true})); });
> }
> ```
> That should flush effects between events.
> 
> Let me apply that.

Selesai. Ringkasan akhir:

**Deliverable:** aplikasi web sesuai blueprint di repo tersebut, sudah dipush.

**Yang dipush (`main`, commit `7e602ea`):**
- `shared/` — mesin inti: kalender, penjadwalan, CPM, PERT, biaya, skema (32 unit test, termasuk vektor uji §6.11)
- `server/` — Express + Prisma + SQLite, auth JWT, rute proyek/tugas/dependensi/resource/assignment/kalender/analisis, seed (51 test integrasi)
- `client/` — React + Vite + Tailwind: halaman Login/Register/Proyek/Gantt/Tim/Kalender/Analisis (20 test)
- `README.md`, `PROGRESS.md`, konfigurasi monorepo npm workspaces

**Verifikasi yang dilakukan:**
- `npm ls-remote` vs HEAD → hash identik (`7e602ea4…`), branch `main` ada di remote
- `git status` bersih, tidak ada perubahan tertinggal
- `.env` rahasia tidak ikut; hanya `.env.example` yang ter-track

**Batasan:**
- Autentikasi push memakai Git Credential Manager; percobaan pertama dibatalkan, kedua berhasil. Jika credential di mesin lain belum ada, login GitHub akan diminta lagi.
- Suite test/typecheck terakhir dijalankan sebelum commit dokumentasi (README/PROGRESS) yang tidak memengaruhi kode; tidak dijalankan ulang setelah push karena tidak ada perubahan source.
